SLAI2培训平台技术报告（上）Training Platform Technical Report (Part I)

SLAI T-Rex：DeepSeek-V4 系列在 Ascend SuperPOD 上的全参数后训练SLAI T-Rex: Full-Parameter Post-training of the DeepSeek-V4 Family on Ascend SuperPOD

深圳环区研究院人工智能训练平台团队模型代码AI Training Platform Team, Shenzhen Loop Area Institute Models Codes

摘要Abstract

万亿参数级混合专家（MoE）模型的全参数后训练给大规模分布式训练带来了巨大的系统级挑战，包括严重的内存压力、非重叠的通信开销和低效的内核执行。虽然大多数大型 LLM 训练系统都是围绕基于 GPU 的集群构建的，但本报告介绍了 Ascend NPU SuperPOD 上的端到端优化实践。使用 DeepSeek-V4 模型系列作为目标工作负载，我们开发了一个涵盖模型级并行性、计算通信编排和低级内核执行的分层优化框架。由此产生的系统实现了 34.22% 的模型 FLOP 利用率 (MFU)，比开源基线配方提高了 2.93 倍，同时保持了训练稳定性。在此优化的基础设施的基础上，我们进一步为复杂的运筹学（OR）任务建立了持续预训练（CPT）和监督微调（SFT）工作流程。我们将集成框架称为Full-parameter post-training of trillion-parameter-scale Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models introduces substantial system-level challenges for large-scale distributed training, including severe memory pressure, non-overlapped communication overhead, and inefficient kernel execution. While most large-scale LLM training systems are built around GPU-based clusters, this report presents an end-to-end optimization practice on the Ascend NPU SuperPOD. Using the DeepSeek-V4 model family as the target workload, we develop a hierarchical optimization framework spanning model-level parallelism, computation–communication orchestration, and low-level kernel execution. The resulting system achieves 34.22% Model FLOPs Utilization (MFU) with a 2.93× improvement over the open-source baseline recipe while maintaining training stability. Building on this optimized infrastructure, we further establish a Continued Pre-Training (CPT) and Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) workflow for complex Operations Research (OR) tasks. We refer to the integrated framework asSLAI 霸王龙SLAI T-Rex，它将全栈 Ascend SuperPOD 优化与基于求解器的 CPT-SFT 专业化相结合。选择 OR 建模作为目标领域是因为它同时需要数学公式、结构化推理和代码生成。使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 作为专业化平台，我们开发了面向 OR 的 CPT 和 SFT 数据管道，将收集的领域资源与求解器验证的合成优化文档相结合。生成的数据集包含 10K 个高质量 SFT 样本，涵盖四个任务类别和三个问题表示。该专用模型在评估模型中获得了最高的平均零样本 Pass@1 分数，达到 71.81%，分别比 GPT-5.4-Mini 和基础 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 模型高出 3.98 和 11.27 个百分点。进一步的评估表明，CPT 建立了可转移的 OR 域建模先验，提高了 SFT 后面向求解器的可行性和结构等效性。总体而言，这项工作展示了从 Ascend 基础设施上高效的万亿参数模型后训练到用于基于求解器的数学建模的领域专用 Flash 模型的全栈途径，推进复杂推理的前沿模型系统。, which couples full-stack Ascend SuperPOD optimization with solver-grounded CPT-SFT specialization. OR modeling is selected as the target domain because it jointly requires mathematical formulation, structured reasoning, and code generation. Using DeepSeek-V4-Flash as the specialization platform, we develop OR-oriented CPT and SFT data pipelines that combine collected domain resources with solver-verified synthetic optimization documents. The resulting dataset contains 10K high-quality SFT samples spanning four task categories and three problem representations. The specialized model achieves the highest average zero-shot Pass@1 score among the evaluated models, reaching 71.81% and outperforming GPT-5.4-Mini and the base DeepSeek-V4-Flash model by 3.98 and 11.27 percentage points, respectively. Further evaluation indicates that CPT establishes transferable OR-domain modeling priors, improving both solver-facing feasibility and structural equivalence after SFT. Overall, this work demonstrates a full-stack pathway from efficient trillion-parameter model post-training on Ascend infrastructure to domain-specialized Flash models for solver-grounded mathematical modeling, advancing frontier-model systems for complex reasoning.

图1：Figure 1:(a) 在 Ascend SuperPOD 上进行 DeepSeek-V4-Pro (1.6T) 训练期间的 MFU 改进。从开源配方开始，优化并行性、NPU-CPU 协同优化和内核优化将 MFU 从 11.67% 提高到 34.22%。 (b) 总体精度，计算为 NL4OPT（Ramamonjison 等人，2022）、OptiBench（Yang 等人，2025c）、Bench4Opt（B4O）-Feasible（Wang 等人，2025b）和 Bench4Opt（B4O）-ORGEval（Wang 等人，2025b）的平均值。 SLAI T-Rex 在使用较小的公开激活参数预算的情况下获得了最高的总体得分。(a) MFU improvements during DeepSeek-V4-Pro (1.6T) training on Ascend SuperPOD. Starting from open-sourced recipes, optimized parallelism, NPU-CPU co-optimization, and kernel optimization increase MFU from 11.67% to 34.22%. (b) Overall accuracy, computed as the mean of NL4OPT (Ramamonjison et al., 2022), OptiBench (Yang et al., 2025c), Bench4Opt (B4O) -Feasible (Wang et al., 2025b), and Bench4Opt (B4O)-ORGEval (Wang et al., 2025b). SLAI T- Rex achieves the highest overall score while using a smaller disclosed activated-parameter budget.

3

  1. 简介Introduction

1 简介1 Introduction

训练后已成为预训练后增强法学硕士的标准阶段（Lai et al., 2025; Cheng et al., 2025），提高指令遵循能力、推理能力、与人类偏好的一致性以及长期执行的代理任务的能力。特别是，最近在 LLM 后培训方面的努力主要集中在持续预训练（CPT）、监督微调（SFT）（Mecklenburg 等人，2024）和强化学习（Schulman 等人，2017；Shao 等人，2024；Li 等人，2026b）以及专用代理环境（GLM-5-Team 等人， 2026；Dong 等人，2026）和用于一般或特定领域任务的数据管道（NVIDIA 等人，2026）。Post-training has become a standard stage for enhancing LLMs after pretraining (Lai et al., 2025; Cheng et al., 2025), improving instruction-following, reasoning power, alignment with human preferences, and the power of agentic tasks with long-horizon executions. In particular, recent efforts on LLM post-training mainly focus on Continued Pre-Training (CPT), Supervised FineTuning (SFT) (Mecklenburg et al., 2024) and reinforcement learning (Schulman et al., 2017; Shao et al., 2024; Li et al., 2026b), together with dedicated agentic environments (GLM-5-Team et al., 2026; Dong et al., 2026) and data pipelines (NVIDIA et al., 2026) for general or domain-specific tasks.

随着法学硕士从简单的聊天机器人演变为代理劳动力，对长上下文代理推理不断增长的需求继续推动模型扩展，以追求更强大的智能。最近的前沿法学硕士（DeepSeek-AI et al., 2025; GLM-5-Team et al., 2026）已将模型规模推向万亿参数规模，同时采用混合注意力机制（Yang et al., 2025b; Dao & Gu, 2024）或复杂的残差连接设计（Xie et al., 2025）来提高长上下文计算和相关优化的效率（Chen 等人，2026c；Li 等人，2026a）。与此同时，专家混合 (MoE)（Fedus 等人，2022）架构引入了一种不同的扩展路线，通过将每个令牌路由到选定的专家网络子集而不是激活所有参数，每个令牌计算量随模型大小呈亚线性增长，从而在保持内存效率的同时增强推理能力。As LLMs evolve from simple chatbots into agentic workforces, the growing demand for long-context agentic reasoning continues to drive model scaling in pursuit of stronger intelligence. Recent frontier LLMs (DeepSeek-AI et al., 2025; GLM-5-Team et al., 2026) have pushed model sizes toward the trillion-parameter scale while adopting the hybrid attention mechanism (Yang et al., 2025b; Dao & Gu, 2024) or intricate residual connection design (Xie et al., 2025) to improve the efficiency of long-context computation and related optimizations (Chen et al., 2026c; Li et al., 2026a). In the meantime, Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) (Fedus et al., 2022) architecture introduces a different route for scaling by routing each token to a selected subset of expert networks rather than activating all parameters, per-token computation grows sub-linearly with model size, enhancing reasoning power while preserving memory efficiency.

大多数先前关于大规模 MoE 后训练的研究仍然与面向 CUDA 或 TPU 的训练基础设施紧密结合（DeepSeek-AI 等人，2025；Kimi Team 等人，2026；GLM-5-Team 等人，2026），这些基础设施围绕单指令、多线程（SIMT）执行或基于脉动阵列的加速器架构构建。相比之下，单指令、多数据 (SIMD) 硬件上的万亿参数级 LLM 的训练后研究仍然相对不足。受这一研究空白的推动，本报告首先提出了基于升腾 CloudMatrix384 SuperPOD 和升腾 910C NPU 的万亿参数级 LLM 后训练的系统级基础设施优化。作为最近最先进的代理任务和推理法学硕士之一，DeepSeek-V4（DeepSeek-AI et al., 2026）通过复杂的稀疏注意力和残差连接机制达到了顶级智能。 SuperPOD 集群上的全参数训练后 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 模型引入了一系列挑战，涵盖 AscendC 内核设计、通信编排、内存管理和多维并行性，如图 1 所示。Most prior studies on large-scale MoE post-training remain tightly coupled with CUDA- or TPUoriented training infrastructure (DeepSeek-AI et al., 2025; Kimi Team et al., 2026; GLM-5-Team et al., 2026), which are built around either Single-Instruction, Multiple-Thread (SIMT) execution or systolic-array-based accelerator architectures. In contrast, post-training trillion-parameter-class LLMs on Single-Instruction, Multiple-Data (SIMD) hardware remains comparatively underexplored. Motivated by this research gap, this report first presents a system-level infrastructure optimization for trillion-parameter-class LLM post-training based on Ascend CloudMatrix384 SuperPOD with Ascend 910C NPUs. As one of the recent state-of-the-art LLMs for agentic tasks and reasoning, DeepSeek-V4 (DeepSeek-AI et al., 2026) reaches top-tier intelligence via intricate sparse attention and residual connection mechanisms. Full-parameter post-training DeepSeek-V4-Pro model on SuperPOD cluster introduces a host of challenges, spanning AscendC kernel design, communication orchestration, memory management, and multi-dimensional parallelism, as illustrated in Figure 1.

除了培训基础设施的挑战之外，最近的法学硕士培训后工作越来越集中在可验证的推理领域，特别是数学推理、编码和代理软件工程任务。然而，许多长尾工业决策领域的代表性仍然相对不足。例如，运筹学 (OR) 是调度、运输、供应链管理和资源分配等现实世界优化问题的核心，但其与法学硕士培训和评估的整合仍处于早期阶段。最近基于 LLM 的 OR 的研究主要集中在自动模型制定和面向求解器的程序综合，其中 LLM 将自然语言问题描述转换为数学公式或可执行的优化代码。代表性工作（Xiao et al., 2024；Zhang & Luo, 2025）进一步将领域知识检索、多智能体协作和基于执行反馈的细化引入 OR 建模管道。然而，这些方法仍然受到小规模基准、脆弱的语义到结构映射以及复杂工业 OR 场景中公式正确性保证不足的限制。此外，现有的基于法学硕士的 OR 研究主要强调代理系统设计，而针对 OR 的特定领域培训仍未得到充分探索。对于万亿参数级模型（例如 DeepSeek-V4-Pro），这种差距变得更加明显，其中面向 OR 的后期训练不仅需要通过 SFT 或 CPT 获取领域知识，还需要专用的数据准备管道以及来自优化环境的可验证反馈。Beyond the challenges of training infrastructure, recent LLM post-training efforts have increasingly concentrated on verifiable reasoning domains, particularly mathematical reasoning, coding, and agentic software-engineering tasks. However, many long-tailed industrial decision-making domains remain comparatively under-represented. Operations Research (OR), for example, is central to real-world optimization problems such as scheduling, transportation, supply-chain management, and resource allocation, yet its integration with LLM training and evaluation is still at an early stage. Recent studies on LLM-based OR mainly focus on automatic model formulation and solveroriented program synthesis, where LLMs translate natural-language problem descriptions into mathematical formulations or executable optimization code. Representative works (Xiao et al., 2024; Zhang & Luo, 2025) further introduce domain-knowledge retrieval, multi-agent collaboration, and execution-feedback-based refinement into the OR modeling pipeline. However, these approaches remain limited by small-scale benchmarks, brittle semantic-to-structure mapping, and insufficient guarantees of formulation correctness in complex industrial OR scenarios. Furthermore, existing LLM-based OR studies primarily emphasize agentic-system design, while domain-specific training for OR remains under-explored. This gap becomes more pronounced for trillion-parameter-class models (e.g., DeepSeek-V4-Pro), where OR-oriented post-training requires not only domain knowledge acquisition via SFT or CPT, but also a dedicated data preparation pipeline, and verifiable feedback from optimization environments.

在这项工作中，我们基于配备 910C NPU 的 Ascend SuperPOD，为 DeepSeek-V4 模型系列提供了全栈端到端训练（Zuo 等人，2025）。我们将整体框架称为In this work, we present full-stack end-to-end training for the DeepSeek-V4 model family based on Ascend SuperPOD with 910C NPUs (Zuo et al., 2025). We refer to the overall framework asSLAI 霸王龙SLAI T-Rex，它将全栈 Ascend SuperPOD 训练优化与基于求解器的 CPT-SFT 专业化 OR 结合起来。为了提高系统级训练效率，这项工作首先提出了基于 SuperPOD 的训练基础设施的全栈优化，包括内核级优化和顶层训练管道编排。特别是，我们提出了 AuraKernel，这是一种专门的 AscendC 内核优化代理，它可以自动优化 DeepSeek-V4 模型系列的主要瓶颈内核。通过结合层次优化, which couples full-stack Ascend SuperPOD training optimization with solvergrounded CPT–SFT specialization for OR. To improve system-level training efficiency, this work first presents a full-stack optimization for SuperPOD-based training infrastructure with both kernel-level optimization and top-level training pipeline orchestration. In particular, we propose AuraKernel, a specialized AscendC kernel optimization agent which automatically optimizes the dominant bottleneck kernels of the DeepSeek-V4 model family. By combining the hierarchical optimization

  1. Ascend CloudMatrix384 SuperPOD 上的训练基础设施Training Infrastructure on Ascend CloudMatrix384 SuperPOD

策略中，我们使用 DeepSeek-V4Pro 模型实现了 2.93 倍的模型 FLOP 利用率 (MFU) 改进。利用 Ascend SuperPOD 上优化的系统级训练堆栈，我们进一步在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 上验证了面向 OR 的后训练工作流程，涵盖基于求解器的数据构建、SFT 数据清理、CPT 到 SFT 传输和基准评估。在相同SFT条件下的CPTto-SFT转移评估中，直接SFT将B4O-Feasible从60.47％提高到65.93％，将B4O-ORGEval从34.26％提高到48.73％，而CPT初始化SFT将它们分别进一步提高到71.22％和59.39％。汇总多个 OR 评估指标，我们的模型获得了 71.81% 的平均准确率，比 GPT-5.4-Mini 提高了 3.98 点，比基础 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 提高了 11.27 点。这些结果表明，SFT 提供了监督任务对齐，而 CPT 贡献了 OR 域建模先验，提高了面向求解器的可行性和结构等效性，在 B4O-ORGEval 数据集上获得了最大收益。 Pro 和 Flash 后训练共同验证了两个互补的优势：基础设施可扩展性和领域适应。据我们所知，这是第一个公开记录的针对 Ascend SuperPOD 上万亿参数级 MoE 模型的全参数后训练的全栈工程研究之一，它证明了基于 SIMD 的 NPU 对于大规模 LLM 训练的有效性。strategies, we achieve a 2.93× Model FLOPs Utilization (MFU) improvement with the DeepSeek-V4Pro model. Exploiting the optimized system-level training stack on Ascend SuperPOD, we further validate an OR-oriented post-training workflow on DeepSeek-V4-Flash, covering solver-grounded data construction, SFT data cleaning, CPT-to-SFT transfer, and benchmark evaluation. In the CPTto-SFT transfer evaluation under identical SFT conditions, direct SFT improves B4O-Feasible from 60.47% to 65.93% and B4O-ORGEval from 34.26% to 48.73%, while CPT-initialized SFT further raises them to 71.22% and 59.39%, respectively. Aggregating over multiple OR evaluation metrics, our model attains an average accuracy of 71.81%, outperforming GPT-5.4-Mini by 3.98 points and the base DeepSeek-V4-Flash by 11.27 points. These results show that SFT provides supervised task alignment, while CPT contributes OR-domain modeling priors that improve both solver-facing feasibility and structural equivalence, with the largest gain on B4O-ORGEval dataset. Together, the Pro and Flash post-training validate two complementary benefits: infrastructure scalability and domain adaptation. To the best of our knowledge, this is among the first publicly documented fullstack engineering studies of full-parameter post-training for a trillion-parameter-class MoE model on Ascend SuperPOD and it demonstrate the effectiveness of SIMD-based NPUs for large-scale LLM training.

总的来说，这项工作的贡献是：Overall, the contributions of this work are:

  • 用于训练复杂的万亿参数 MoE LLM 的 Ascend-native 系统优化：Ascend-native system optimization for training complex trillion-parameter MoE LLMs:基于升腾CloudMatrix384 SuperPOD，我们提出了万亿参数规模DeepSeek-V4-Pro训练的全栈优化框架，实现了34.22%的MFU，较默认基线提升了2.93倍。除了训练加速之外，这项工作还描述了在 Ascend SuperPOD 架构约束下联合优化并行性、通信编排、内存移动和内核执行的 Ascend 友好系统级优化的关键原则。Based on Ascend CloudMatrix384 SuperPOD, we present a full-stack optimization framework for trillion-parameter-scale DeepSeek-V4-Pro training, achieving 34.22% MFU with 2.93× improvement over the default baseline. In addition to the training acceleration, this work characterizes the key principles of Ascend-friendly system-level optimization with jointly optimized parallelism, communication orchestration, memory movement, and kernel execution under the architectural constraints of Ascend SuperPOD.

  • Ascend 上 MoE、稀疏注意力和内存层次结构的硬件感知分析：Hardware-aware analysis of MoE, sparse attention, and memory hierarchy on Ascend:通过对 Ascend 上的 DeepSeek-V4 训练进行详细分析，我们提供了模型架构如何与 Ascend 内核执行、通信模式、内存层次结构和分布式编排交互的端到端分析。由此产生的见解将模型级设计选择与硬件级计算效率联系起来，全面的分析和瓶颈分析进一步激发了所提出的优化策略和未来的研究。Through detailed profiling of DeepSeek-V4 training on Ascend, we provide an end-to-end analysis of how model architecture interacts with Ascend kernel execution, communication patterns, memory hierarchy, and distributed orchestration. The resulting insights bridge model-level design choices with hardware-level computation efficiency, and comprehensive profiling and bottleneck analysis further motivate the proposed optimization strategies and future research.

  • 求解器引导的 AscendC 内核优化超越手动和启发式优化：Solver-guided AscendC kernel optimization beyond manual and heuristic optimization:我们提出了将运筹学应用于 Ascend 内核优化的首次尝试之一。与之前主要依赖于线束构建和循环级工程的内核代理方法相比，所提出的 AuraKernel 将 OR 引导的平铺优化引入到代理内核优化工作流程中。 AscendC OR 求解器和专用线束设计可实现精确的平铺选择、长范围内核优化以及对 Ascend 内核搜索空间的更系统的探索。We present one of the first attempts to apply Operations Research to Ascend kernel optimization. In contrast to prior kernel-agent approaches that mainly rely on harness construction and loop-level engineering, the proposed AuraKernel introduces OR-guided tiling optimization into the agentic kernel optimization workflow. The AscendC OR solver and dedicated harness design enable accurate tiling selection, long-horizon kernel optimization, and more systematic exploration of the Ascend kernel search space.

  • 用于复杂 MoE 通信和并行性的 SuperPOD 感知编排：SuperPOD-aware orchestration for complex MoE communication and parallelism:我们提出了 Ascend SuperPOD 的端到端优化方法，具有多维并行性和针对大规模 MoE 架构的优化训练编排。We present an end-to-end optimization recipe for Ascend SuperPOD with multi-dimensional parallelism and optimized training orchestration for large-scale MoE architectures.

  • 用于特定领域增强的可重复的训练后工作流程：Reproducible post-training workflow for domain-specific enhancement:我们在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 上构建了面向 OR 的 CPT-SFT 工作流程，涵盖基于求解器的 CPT 数据构建、自蒸馏 SFT 数据生成、契约感知清理和匹配基准评估。We construct an OR-oriented CPT-SFT workflow on DeepSeek-V4-Flash, covering solver-grounded CPT data construction, self-distilled SFT data generation, contract-aware cleaning, and matched benchmark evaluation.

  • CPT-SFT 在结构化 OR 推理中协同作用的经验证据：Empirical evidence for CPT–SFT synergy in structured OR reasoning:我们表明，SFT 提供了监督任务对齐并改进了原始检查点，而 CPT 提供了额外的 OR 域建模先验，进一步提高了面向求解器的可行性和结构等效性，并在 B4OORGEval 数据集上观察到了最大的额外增益。We show that SFT provides supervised task alignment and improves the original checkpoint, while CPT supplies additional OR-domain modeling priors that further improve both solver-facing feasibility and structural equivalence, with the largest additional gain observed on B4OORGEval dataset.

2.1 在Ascend SuperPOD上训练万亿参数LLM的瓶颈2.1 Bottlenecks of Training Trillion-parameter LLM on Ascend SuperPOD

并降低长上下文处理的成本（Krajewski 等人，2024；DeepSeek-AI 等人，2026）。然而，这些架构优势带来了更加复杂的训练工作负载。专家路由需要频繁的令牌调度和全对全（A2A）通信，而稀疏注意力会产生不规则的计算模式和异构内核行为。因此，高效的训练需要跨并行性、通信调度、内存管理、运行时编排和内核执行的协调优化。and reducing the cost of long-context processing (Krajewski et al., 2024; DeepSeek-AI et al., 2026). These architectural benefits, however, introduce substantially more complex training workloads. Expert routing requires frequent token dispatch and All-to-All (A2A) communication, while sparse attention produces irregular computation patterns and heterogeneous kernel behavior. Efficient training therefore requires coordinated optimization across parallelism, communication scheduling, memory management, runtime orchestration, and kernel execution.

现有的大规模 LLM 训练系统主要优化基于 GPU 或 TPU 的集群上的多维并行性和计算通信重叠（Yan 等人，2026）。相比之下，在面向 SIMD 的加速器集群上对万亿参数级 MoE 模型进行全参数训练的探索仍然较少。本节介绍了在 Ascend CloudMatrix384 SuperPOD（Zuo 等人，2025）上进行此类训练的端到端研究，其中 Ascend 原生优化涵盖了分布式训练系统和特定于架构的内核。Existing large-scale LLM training systems primarily optimize multi-dimensional parallelism and computation–communication overlap on GPU- or TPU-based clusters (Yan et al., 2026). By comparison, full-parameter training of trillion-parameter-class MoE models on SIMD-oriented accelerator clusters remains less explored. This section presents an end-to-end study of such training on the Ascend CloudMatrix384 SuperPOD (Zuo et al., 2025), with Ascend-native optimizations spanning both the distributed training system and architecture-specific kernels.

我们使用 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 作为目标工作负载，它具有 1.6 万亿个参数，并将大规模 MoE 层与混合稀疏注意力相结合（DeepSeek-AI et al., 2026）。其架构强调训练基础设施的三个主要维度：1万亿参数规模的参数和优化器状态管理，2具有密集A2A通信的细粒度专家路由，以及3需要专门内核优化的不规则稀疏注意力计算。这些特征暴露了大规模 MoE 训练的主要瓶颈，包括内存压力、专家并行可扩展性、令牌分发效率、通信计算重叠和内核性能。We use DeepSeek-V4-Pro as the target workload, which has 1.6 trillion parameters and combines large-scale MoE layers with hybrid sparse attention (DeepSeek-AI et al., 2026). Its architecture stresses three major dimensions of the training infrastructure: 1 parameter and optimizer-state management at trillion-parameter scale, 2 fine-grained expert routing with intensive A2A communication, and 3 irregular sparse-attention computation requiring specialized kernel optimization. These characteristics expose the principal bottlenecks of large-scale MoE training, including memory pressure, expert-parallel scalability, token-dispatch efficiency, communication–computation overlap, and kernel performance.

2.1 节首先描述了 Ascend SuperPOD 上的端到端训练瓶颈。 2.2 节随后介绍了所提议的并行性、通信、内存和运行时编排优化。最后，2.3 节介绍了内核级优化工作流程，包括 AuraKernel，我们用于长期 AscendC 性能调整的运筹学指导代理。Section 2.1 first characterizes the end-to-end training bottlenecks on Ascend SuperPOD. Section 2.2 then presents the proposed parallelism, communication, memory, and runtime-orchestration optimizations. Finally, Section 2.3 introduces the kernel-level optimization workflow, including AuraKernel, our operations-research-guided agent for long-horizon AscendC performance tuning.

并行组之间的队列同步、集体通信和数据重新分配，如表 1 所示。quent synchronization, collective communication, and data redistribution among parallel groups, as summarized in Table 1.

分析结果清楚地表明主要延迟瓶颈是从 TP 和 DP 分区收集部分结果。同时，传统 1F1B 管道并行性 (PP)（Narayanan 等人，2021）引入的发送和接收延迟在每个训练步骤的总体延迟中占了很大一部分。受益于 Ascend SuperPOD 的高速互连，MoE 引入的通信开销仍然很小。The profiling results clearly indicate that the primary latency bottleneck is collecting partial results from the TP and DP partitions. Meanwhile, the send and receive latency introduced by the traditional 1F1B pipeline parallelism (PP) (Narayanan et al., 2021) contributes a large portion toward the overall latency per training step. Benefiting from the high-speed interconnects of the Ascend SuperPOD, the communication overhead introduced by MoE remains minimal.

根据这一分析，一个训练步骤的延迟可以分解为四个主要部分：Based on this analysis, the latency of one training step can be decomposed into four major components:

除了并行引起的通信引入的绝对延迟之外（In addition to the absolute latency introduced by the parallelism-induced communication (hcom_-内核），不同 PP 阶段之间的不平衡计算工作量进一步在整个训练过程中引入了延迟泡沫。如图 2(b) 所示，计算气泡在管道并行的不同阶段均匀存在。kernels), the imbalanced computation workload between different PP stages further introduces a latency bubble throughout the training process. As depicted in Figure 2(b), the computation bubble exists uniformly across different stages of pipeline parallelism.

来自特定于模型的内核的主要延迟。Dominant latency from model-specific kernels.仔细观察详细的内核执行时间，我们发现计算延迟主要由一小组特定于架构的内核决定。特别是，DeepSeek-V4 引入了分层稀疏注意力，在 Ascend NPU 上执行时间较长。如表 2 所示，稀疏注意力的后向内核构成了所有 AscendC 算子中最重要的瓶颈之一，其总执行时间甚至与通用矩阵乘法算子相当。在整个分析过程中，算子的任务持续时间份额是其总的核心繁忙时间（每个内核的 AI-Core 或矢量核心执行时间），在排除通信和 AI-CPU 条目后，在一步的 206,503 个计算内核上进行归一化，同时列出调用计数和平均每次调用持续时间，以暴露碎片化的长尾算子。Taking a closer look at the detailed kernel execution time, we observe that the latency of computation is dominated by a small set of architecturespecific kernels. In particular, DeepSeek-V4 introduces the hierarchical sparse attention with heavy execution time on Ascend NPUs. As depicted in Table 2, the backward kernel of sparse attention constitutes one of the most significant bottlenecks among all AscendC operators, where the total execution time is even comparable to the generic matrix multiplication operators. Throughout this analysis, an operator’s Task-Duration share is its summed core-busy time—the per-kernel AI-Core or vector-core execution time—normalized over the 206,503 compute kernels of one step after excluding communication and AI-CPU entries, with invocation count and average per-call duration listed alongside to expose the fragmented, long-tailed operators.

具体来说，稀疏注意力家族贡献了大约四分之一的非通信内核时间，超过了密集注意力家族的总和。Concretely, the sparse-attention family contributes roughly a quarter of the non-communication kernel time, exceeding the combined share of the denseMatMulV3和教育部and MoEGroupedMatmul运营商。这种集中是模型架构所固有的，而不是特定层配置的产物：选择稀疏键值上下文的闪电索引器和消耗它的共享 KV 注意力在每个注意力块中被调用（DeepSeek-AI 等人，2026），因此它们的成本直接随着网络的深度而变化。operators. This concentration is intrinsic to the model architecture rather than an artifact of a particular layer configuration: the lightning indexer that selects the sparse key–value context and the shared-KV attention that consumes it are invoked in every attention block (DeepSeek-AI et al., 2026), so their cost scales directly with the depth of the network.

分析结果还清楚地表明了这些内核速度缓慢的原因，并且它们的瓶颈与矩阵乘法运算符的瓶颈根本不同。密集的The profiling results also make it clear why these kernels are slow, and their bottlenecks differ fundamentally from those of matrix-multiplication operators. The denseMatMulV3和教育部and MoEGroupedMatmul内核受计算限制并在接近饱和（利用率超过 90%）的 CUBE 核心上运行，将其延迟转换为有用的矩阵吞吐量。其中，kernels are compute-bound and run on the CUBE core near saturation (over 90% utilization), converting their latency into useful matrix throughput. Among these,GroupedMatmul

7

2.2 在Ascend SuperPOD上优化万亿参数LLM训练系统2.2 Optimizing Trillion-parameter LLM Training System on Ascend SuperPOD

确实是 MAC 主导的，而is genuinely MAC-dominated, whereasMatMulV3受操作数负载限制，其 MTE2 负载份额 (96.3%) 远远超过其 MAC 份额 (52.5%)。稀疏注意力内核则相反：它们的计算引擎大部分处于闲置状态（向量计算管道比率约为 9.5%），它们的运行时间用于通过本地缓冲区移动键值图块，并执行组装稀疏注意力模式所需的标量索引和收集/分散逻辑。换句话说，这些运算符是受内存和控制限制的，而不是受计算限制的——它们占据延迟曲线的顶部，不是因为它们执行繁重的计算，而是因为它们依赖于数据的、不规则的访问模式使硬件的矩阵引擎处于饥饿状态。is operand-load-bound, its MTE2 load share (96.3%) far exceeding its MAC share (52.5%). The sparse-attention kernels do the opposite: their compute engines sit largely idle (vector-compute pipeline ratio of about 9.5%), and their runtime is instead spent moving key–value tiles through the local buffers and executing the scalar index and gather/scatter logic needed to assemble the sparse attention pattern. In other words, these operators are memory- and control-bound rather than compute-bound—they occupy the top of the latency profile not because they perform heavy computation, but because their data-dependent, irregular access pattern keeps the hardware’s matrix engine starved.

受这些发现的启发，这项工作提出Motivated by these findings, this work proposes灵气内核AuraKernel，一个端到端 AscendC 内核优化代理，可自动重组大型、内存和控制绑定的 AscendC 内核，如第 2.3.2 节中所述。, an end-to-end AscendC kernel optimization agent that automatically restructures the large, memory- and control-bound AscendC kernels, as presented in Section 2.3.2.

碎片化的、受内存限制的内核的长尾。The long tail of fragmented, memory-bound kernels.除了在 Ascend NPU 上利用率不足的排名靠前的瓶颈内核之外，我们还观察到在每个训练步骤中一致出现的长尾小内核调用，如表 2 所示。虽然每个单独的内核仅贡献很短的执行时间，但这些碎片化的内核调用共同占据了总体计算时间的很大一部分。In addition to the top-ranked bottleneck kernels with insufficient utilization on Ascend NPUs, we observe a long tail of small kernel invocations that consistently appear in each training step, as shown in Table 2. While each individual kernel contributes only a short execution time, these fragmented kernel calls collectively account for a substantial fraction of the overall computation time.

仔细检查发现，这些长尾内核主要是逐元素和数据移动运算符，例如A closer inspection shows that these long-tailed kernels are primarily element-wise and datamovement operators, such asCast和andAdd，以及张量复制和重塑操作。这些运算符被频繁调用，但每个运算符通常仅在数十微秒内完成。与立方体绑定矩阵乘法内核不同，这些碎片运算符几乎完全在矢量核心上执行，其中加载/存储管道比矢量计算管道更加活跃。如表2所示，, together with tensor copy and reshape operations. These operators are invoked frequently, yet each typically completes within only tens of microseconds. Unlike cube-bound matrix-multiplication kernels, these fragmented operators execute almost entirely on the vector core, where the load/store pipelines are significantly more active than the vector-compute pipeline. As shown in Table 2, theCast仅算子就贡献了总计算时间的 5.7%。然而，矢量计算的利用operator alone contributes 5.7% of the total computation time. However, the vector-compute utilization ofCast仍然几乎空闲（计算核心利用率约 1.7%），而内存传输管道仍然被大量占用，AIV MTE2/MTE3 为 89.7%/59.9%。这种不平衡表明此类运算符主要受内存限制，并且是与相邻内核融合的自然候选者。remains nearly idle (computing core utilization ≈ 1.7%), whereas the memory-transfer pipelines remain heavily occupied, with AIV MTE2/MTE3 at 89.7%/59.9%. This imbalance indicates that such operators are primarily memory-bound and are natural candidates for fusion with neighboring kernels.

总体而言，由于 DeepSeek-V4 模型中新提出的模型架构（例如 mHC (Xie et al., 2025) 超连接），内核碎片进一步降低了系统级效率。这些内核的执行时间非常短，内核启动和调度的固定成本无法再有效地摊销。此外，重复的内存往返会引入冗余数据移动并增加总开销。因此，这些碎片化的、受内存限制的运算符的集体占用空间远不能忽略不计，这激发了基于专用内核融合的整合策略，以抑制启动开销并消除冗余内存流量，如第 2.3.3 节所述。Overall, kernel fragmentation further degrades system-level efficiency due to the newly proposed model architecture (e.g., mHC (Xie et al., 2025) hyper-connections) in the DeepSeek-V4 model. These kernels have very short execution times, where the fixed costs of kernel launch and scheduling can no longer be effectively amortized. Furthermore, repeated round trips to memory introduce redundant data movement and inflate the aggregate overhead. As a result, the collective footprint of these fragmented, memory-bound operators is far from negligible, motivating a consolidation strategy based on dedicated kernel fusion to suppress launch overhead and eliminate redundant memory traffic, as presented in Section 2.3.3.

负载平衡管道调度和专家张量并行（ETP）。Load-balanced pipeline scheduling and expert–tensor parallelism (ETP).正如第 2.1 节中所讨论的，减少张量并行性 (TP) 引入的暴露通信开销是主要优化目标。由于每个Ascend 910C NPU（64GB）的内存容量有限，简单的并行化策略需要相对较高的TP程度，例如TP=4，以适应设备内存预算内的模型状态和激活。不过，这也增加了TP集体的频率和成本。As discussed in Section 2.1, reducing the exposed communication overhead introduced by tensor parallelism (TP) is a primary optimization target. Due to the limited memory capacity of each Ascend 910C NPU (64GB), a naive parallelization strategy requires a relatively high TP degree, such as TP=4, to fit the model states and activations within the device memory budget. However, this also increases the frequency and cost of TP collectives.

遵循 Megatron-LM 中的并行折叠策略（Shoeybi 等人，2019），我们为 MoE 层启用专家张量并行性（ETP），同时保持管道并行性（PP）配置固定。这解耦了注意力模块和 MoE 模块之间的并行性选择：注意力层可以采用较低的 TP 度（TP=2）来减少通信开销并提高大型密集矩阵上的 GEMM 效率，而 MoE 层使用 ETP=1 来保留完整的专家宽度并避免进一步分割专家计算。由此产生的调度和执行策略如图 4 所示。Following the Parallel Folding strategy in Megatron-LM (Shoeybi et al., 2019), we enable experttensor parallelism (ETP) for MoE layers while keeping the pipeline parallelism (PP) configuration fixed. This decouples the parallelism choices between attention and MoE modules: attention layers can adopt a lower TP degree (TP=2) to reduce communication overhead and improve GEMM efficiency on large dense matrices, while MoE layers use ETP=1 to preserve the full expert width and avoid further fragmenting expert computation. The resulting dispatch and execution strategy is illustrated in Figure 4.

虚拟管道并行性 (VPP) 与 DualPipeV。Virtual Pipeline Parallelism (VPP) vs. DualPipeV.对于管道维度（管道度为 8），我们采用 VPP（Narayanan 等人，2021）而不是双向 DualPipeV（Qi 等人，2025；DeepSeek-AI 等人，2025）计划，这是基于我们的分析的两个互补原因。首先，DualPipeV（Qi 等人，2025 年；DeepSeek-AI 等人，2025 年）在这种规模下内存不足：为了维持其双向重叠，每个设备必须保留两个模型块，将驻留参数和梯度大致加倍，以及更多的运行中微批量激活。我们的跟踪显示，即使在内存更加节约的 VPP 计划下，在分析的队列中，峰值保留设备内存也已达到每个 NPU 51.7–54.6 GB，与每个设备的 HBM 预算只留下了很小的余量； DualPipeV 要求的额外驻留很容易超出此限制，迫使更小的微批量大小或更重的重新计算，从而削弱其所谓的效率增益。其次，也是更根本的，DualPipeV 的主要功能，重叠了专家并行的全部功能（MoE 调度/组合）For the pipeline dimension (with a pipeline degree of 8), we adopt VPP (Narayanan et al., 2021) rather than the bidirectional DualPipeV (Qi et al., 2025; DeepSeek-AI et al., 2025) schedule, for two complementary reasons grounded in our profiling. First, DualPipeV (Qi et al., 2025; DeepSeek-AI et al., 2025) is memory-prohibitive at this scale: to sustain its bidirectional overlap, each device must retain two model chunks, roughly doubling the resident parameters and gradients, together with a larger population of in-flight micro-batch activations. Our traces show that, even under the more memory-frugal VPP schedule, the peak reserved device memory already reaches 51.7–54.6 GB per NPU across the profiled ranks, leaving only a narrow margin from the per-device HBM budget; the additional residency demanded by DualPipeV would readily overshoot this limit, forcing a smaller micro-batch size or heavier recomputation that erodes its purported efficiency gains. Second, and more fundamentally, DualPipeV’s headline capability, overlapping the expert-parallel all-to-all (MoE dispatch/combine)

通过计算，目标成本根本不是我们的瓶颈。在我们的分析中，全对全仅占通信时间的 14.5%，而通信成本主要由管道点对点传输（占通信的 36.7%，主要由反映阶段级泡沫和负载不平衡的接收端停顿驱动）和张量/数据并行驱动。with computation, targets a cost that is simply not our bottleneck. In our profiling, the all-to-all accounts for only 14.5% of communication time, whereas the communication cost is dominated by pipeline point-to-point transfers (36.7% of communication, driven largely by receive-side stalls that reflect stage-level bubbles and load imbalance) and by tensor-/data-parallelAllReduce（沟通的 48.4%）。(48.4% of communication).

VPP 通过引入虚拟阶段的细粒度交错来缓解管道泡沫。因此，DualPipeV 会产生大量内存损失，以换取加速对端到端延迟影响很小的组件，而 VPP 会抑制主要的管道气泡，同时将每个设备的占用空间保持在硬件预算和训练稳定性范围内。我们将在不久的将来进一步研究最佳管道并行策略。The pipeline-bubble is precisely what VPP mitigates by introducing fine-grained interleaving of virtual stages. Consequently, DualPipeV would incur a substantial memory penalty in exchange for accelerating a component that contributes marginally to our end-to-end latency, whereas VPP suppresses the dominant pipeline bubbles while keeping the per-device footprint within the hardware budget and training stability. We will further investigate the optimal pipeline parallelism strategy in the near future.

MoE 令牌调度的重叠同步和计算。Overlapped synchronization and computation for MoE token dispatch.专家并行性将路由转变为分布式 LLM 培训中的全对全 (A2A) 通信。我们的调度程序将一个 MoE 层构建为管道（排列、A2A 调度、分组 GEMM 专家计算、A2A 组合和取消排列），并应用三种以 Ascend 为中心的选择来使集合远离关键路径。Expert parallelism turns routing into an all-to-all (A2A) communication in distributed LLM training. Our dispatcher structures one MoE layer as a pipeline (permutation, an A2A dispatch, grouped-GEMM expert computation, an A2A combine, and unpermutation), and applies three Ascend-centric choices to keep the collectives off the critical path.

10

2.3 加速 LLM 培训的内核优化2.3 Kernel Optimization for Accelerated LLM Training

对于 MoE 模块，令牌分发和组合都是使用异步 allto-all (A2A) 集合体实现的。同步被推迟到通信结果被消耗为止，允许独立计算在单独的流上同时执行，从而减少暴露的通信延迟。特别是对于基于 Ascend 的 MoE 训练，我们进一步隐藏了调度操作之前的同步停顿，如图 5 所示。由于在令牌路由完成之前无法确定每个专家的 A2A 分割大小，因此解决这些动态分割大小会引入一个可见的问题。For the MoE module, both token dispatch and combine are implemented using asynchronous allto-all (A2A) collectives. Synchronization is deferred until the communicated results are consumed, allowing independent computation to execute concurrently on separate streams and thereby reducing the exposed communication latency. Specifically for Ascend-based MoE training, we further hide the synchronization stall preceding the dispatch operation, as illustrated in Figure 5. Because the per-expert A2A split sizes cannot be determined until token routing is completed, resolving these dynamic split sizes introduces a visiblebefore_ep_alltoall设备时间线上的同步停止。然而，共享专家仅依赖于 MoE 层的输入，并且与路由导出的分割大小无关。因此，我们将其前向计算提前以与该同步间隔重叠。这种调度策略用有用的 Cube 计算来填充空闲期，同时保留后续的计算通信重叠，从而有效地将第 2.1 节中确定的屏障引起的延迟转换为高效执行。synchronization stall on the device timeline. The shared expert, however, depends only on the MoE-layer input and is independent of the routing-derived split sizes. We therefore advance its forward computation to overlap with this synchronization interval. This scheduling strategy fills an otherwise idle period with useful Cube computation while preserving the subsequent computation-communication overlap, effectively converting the barrier-induced latency identified in Section 2.1 into productive execution.

双缓冲交换优化器。Double-buffered Swap Optimizer.在万亿参数规模上，优化器状态成为设备内存消耗的主要来源。分布式 AdamW（Loshchilov & Hutter，2017）维护参数的 FP32 主副本以及第一和第二矩状态，与单独的模型参数相比，放大了整体 HBM 使用。在升腾 NPU 上，启用交换优化器可以缓解内存压力。At the trillion-parameter scale, optimizer states become a dominant source of device-memory consumption. Distributed AdamW (Loshchilov & Hutter, 2017) maintains an FP32 master copy of the parameters together with the first- and second-moment states, amplifying the overall HBM usage compared to the model parameters alone. On Ascend NPUs, enabling swap optimizer alleviates the memory pressure.

传统的交换优化器通过卸载优化器状态（Ren 等人，2021）以及配置后的 FP32 主参数来固定主机内存，并仅在需要参数更新时重新加载它们，从而减轻了这种压力。尽管这种方法减少了 HBM 占用空间，但它围绕每个优化器更新引入了串行化主机到设备和设备到主机传输。这些传输扩展了优化器步骤，并且可以抵消从回收的内存中获得的性能优势。A conventional swap optimizer mitigates this pressure by offloading optimizer states (Ren et al., 2021), together with the FP32 master parameters when configured, to pinned host memory and reloading them only when needed for parameter updates. Although this approach reduces the HBM footprint, it introduces serialized host-to-device and device-to-host transfers around each optimizer update. These transfers extend the optimizer step and can offset the performance benefits gained from the reclaimed memory.

为了减少这种开销，我们实现了一个双缓冲交换优化器，它将状态传输与优化器计算重叠。交换的状态被划分为块To reduce this overhead, we implement a double-bufferedt swap optimizer that overlaps state transfers with optimizer computation. The swapped states are partitioned into chunks ofswap_numel = 𝑁交换/swap/swap_optimizer_times元素，其中elements, where𝑁swap 表示交换参数的总数。当融合的 AdamW 内核更新当前块时，会从主机内存中预取下一个块，并且之前更新的块会通过专用的换入和换出流异步写回。这种流水线调度消除了关键路径中的大部分传输延迟。块大小控制临时 HBM 工作集、传输粒度和可实现的重叠之间的权衡。因此，优化器以有限的运行时开销显着降低了 HBM 消耗。回收的内存可以支持更多的激活和更大的微批次，从而减少激活重新计算和管道气泡并增加有用计算份额swap denotes the total number of swapped parameters. While a fused AdamW kernel updates the current chunk, the next chunk is prefetched from host memory and the previously updated chunk is asynchronously written back through dedicated swap-in and swap-out streams. This pipelined schedule removes most transfer latency from the critical path. The chunk size controls the trade-off among the temporary HBM working set, transfer granularity, and achievable overlap. As a result, the optimizer substantially reduces HBM consumption with limited runtime overhead. The reclaimed memory can support more activations and larger micro-batches, thereby reducing activation recomputation and pipeline bubbles and increasing the useful-computation share𝑇比较。comp.

2.3 加速 LLM 培训的内核优化2.3 Kernel Optimization for Accelerated LLM Training

2.3.1 瓶颈核分析2.3.1 Bottleneck Kernel Analysis

按照 2.1 节中的训练步骤级分解，我们进一步研究内核级的设备计算瓶颈。分析结果揭示了两个不同的低效率根源。 1 架构固有的内核（包括稀疏注意力、密集矩阵乘法和分组专家 GEMM）占用了各个调用中的大量设备时间。 2 特定于模型的阶段仍然作为 PyTorch 级别的组合实现，并由Following the training step-level decomposition in Section 2.1, we further investigate the devicecompute bottlenecks at the kernel level. The profiling results reveal two distinct sources of inefficiency. 1 The architecture-intrinsic kernels, including sparse attention, dense matrix multiplications, and grouped expert GEMMs, account for substantial device time within individual invocations. 2 Model-specific stages remain implemented as PyTorch-level compositions and are lowered by theeager mode成长序列的转换、视图、张量移动和缩减，导致过多的内核启动和碎片化的执行。这些观察结果激发了两个互补的优化方向：针对占主导地位的计算密集型内核的内核内部调整，以及针对启动碎片运营商链的融合或重新表达。into long sequences of casts, views, tensor-movement, and reductions, resulting in excessive kernel launches and fragmented execution. These observations motivate two complementary optimization directions: kernel-internal tuning for dominant compute-intensive kernels, and fusion or re-expression for launch-fragmented operator chains.

我们按照可以做出优化决策的粒度来组织分析结果。任务持续时间识别主导设备时间线的各个内核，因此是内核内部优化的候选者。内核启动计数和子模块注释揭示了高级模型阶段如何分解为低级运行时操作。对于AIV主导的内核，矢量计算、标量、MTE2加载和MTE3存储管道的利用率进一步表明执行是否受到算术、标量控制或数据移动的限制。综合起来，这些测量结果区分了计算密集型独立内核We organize the profiling results at the granularity at which optimization decisions can be made. Task Duration identifies individual kernels that dominate the device timeline and are therefore candidates for kernel-internal optimization. Kernel launch counts and submodule annotations reveal how high-level model stages are decomposed into low-level runtime operations. For AIVdominated kernels, the utilization ratios of the vector-compute, scalar, MTE2 load, and MTE3 store pipelines further indicate whether execution is constrained by arithmetic, scalar control, or data movement. Taken together, these measurements distinguish compute-intensive standalone kernels

在分析的 NPU 上，在排除通信和 AI-CPU 执行后，跟踪包含 206,503 次计算内核启动和 39.84 秒的设备端聚合持续时间。生成的内核配置文件呈现出四种重复出现的模式。第一个是架构固有内核的一个小而沉重的头，其延迟由内核主体内的计算决定。第二个是PyTorch制作的发射碎片尾部On the profiled NPUs, the trace contains 206,503 compute-kernel launches and 39.84 s of aggregate device-side compute duration after excluding communication and AI-CPU execution. The resulting kernel profile exhibits four recurring patterns. The first is a small heavy head of architecture-intrinsic kernels whose latency is dominated by computation within the kernel body. The second is a launch-fragmented tail produced by PyTorcheager computation，其中大量短期内核的开销会累积。第三种是内存绑定的向量尾部，其中执行时间主要由数据移动而不是算术决定。第四个由 Triton 生成的通用内核组成，其功能计算是正确的，但其降低并不能充分利用片上数据驻留或管道调度。第一类主要需要内核内部优化，而其余类别则激励算子融合或重新表达为 AscendC 内核，尽可能在统一缓冲区中保留中间张量。表 2 总结了代表性运算符及其向量管道特征。我们首先检查主导内核，然后转向碎片化且受内存限制的长尾。, where overhead accumulates across a large number of short-lived kernels. The third is a memory-bound vector tail, in which execution time is dominated by data movement rather than arithmetic. The fourth consists of generic Triton-generated kernels whose functional computation is correct, but whose lowering does not fully exploit on-chip data residency or pipeline scheduling. The first category primarily requires kernel-internal optimization, whereas the remaining categories motivate operator fusion or re-expression as AscendC kernels that retain intermediate tensors in Unified Buffer whenever possible. Table 2 summarizes representative operators and their vector-pipeline characteristics. We first examine the dominant kernels and then turn to the fragmented and memory-bound long tail.

我们从主要内核开始，这是一小部分特定于体系结构的操作，占设备计算时间的很大一部分。特别是，We begin with the dominant kernels, a small set of architecture-specific operations that account for a substantial fraction of device-compute time. In particular,SparseAttnSharedkvGrad是最大的个人贡献者，由于 DeepSeek 稀疏注意力的梯度计算成本高昂，占总内核计算时间的 16.86%。与经过彻底优化的普通运算符（例如矩阵乘法）不同，特定于体系结构的内核在分层内存布局和计算管道中表现出较高的代码级复杂性。这项工作通过提出的方法解决了内核复杂性的挑战is the largest individual contributor, accounting for 16.86% of the total kernel computation time due to the expensive gradient computation with respect to the DeepSeek sparse attention. Unlike ordinary operators (e.g., Matrix Multiplication) which are thoroughly optimized, architecture-specific kernels exhibits high code-level complexity in hierarchical memory layout and computation pipelines. This work addresses the challenge of kernel complexity via the proposed灵气内核AuraKernel代理，如第 2.3.2 节所述。agent, as elaborated in Section 2.3.2.

相同的跟踪还暴露了第二个执行机制。旋转位置嵌入、无重量 RMS 归一化、有限 SwiGLU 和 mHC 投影路径被实现为火炬级组合，并且 autograd 将它们扩展为通用运算符的长序列。在分析步骤中，非 matmul 急切运算符占启动次数的 91.3%，但仅占任务持续时间的 66.7%，而数量少得多的类似 matmul 的运算符则占 33.3%（图 6b）。密集的小内核尾部赋予了该政权其形状：58.0% 的发射在 20 以下完成The same trace also exposes a second execution regime. Rotary position embedding, weight-free RMS normalization, limited SwiGLU, and the mHC projection path are implemented as torch-level compositions, and autograd expands them into long sequences of generic operators. In the profiled step, non-matmul eager operators account for 91.3% of launches but only 66.7% of the Task Duration, against 33.3% for the far fewer matmul-like operators (Figure 6b). A dense small-kernel tail gives the regime its shape: 58.0% of launches finish in under 20𝜇但它们总共只贡献了任务持续时间的 2.2%。因此，性能成本是由重复和内核之间的边界引起的：张量物化、分派、调度和内存流量在链中的每个环节都付出代价。s yet together contribute only 2.2% of Task Duration. The performance cost is therefore caused by repetition and by the boundaries between kernels: tensor materialization, dispatch, scheduling, and memory traffic are paid at every link in the chain.

矢量管道计数器表明，长尾主要受到数据移动而不是算术的限制。矢量核心内核占总任务持续时间的 36.5%，但其矢量计算利用率仅为 15% 左右，远低于相应的 MTE 加载和存储比率（分别约为 68% 和 40%）（图 6c）。这种不平衡表明，大多数向量内核执行时间都消耗在加载、存储和重新排列数据上，而不是实际计算上。The vector-pipeline counters show that the long tail is primarily constrained by data movement rather than arithmetic. Vector-core kernels account for 36.5% of the total Task Duration, but their vector-compute utilization is only about 15%, substantially lower than the corresponding MTE load and store ratios of approximately 68% and 40%, respectively (Figure 6c). The imbalance indicates that most vector-kernel execution time is consumed by loading, storing, and rearranging data instead of actual computation.

运算符的分布（频率和延迟）进一步证实了长尾由碎片数据移动操作主导。The distribution of operators (frequency and latency) further confirms that the long tail is dominated by fragmented data-movement operations.Cast被调用 40,063 次，占总任务持续时间的 6.02%，而诸如is invoked 40,063 times and contributes 6.02% of the total Task Duration, while operators such asViewCopy , TensorMove , ZerosLike, 和, andSlice通过频繁的张量具体化和缓冲区移动引入额外的开销。当高级反向计算（例如旋转嵌入和有限的 SwiGLU）在急切执行下被分解为多个小内核时，通常会出现这些操作。introduce additional overhead through frequent tensor materialization and buffer movement. These operations commonly arise when high-level backward computations, such as rotary embedding and limited SwiGLU, are decomposed into multiple small kernels under eager execution.

瓶颈分析激发了两条独立的内核优化路径。架构内在内核通过改进的平铺、数据布局、调度和管道重叠在算子内进行优化，这可以通过具有长上下文理解的代理管道来执行（第 2.3.2 节）。另一方面，通过将每个目标阶段融合并重写为硬件感知的 AscendC 内核来解决碎片化的长尾问题。对于急切的 autograd 链，这需要恢复高级计算并消除框架执行引入的不必要的数据移动和类型转换。对于现有的 Triton 内核，计算在 AscendC 中重新实现，并明确控制统一缓冲区 (UB) 驻留、平铺和 MTE 调度。这两种方法都通过在片上保留中间结果并将计算合并到更少的内核通道中来减少全局内存流量。 2.3.3 节详细描述了这种融合和重写路径。The bottleneck analysis motivates two separate kernel-optimization paths. Architecture-intrinsic kernels are optimized within the operator through improved tiling, data layout, scheduling, and pipeline overlap, which can be performed via agentic pipeline with long-context understanding (Section 2.3.2). On the other hand, the fragmented long tail is addressed by fusing and rewriting each target stage as a hardware-aware AscendC kernel. For eager autograd chains, this requires recovering the high-level computation and eliminating unnecessary data movement and type conversion introduced by framework execution. For existing Triton kernels, the computation is reimplemented in AscendC with explicit control over Unified Buffer (UB) residency, tiling, and MTE scheduling. Both approaches reduce global-memory traffic by retaining intermediate results on-chip and consolidating the computation into fewer kernel passes. Section 2.3.3 describes this fusion-and-rewrite path in detail.

2.3.2 AuraKernel：复杂Ascend内核的性能优化代理2.3.2 AuraKernel: Performance Optimization Agent for Complex Ascend Kernels

在 Ascend 910C 上实现高内核效率与优化 CUDA 内核有本质区别。受益于具有多样化数据源的成熟开源社区，基于 CUDA 的内核编写已被广泛研究并用于训练最近的 LLM 模型（Dao et al., 2022; Dao, 2024; Shah et al., 2024; Tillet et al., 2019; Hsu et al., 2024; Ye et al., 2025; Wei et al., 2025；董等人，2025）。然而，由于不常见的硬件架构和编程语言，针对非 CUDA 硬件的基于代理的内核优化仍然具有挑战性。凭借细粒度的内存控制和管道，通过代理系统优化 Ascend 算子面临着两个关键挑战：Achieving high kernel efficiency on Ascend 910C is fundamentally different from optimizing CUDA kernels. Benefiting from the well-established open-source community with diverse data sources, CUDA-based kernel writing has been widely studied and used to train in recent LLM models (Dao et al., 2022; Dao, 2024; Shah et al., 2024; Tillet et al., 2019; Hsu et al., 2024; Ye et al., 2025; Wei et al., 2025; Dong et al., 2025). However, agent-based kernel optimization for non-CUDA hardware remains challenging due to uncommon hardware architecture and programming languages. With fine-grained memory control and pipeline, optimizing Ascend operators via agent system faces two critical challenges:

  • 通过分解的主机内核设计优化操作员：Operator optimization with disaggregated host-kernel design:与 CUDA 内核不同，完整的 AscendC 运算符包包括Unlike CUDA kernels, a complete AscendC operator package consists of两者both主机端调度逻辑和设备端内核实现，能够灵活控制执行参数，例如平铺、内存移动和启动配置。因此，优化 AscendC 运算符需要代理超越孤立的内核代码进行推理，并理解主机和设备端之间的显式契约。host-side scheduling logic and device-side kernel implementation, enabling flexible control over execution parameters such as tiling, memory movement, and launch configuration. Optimizing AscendC operators therefore requires the agent to reason beyond isolated kernel code and understand the explicit contract between the host and device sides.

  • 具有分层代理记忆的长上下文理解：Long-context understanding with hierarchical agent memory:复杂的 AscendC 运算符（例如稀疏注意力内核）通常涉及远程代码依赖、多阶段数据移动、流级协调和管道调度。在迭代硬件与代理交互期间，代理必须保存和检索相关设计Complex AscendC operators, such as sparse-attention kernels, often involve long-range code dependencies, multi-stage data movement, stream-level coordination, and pipeline scheduling. During iterative hardware-agent interaction, the agent must preserve and retrieve relevant design

Starting from a functionally correct baseline kernel, AuraKernel generates an optimized operator in three stages. (1)基于 OR 的平铺优化OR-based tiling optimization诊断操作员的主导状态并实例化硬件感知的参数空间，产生初始配置和一组有希望的优化方向。 (2)diagnoses the operator’s dominant regime and instantiates a hardware-aware parameter space, yielding an initial configuration and a set of promising optimization directions. (2)基于硬件的迭代优化Hardwaregrounded iterative optimization运行一个封闭的修改 - 验证 - 配置文件循环：一个工具编译每个候选者，通过返回的分析来检查其正确性，而 K-Search 使用此反馈来探索可分支、可回滚的优化方向树，并在每个经过验证的改进上提交一个检查点。 (3)runs a closed modify–verify–profile loop: a harness compiles each candidate, gates it on correctness with the returnd profiling, while K-Search uses this feedback to explore a branchable, rollbackable tree of optimization directions and commits a checkpoint on every verified improvement. (3)技能升华Skill distillation将每个经过验证的增益转换为可重复使用的技能，可检索该技能以指导后续剧集中的假设生成。converts each verified gain into a reusable skill that is retrieved to guide hypothesis generation in later episodes.

长优化轨迹中的决策、分析观察结果和正确性约束。因此，有效的分层内存对于保持优化一致性和避免重复或冲突的修改至关重要。decisions, profiling observations, and correctness constraints across long optimization trajectories. Effective hierarchical memory is therefore essential for maintaining optimization consistency and avoiding repeated or conflicting modifications.

  • 启发式优化会导致效率不佳：Heuristic optimization leads to suboptimal efficiency:现有的 AscendC 算子优化仍然严重依赖于专家设计的启发式方法来进行平铺、缓冲和管道调度。然而，这些启发式方法通常是特定于硬件、形状和操作员的，这使得它们很难在不同的工作负载中推广。因此，纯粹的启发式优化可以很容易地收敛到局部有效但全局次优的配置，从而激发更系统的优化框架，该框架将内核代理与数学建模（用于最佳初始化）结合起来，然后进行迭代硬件反馈。Existing AscendC operator optimization still heavily relies on expert-crafted heuristics for tiling, buffering, and pipeline scheduling. However, these heuristics are often hardware-, shape-, and operator-specific, making them difficult to generalize across diverse workloads. As a result, purely heuristic optimization can easily converge to locally efficient but globally suboptimal configurations, motivating a more systematic optimization framework that combines Kernel agent with mathematical modeling (for optimal initialization) followed by iterative hardware feedback.

最近的 AscendC 内核代理专注于特定领域的训练、DSL 引导生成和循环分析搜索（Cao 等人，2026b；Wen 等人，2026；Wu 等人，2025a）。然而，生产 LLM 训练引入了更严格的设置：目标内核必须根据实际应用程序形状、内存压力、布局约束以及来自端到端训练工作负载的分析信号而不是孤立的微基准进行优化。Recent AscendC kernel agents focus on domain-specific training, DSL-guided generation, and profiling-in-the-loop search (Cao et al., 2026b; Wen et al., 2026; Wu et al., 2025a). However, production LLM training introduces a stricter setting: the target kernels must be optimized under real application shapes, memory pressure, layout constraints, and profiling signals from the end-to-end training workload rather than isolated microbenchmarks.

在这项工作中，我们建议In this work, we propose灵气内核AuraKernel，AscendC 内核的性能优化代理。图 7 展示了 AuraKernel 的系统级设计。代理首先构建硬件感知的参数模型来约束优化空间；然后，它进入一个分析驱动的循环，诊断瓶颈指标，将它们映射到具体的优化策略，并应用代码重写；最后，成功的指标-策略-代码模式被提炼成可重用的技能。, a performance optimization agent for AscendC kernels. Figure 7 presents the system-level design of AuraKernel. The agent first builds a hardware-aware parameter model to constrain the optimization space; it then enters a profiling-driven loop that diagnoses bottleneck indicators, maps them to concrete optimization strategies, and applies code rewrites; finally, successful indicator–strategy–code patterns are distilled into reusable skills.

通过运筹学优化平铺策略。Optimized Tiling Strategy via Operations Research.给定预先设计的 AscendC 内核，AuraKernel 使用适合目标计算形状的求解器引导的平铺策略来初始化优化过程。 AuraKernel 不是仅仅依赖启发式搜索，而是将算子调整制定为受限性能建模问题。优化器确定如何在 AI 核心之间划分工作负载，如何通过 GM、L2、L1、L0 和 UB 分级分片，DMA 传输如何与 CUBE 或向量执行重叠，以及如何写回中间结果。Given a pre-crafted AscendC kernel, AuraKernel initializes the optimization process with a solver-guided tiling strategy tailored to the target computation shape. Instead of relying solely on heuristic search, AuraKernel formulates operator tuning as a constrained performance-modeling problem. The optimizer determines how the workload is partitioned across AI Cores, how tiles are staged through GM, L2, L1, L0, and UB, how DMA transfers are overlapped with CUBE or vector execution, and how intermediate results are written back.

14

2.3 加速 LLM 培训的内核优化2.3 Kernel Optimization for Accelerated LLM Training

给定运算符形状，AuraKernel 首先识别其主要执行机制，例如计算限制、内存限制、标量/控制限制或通信限制。然后它实例化特定于族的参数空间。对于类似 GEMM 的计算内核，参数包括 L0 切片形状、L1 阻塞因子、核心分解、跨内存级别的循环排序、L0/L1 缓冲区计数和乒乓缓冲设置。对于向量或缩减式内核，相同的原则适用于行/块粒度、UB 驻留、缩减轴放置、数据类型转换点和 MTE2/MTE3 调度。通过这种方式，性能模型充当硬件约束和代理动作之间的结构化接口。Given an operator shape, AuraKernel first identifies its dominant execution regime, such as computebound, memory-bound, scalar/control-bound, or communication-bound. It then instantiates a family-specific parameter space. For GEMM-like compute kernels, the parameters include L0 tile shapes, L1 blocking factors, core decomposition, loop ordering across memory levels, L0/L1 buffer counts, and ping-pong buffering settings. For vector- or reduction-style kernels, the same principle applies to row/block granularity, UB residency, reduction-axis placement, dtype-conversion points, and MTE2/MTE3 scheduling. In this way, the performance model serves as a structured interface between hardware constraints and agent actions.

该模型不是优化单个不透明延迟值，而是在稳态流水线执行假设下明确针对瓶颈阶段：Rather than optimizing a single opaque latency value, the model explicitly targets the bottleneck stage under a steady-state pipelined execution assumption:

受数据覆盖、L0/L1/UB 容量、合法核心分区、对齐和管道有效性约束。每个阶段的成本被构造为平铺决策的显式时钟周期级函数，定义为传输或指令计数乘以每单元周期常数，而不是单个挂钟延迟样本。每个周期常数分别适合片上存储层次结构的每个级别，GM、L2、L1 和 L0A/L0B/L0C 缓冲区均分配有不同的、独立测量的读取和写入带宽，因此给定传输的成本由其遍历的特定存储级别而不是单个平均速率决定。subject to data-coverage, L0/L1/UB capacity, legal core partitioning, alignment, and pipelinevalidity constraints. Each stage cost is constructed as an explicit clock-cycle-level function of the tiling decision, defined as a transfer or instruction count multiplied by a cycles-per-unit constant, rather than as a single wall-clock latency sample. The per-cycle constants are fitted separately for each level of the on-chip storage hierarchy, with GM, L2, L1, and the L0A/L0B/L0C buffers each assigned a distinct, independently measured read and write bandwidth, so that a given transfer’s cost is determined by the specific storage level it traverses rather than by a single averaged rate.

MTE1/MTE2 周期计数是在与布局、转置和遍历顺序配置交叉的张量形状的系统扫描下拟合的，每个配置都经过重复试验进行分析，并在周期针对传输计数和字节量进行回归之前进行平均以抑制测量噪声；根据观察到，未对齐的传输每移动一个字节会花费不成比例的更多周期，因此对传输大小的对齐约束直接从这种拟合中得出。使用相同的回归过程，将 MAC 成本拟合为每个 L0-tile 元素的周期，将 FixPipe 成本拟合为给定写回布局下每个输出-tile 元素的周期。在特定形状体系中识别的拟合误差通过显式子周期项进行纠正，调整固定的每核启动开销和每指令 MAC 成本，以便拟合的周期计数与测量的行为相匹配，而不是保持未建模。 max{·} 公式背后的稳态管道假设本身源自跨 MTE1 → MTE2 → CUBE → FixPipe 阶段序列的双缓冲乒乓 CUBE 核心执行的周期级跟踪，其中观察到四个阶段是重叠的，而不是串行执行的；因此，它们的每个周期占用率被视为并发，并取最大值而不是串行总和。尽管阶段成本函数本身仍然是周期精确的结构，而不是不透明的经验延迟，但这种周期级校准并不完全精确，因为 L2 级的缓存命中率建模得更粗糙，并且被认为是某些形状上残差预测误差的来源。MTE1/MTE2 cycle counts are fitted under a systematic sweep of tensor shapes crossed with layout, transpose, and traversal-order configurations, with each configuration profiled over repeated trials and averaged to suppress measurement noise before cycles are regressed against transfer count and byte volume; an alignment constraint on transfer size is derived directly from this fit, following the observation that misaligned transfers cost disproportionately more cycles per byte moved. MAC cost is fitted as cycles per L0-tile element, and FixPipe cost as cycles per output-tile element under a given writeback layout, using the same regression procedure. Fitting error identified in specific shape regimes is corrected with explicit sub-cycle terms, adjusting fixed per-core launch overhead and per-instruction MAC cost so that the fitted cycle counts match measured behavior rather than being left unmodeled. The steady-state pipeline assumption behind the max{·} formulation is itself derived from cycle-level traces of the double-buffered, ping-pong CUBE-core execution across the MTE1 → MTE2 → CUBE → FixPipe stage sequence, in which the four stages are observed to overlap rather than execute serially; their per-cycle occupancies are accordingly treated as concurrent, with the maximum taken rather than a serial sum. This cycle-level calibration is not uniformly exact, as cache hit rate at the L2 level is modeled more coarsely and is identified as a source of residual prediction error on some shapes, though the stage-cost functions themselves remain cycle-accurate constructions rather than opaque empirical latencies.

因此，求解器输出不仅是候选平铺键，而且还是对限制因素的诊断：操作员是否因从 GM 到 L1 的移动、L2 重用、CUBE 利用、写回或标量开销而受到瓶颈。这种诊断对于代理优化至关重要，因为它可以防止代理在看似合理但不相关的代码编辑上花费大量迭代。The solver output is therefore not merely a candidate tiling key, but also a diagnosis of the limiting factor: whether the operator is bottlenecked by movement from GM to L1, L2 reuse, CUBE utilization, writeback, or scalar overhead. This diagnosis is crucial for agentic optimization, because it prevents the agent from spending many iterations on plausible but irrelevant code edits.

所提出的 Ascend 求解器首先在 LLM 模型中常见形状的通用矩阵乘法 (MatMul) 内核上进行验证，其中The proposed Ascend solver is first validated on a common Matrix Multiplication (MatMul) kernel across frequent shapes in LLM models, with a6 . 55仅通过优化平铺策略即可减少执行时间百分比。换句话说，当使用优化的超参数启动迭代内核优化过程时，就创建了一个坚实的起点，从而避免了原本会发生的无意义的探索和令牌浪费。% execution time reduction achieved by solely optimizing the tiling strategy. In other words, a solid starting point is created when the iterative kernel optimization process is initiated with optimized hyper-parameters, thereby avoiding the meaningless exploration and token waste that would otherwise occur.

硬件接地线束和回路工程。Hardware-Grounded Harness and Loop Engineering.内核优化代理的有效性取决于三个关键基础：1可扩展且可验证的执行环境，2具有交叉迭代内存管理的强大采样机制，3可大规模编排编译、执行、验证和分析的低开销工具。如果没有这些组件，代理可能无法可靠地验证候选内核，或者将大部分优化预算用于噪声测量和冗余试验。The effectiveness of a kernel optimization agent depends on three critical foundations: 1 a scalable and verifiable execution environment, 2 a robust sampling mechanism with cross-iteration memory management, and 3 a low-overhead harness that can orchestrate compilation, execution, verification, and profiling at scale. Without these components, the agent may either fail to validate candidate kernels reliably or spend most of its optimization budget on noisy measurements and redundant trials.

考虑到这些关键因素，AuraKernel 集成了异步沙箱系统、交叉迭代优化内存和用于并行候选采样的专用编排器。客户端托管 LLM 决策循环、K-Search 编排器和持久世界模型，而Considering these key factors, AuraKernel integrates an asynchronous sandbox system, crossiteration optimization memory, and a dedicated orchestrator for parallel candidate sampling. The client side hosts the LLM decision loop, K-Search orchestrator, and persistent world model, while

2.3 加速 LLM 培训的内核优化2.3 Kernel Optimization for Accelerated LLM Training

后端在真实的升腾NPU上进行源码修改、编译、执行、正确性验证、分析和版本管理。所有与硬件相关的操作都通过稳定的 JSON-over-HTTP 工具调用公开，允许更改搜索策略或 LLM 提供程序，而无需修改后端执行系统。the backend performs source modification, compilation, execution, correctness verification, profiling, and version management on real Ascend NPUs. All hardware-related actions are exposed through stable JSON-over-HTTP tool calls, allowing the search policy or LLM provider to be changed without modifying the backend execution system.

利用高度并行的沙箱服务进行编译和执行。Compilation and execution harness with highly parallel sandbox services.后端为 AscendC 和 Triton-Ascend 操作员提供统一的接口。对于AscendC，它支持主机端平铺逻辑和设备端内核代码的联合优化。对于 Triton-Ascend，它支持元参数调整，例如网格分解、块大小、管道阶段以及 Ascend NPU 的其他后端特定配置选择。内核开发和评估在高度并行的沙箱系统中进行，该系统为各个 Ascend NPU 执行提供轻量级隔离，同时保留跨设备的可扩展并行评估。The backend provides a unified interface for both AscendC and Triton-Ascend operators. For AscendC, it supports the joint optimization of host-side tiling logic and device-side kernel code. For Triton-Ascend, it supports meta-parameter tuning such as grid decomposition, block size, pipeline stages, and other backendspecific configuration choices for Ascend NPUs. Kernel development and evaluation are conducted in a highly parallel sandbox system, which provides lightweight isolation for individual Ascend NPU executions while preserving scalable parallel evaluation across devices.

特别是，每个沙箱都维护一个独立的执行上下文，包括运行时配置、运算符依赖项、临时构建工件和分析输出，以减少跨候选者干扰并提高测量再现性。同时，独立的沙箱实例可以同时编译、执行和分析多个候选者。这种设计允许 AuraKernel 将昂贵的硬件反馈转化为可扩展的优化信号，使代理能够在实际执行约束下探索循环转换、平铺策略、缓冲选择和管道调度。对于每个候选核（In particular, each sandbox maintains an isolated execution context, including runtime configuration, operator dependencies, temporary build artifacts, and profiling outputs, to reduce cross-candidate interference and improve measurement reproducibility. Meanwhile, independent sandbox instances can compile, execute, and profile multiple candidates concurrently. This design allows AuraKernel to turn expensive hardware feedback into a scalable optimization signal, enabling the agent to explore loop transformations, tiling strategies, buffering choices, and pipeline schedules under real execution constraints. For each candidate kernel (𝑘εK），该工具首先根据用户提供的精度测试编译并执行算子，该测试对于代理来说是只读的。仅满足功能正确性谓词的实现（∈K), the harness first compiles and executes the operator against a user-provided accuracy test that is read-only to the agent. Only implementations satisfying the functional-correctness predicate (𝐶 ( 𝑘) = 1) 输入性能评估，定义可行集) = 1) enter performance evaluation, which defines the feasible set

由于硬件测量不可避免地包含噪声，因此候选者 (k’) 取代了现有的 (Because hardware measurements inevitably contain noise, a candidate (k’) replaces the incumbent (𝑘𝑏）仅当它同时通过改进阈值和重复数据删除检查时：) only when it passes both an improvement threshold and a deduplication check:

The first condition rejects changes that are indistinguishable from profiling jitter, while the second avoids redundant evaluation of textually equivalent submissions. Accepted candidates are stored as versioned checkpoints together with their source code, correctness status, measured duration, and profiling metrics.

该工具还增强了测量目标的有效性。测量边界规则禁止将张量计算从测量的内核移动到 Python 脚本或主机启动器中；操作符类型的锁可以防止跨范式替换，例如当任务需要保留原始实现范式时，将 Triton-Ascend 操作符重写为 AscendC；这些约束阻止通过更改操作员合同、绕过不受支持的硬件路径或将工作移至分析边界之外来获得直接加速。因此，每一个The harness also enforces the validity of the measured objective. A measurement-boundary rule forbids moving tensor computation from the measured kernel into the Python script or host launcher; an operator-kind lock prevents cross-paradigm replacement, such as rewriting a Triton-Ascend operator as AscendC when the task requires preserving the original implementation paradigm; These constraints prevent direct speedups obtained by changing the operator contract, bypassing unsupported hardware paths, or moving work outside the profiling boundary. Consequently, every

2.3 加速 LLM 培训的内核优化2.3 Kernel Optimization for Accelerated LLM Training

优化假设通过相同的可重现序列进行评估：修改代码、精度验证、候选分析和行为记录。optimization hypothesis is evaluated through the same reproducible sequence: modify the code, precision verification, candidate profiling, and behavior recording.

与最近的面向 CUDA 的内核代理（Chen 等人，2026b；MIT HAN Lab，2026）不同，其中预定义的知识库在指导优化中发挥着重要作用，我们的研究结果表明，静态知识库和特定于案例的优化技能可以限制 LLM 代理的探索空间，特别是在 Ascend NPU 等非传统计算平台上。Unlike recent CUDA-oriented kernel agents (Chen et al., 2026b; MIT HAN Lab, 2026), where the pre-defined knowledge bases play an important role in guiding optimization, our findings suggest that static knowledge bases and case-specific optimization skills can constrain the exploration space of LLM agents, especially on non-traditional computing platforms such as Ascend NPUs.

相比之下，AuraKernel 依赖于自我进化的内存和自我维护的技能，这些技能通过硬件验证的反馈不断更新。在迭代搜索过程中，成功的转换、失败的编辑、分析诊断和版本化性能记录都会累积到持久内存中，从而允许代理在候选者之间细化其优化策略，而不是重复依赖固定的逐例规则。这种反馈驱动的内存机制使 AuraKernel 能够在复杂的 AscendC 内核和 Triton-Ascend 运算符上实现直接性能改进，如第 4 节所示。In contrast, AuraKernel relies on self-evolved memory and a self-maintained Skills that are continuously updated through hardware-verified feedback. During iterative search, successful transformations, failed edits, profiling diagnoses, and versioned performance records are accumulated into persistent memory, allowing the agent to refine its optimization strategy across candidates rather than repeatedly relying on fixed, case-by-case rules. This feedback-driven memory mechanism enables AuraKernel to achieve direct performance improvements on both complex AscendC kernels and Triton-Ascend operators, as shown in Section 4.

K-Search 优化路径探索。K-Search optimization-path exploration.在此工具之上，我们采用 K-Search（Cao 等人，2026a）将候选生成组织为有状态、可分支和可回滚的树搜索（以下称为世界模型），而不是单个线性“查看-编辑-配置文件”循环。它的根是经过验证的基线，其内部节点描述了平铺粒度调整、MTE2缩减和管道交织等优化方向，其叶子是具体的代码更改尝试。每个叶子都注释有其预测增益、置信度、实现难度、访问计数、正确性状态、分析结果和度量向量转换。On top of this harness, we adopt K-Search (Cao et al., 2026a) to organize candidate generation as a stateful, branchable, and rollbackable tree search, referred to hereafter as the world model, rather than a single linear “view–edit–profile” loop. Its root is the verified baseline, its internal nodes describe optimization directions such as tiling-granularity adjustment, MTE2 reduction, and pipeline interleaving, and its leaves are concrete code-change attempts. Each leaf is annotated with its predicted gain, confidence, implementation difficulty, visit count, correctness status, profiling result, and metric-vector transition.

在每次迭代中，K-Search 从当前边界中选择一个叶子At each iteration, K-Search selects a leaf from the current frontier𝐿(T) 使用上置信度获取函数来平衡预期收益、探索和实现难度：(T) using an upper-confidence acquisition function that balances expected gain, exploration, and implementation difficulty:

Directions related to an unexpectedly effective candidate are promoted, whereas those associated with an underperforming candidate are demoted, and this adjustment is what carries the evaluated leaf’s outcome into the selection score of its still-unevaluated siblings. The WorldModel is thus continuously recalibrated by measurements from the actual NPU rather than by the LLM’s initial confidence alone.

当路径未通过正确性检查、延迟回归或达到性能稳定状态时，K-Search 会回滚到之前验证的检查点并打开一个新分支。这使得能够对耦合的平铺、缓冲、内存布局和管道调度空间进行非单调探索，同时保护全局现有者。每个周期都受到分析调用的限制，When a path fails correctness checks, regresses in latency, or reaches a performance plateau, K- Search rolls back to a previously verified checkpoint and opens a new branch. This enables nonmonotonic exploration of the coupled tiling, buffering, memory-layout, and pipeline-scheduling space while protecting the global incumbent. Each cycle is governed by limits on profiling calls,

2.3 加速 LLM 培训的内核优化2.3 Kernel Optimization for Accelerated LLM Training

工具调用、连续的非改进尝试以及全局周期上限。对于跨越数十个周期的搜索，三层上下文压缩机制使记录易于处理，而不会丢弃可恢复状态：失败的分析轮次，跨越自上次成功分析以来的每条消息，被折叠成一个摘要，记录失败原因和最后验证的检查点；同一文件的重复视图被引用最新视图的紧凑标记所取代，并由文件和板键入；重复的目录列表以相同的方式进行重复数据删除，由操作员和董事会键入。因此，harness 和 K-Search 形成了同一循环的互补部分：前者确定修改是否正确且速度明显更快，而后者确定下一步尝试的优化方向以及硬件证据应如何重塑剩余搜索。tool calls, and consecutive non-improving attempts, together with a global cycle cap. For searches spanning dozens of cycles, a three-layer context-compression mechanism keeps the transcript tractable without discarding recoverable state: a failed profiling round, spanning every message since the last successful profile, is collapsed into a single summary recording the failure cause and the last verified checkpoint; repeated views of the same file are replaced by a compact marker referencing the most recent view, keyed by file and board; and repeated directory listings are deduplicated the same way, keyed by operator and board. The harness and K-Search therefore form complementary halves of the same loop: the former establishes whether a modification is correct and measurably faster, while the latter determines which optimization direction to attempt next and how hardware evidence should reshape the remaining search.

技能蒸馏和持续改进。Skill distillation and continual improvement.对于实现验证增益的每个检查点，代理从代码更改之前和之后观察到的度量向量转换中提取结构化优化技能，表示为三元组For each checkpoint that achieves a verified gain, the agent distills a structured optimization skill from the metric-vector transition observed before and after the code change, represented as a triple

有温度with temperature𝛽>0. 这些技能存储在经验库中，并在后续阶段中检索以条件 LLM 假设生成，因此随着经验库的发展，代理逐渐积累可转移的 AscendC 优化知识。所有运行都会发出完整的、可重播的工件（包括序列化的世界模型），因此可以从另一台机器上的中间周期恢复优化。实际上，AuraKernel 将内核优化重塑为一个可自动化的搜索问题，其中 LLM 的假设生成和 NPU 的客观测量不断相互校准，在严格的预算内向接近最优的 AscendC 实现收敛。不可否认的是0. These skills are stored in an experience bank and retrieved to condition LLM hypothesis generation in subsequent episodes, so the agent progressively accumulates transferable AscendC optimization knowledge as the bank grows. All runs emit complete, replayable artifacts— including the serialized world model—so an optimization can be resumed from an intermediate cycle on another machine. In effect, AuraKernel recasts kernel optimization as an automatable search problem in which the LLM’s hypothesis generation and the NPU’s objective measurement continually calibrate each other, converging toward near-optimal AscendC implementations within a strict budget. Admittedly the

应用于 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 训练（第 4.1 节）时，AuraKernel 在端到端训练环境中将共享键值稀疏注意力后向步骤（单个最大的设备时间贡献者）加速了高达 1.24 倍，并且在单个活动中优化了 14 个大量减少的框架运算符，使其完全正确，平均速度提高了 2.06 倍，最佳加速比提高了 11.90 倍。与我们的瓶颈分析一致，这些收益来自减少冗余全局内存流量，而不是提高原始计算利用率。Applied to DeepSeek-V4-Pro training (Section 4.1), AuraKernel accelerates the shared-key-value sparse-attention backward step, the single largest device-time contributor, by up to 1.24× in its end-to-end training context, and in a single campaign optimizes 14 reduction-heavy framework operators to full correctness with a 2.06× average and an 11.90× best speedup. Consistent with our bottleneck analysis, these gains come from cutting redundant global-memory traffic rather than from raising raw compute utilization.

2.3.3 升腾NPU感知内核融合与重写2.3.3 Ascend NPU-aware Kernel Fusion and Rewrite

第二条优化路径解决了瓶颈分析的三种非头模式——启动碎片急切尾部、内存绑定向量尾部和通用 Triton 内核。尽管 AuraKernel 改进了已经很大的算子的主体，但该路径将每个目标阶段重新表示为一个或少量硬件仿射 AscendC 内核。前两种模式作为急切的 Python 和火炬链到达运行时，第三种作为通用 Triton 内核，因此所有三种模式都崩溃到上面介绍的两个条目。对于急切链，融合从跟踪中恢复高级操作，将发出的内核分类为模型算术、形状簿记、数据移动、精度转换和自动梯度写回，并折叠链。对于Triton内核，算法不变，但算子在AscendC中重建，具有片上驻留和调度通用降低不能表达。这两个条目重叠：无权 RMS 归一化和 mHC 预投影收缩同时融合和重写，因为它们的 Triton 内核也带有可避免的阶段边界。系统级通信和管道瓶颈仍然超出了这个范围。在这两种情况下，算法都没有改变，只是运行的基板发生了变化。The second optimization path addresses the three non-head patterns of the bottleneck analysis— the launch-fragmented eager tail, the memory-bound vector tail, and the generic Triton kernels. Whereas AuraKernel improves the body of already-large operators, this path re-expresses each target stage as one or a small number of hardware-affine AscendC kernels. The first two patterns reach the runtime as eager Python and torch chains and the third as generic Triton kernels, so all three collapse onto the two entries introduced above. For the eager chains, fusion recovers the high-level operation from the trace, classifies the emitted kernels into model arithmetic, shape bookkeeping, data movement, precision conversion, and autograd writeback, and collapses the chain. For the Triton kernels, the arithmetic is unchanged but the operator is rebuilt in AscendC with the on-chip residency and scheduling the generic lowering cannot express. The two entries overlap: the weight-free RMS normalization and the mHC pre-projection contraction are simultaneously fused and rewritten, because their Triton kernels also carried avoidable stage boundaries. The system-level communication and pipeline bottlenecks remain outside this scope. In both cases the arithmetic is unchanged, and only the substrate on which it runs changes.

在较高的层面上，数学契约记录了融合实现必须保留的公式，包括钳位次梯度、复杂的 RoPE 旋转、加权扩展、缩减和数据类型敏感的累加。前端工件记录标识由于 Python 表达式、视图语义、数据类型提升或自动分级扩展而出现在运行时跟踪中的运算符，例如强制转换、视图、填充、张量移动、串联和分散式写回。On a high level, the mathematical contract records the formula that the fused implementation must preserve, including clamp subgradients, complex RoPE rotation, weighted expansion, reduction, and dtype-sensitive accumulation. The frontend-artifact record identifies operators that appear in the runtime trace because of the Python expression, view semantics, dtype promotion, or autograd expansion, such as casts, views, fills, tensor moves, concatenations, and scatter-style writebacks.

18

2.3 加速 LLM 培训的内核优化2.3 Kernel Optimization for Accelerated LLM Training

硬件执行合约指定了 Ascend 计划：哪些张量驻留在 UB 中，如何通过 MTE2/MTE3 安排 GM-UB 移动，在哪里进行缩减或广播，以及每个边界携带哪种 dtype。这种结构使每个融合都被视为数学保留重写，而不是局部窥视孔优化的集合。The hardware execution contract specifies the Ascend plan: which tensors are resident in UB, how GM–UB movement is scheduled through MTE2/MTE3, where reductions or broadcasts occur, and which dtype is carried at each boundary. This structure lets each fusion be reviewed as a mathematics-preserving rewrite rather than as a collection of local peephole optimizations.

生成的内核遵循三个面向硬件的设计规则。首先，只要中间张量的唯一作用是连接相邻的急切运算符，它们就会保留在统一缓冲区内。其次，内核接口返回整行或整个阶段输出，因此首先不会生成切片分配和分散式自动分级扩展。第三，数据类型转换遵循阶段的数字契约，而不是从前端表达式继承的保守数据类型。这些规则不会删除模型算术。相反，它们改变了算术的执行位置以及周围内存流量的支付次数。我们将此方法应用于三个代表性目标，按照整个评估中使用的顺序呈现：mHC 压缩套件、有限的 SwiGLU 向后和统一旋转嵌入。 mHC 预投影路径的混合精度数据流重写是与第一个路径一起开发的，并在下面的图集中显示为单独的行。图 8 是这些重写的方法概述。它将每个案例分为观察到的跟踪、用于审核正确性的语义契约、通过融合删除的前端工件以及实现修订后的数据流的 AscendC 边界。The resulting kernels follow three hardware-facing design rules. First, intermediate tensors remain inside the Unified Buffer whenever their only role is to connect adjacent eager operators. Second, the kernel interface returns whole rows or whole stage outputs, so that slice-assignment and scatterstyle autograd expansions are not generated in the first place. Third, dtype transitions follow the numerical contract of the stage rather than the conservative dtype inherited from a frontend expression. These rules do not remove model arithmetic. Instead, they change where the arithmetic is executed and how many times the surrounding memory traffic is paid. We apply this method to three representative targets, presented in the order used throughout the evaluation: the mHC compression suite, limited SwiGLU backward, and unified rotary embedding. A mixed-precision dataflow rewrite of the mHC pre-projection path is developed alongside the first, and appears as its own row in the atlas below. Figure 8 serves as the method overview for these rewrites. It separates each case into the observed trace, the semantic contract used to audit correctness, the frontend artifacts removed by fusion, and the AscendC boundary that implements the revised dataflow.

图8：Figure 8:运营商链融合图集。行显示了四个 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 重写：mHC 加权扩展、mHC 混合精度数据流、有限的 SwiGLU 向后（具有路由权重梯度）和统一 RoPE。列给出了发出的跟踪、语义不变、删除的前端工件和融合的 AscendC 边界。Operator-chain fusion atlas. Rows show four DeepSeek-V4-Pro rewrites: mHC weighted expansion, mHC mixed-precision dataflow, limited SwiGLU backward (with routing-weight gradient) and unified RoPE. Columns give the emitted trace, semantic invariant, removed frontend artifacts and fused AscendC boundary.

mHC 融合套件。mHC fusion suite.流形约束超连接 (mHC) 路径（Xie et al., 2025）是同一问题的一个更大的实例，并且它不是由单个融合算子表示的。它还同时执行该路径的两个条目：无重量 RMS 归一化和预投影收缩起源于通用 Triton 内核，因此它们重写到 AscendC 中是基底更改，而周围的转换和元素累积是通过阶段边界融合移除的急切片段。其预投影阶段结合了无权重 RMS 归一化、一个小投影（其输出通过 Sinkhorn 步骤归一化为每通道混合权重）以及由这些权重驱动的批量加权扩展。其后投影阶段将另一个加权扩展与累积张量和最终 BF16 输出相结合。在原始跟踪中，这些语义显示为The Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) path (Xie et al., 2025) is a larger instance of the same problem, and it is not represented by a single fused operator. It also exercises both entries of this path at once: the weight-free RMS normalization and the preprojection contraction originated as generic Triton kernels, so their rewrite into AscendC is a substrate change, while the surrounding casts and elementwise accumulation are eager fragments removed by a stage-boundary fusion. Its pre-projection stage combines weight-free RMS normalization, a small projection whose output is normalized by a Sinkhorn step into per-channel mixture weights, and a batched weighted expansion driven by those weights. Its post-projection stage combines another weighted expansion with an accumulated tensor and a final BF16 output. In the original trace, these semantics appear asPows / ReduceMean / Add / Rsqrt , Cast , MatMul、自定义 BMM 内核和元素累加。成本分散在标准化、转换和围绕投影的移动中，而不是集中在一个 matmul 中。图 9 总结了使该路径在 NPU 上高效的三种机制：加权扩展 (a)、二维平铺和管道 (b) 以及混合精度数据流 (c)。, custom BMM kernels, and elementwise accumulation. The cost is spread across normalization, conversion, and movement around the projection rather than concentrated in one matmul. Figure 9 summarizes the three mechanisms that make this path efficient on the NPU: the weighted expansion (a), the two-dimensional tiling and pipeline (b), and the mixed-precision dataflow (c).

WindMhcPostPart处理后投影阶段。它将批次和序列维度扁平化为handles the post-projection stage. It flattens the batch and sequence dimensions into𝑇 = 𝐵𝑆，计算加权展开, computes the weighted expansion𝑥 [ 𝑡 , 𝑑 ] ℎ发布[post [𝑡 , 𝑛]，累积现有的], accumulates the existing𝑏2 张量，并直接发出 BF16 输出。内核平铺在行上2 tensor, and emits BF16 output directly. The kernel tiles over rows𝑇和隐藏维度and hidden dimension𝐷：行在核心之间分割以提供独立的令牌级工作，而: rows are split across the cores to provide independent token-level work, while𝐷被平铺为 512 个 B 对齐块，这些块限制了 UB 驻留和 MTE 传输粒度。这种二维平铺是 mHC 内核的通用硬件契约。它允许实现在扩展、累积和输出转换中重用本地缓冲区，并在融合运算符内部安排 MTE2/VEC/MTE3 移动，而不是按框架级内核边界安排。is tiled into 512 B-aligned blocks that bound UB residency and MTE transfer granularity. This two-dimensional tiling is the common hardware contract across the mHC kernels. It lets the implementation reuse local buffers across expansion, accumulation, and output conversion, with MTE2/VEC/MTE3 movement scheduled inside the fused operator rather than by framework-level kernel boundaries.

预投影侧将相同的边界原理应用于缩减和加权扩展，这也是基底重写最明确的地方：无权 RMSNorm 和 preBMM 收缩以前是通用的 Triton 内核，在 AscendC 中重建它们是暴露亲和性论证所依赖的片上控制的地方。 RMS 归一化前向和后向内核融合了平方、归约、epsilon 加法、倒数平方根和梯度扩展，以便行标量计算一次并在本地重复使用。The pre-projection side applies the same boundary principle to reductions and weighted expansion, and it is also where the substrate rewrite is most explicit: the weight-free RMSNorm and the preBMM contraction were previously generic Triton kernels, and rebuilding them in AscendC is what exposes the on-chip control the affinity argument relies on. The RMS-normalization forward and backward kernels fuse the square, reduction, epsilon addition, reciprocal square root, and gradient expansion so that the row scalar is computed once and reused locally.WindHcPreBmmForward进行前向加权展开carries the forward weighted expansion𝑦 [ 𝑡 , 𝑑 ] =� 𝑛ℎ前pre[𝑡,𝑛]𝑥[𝑡,𝑛,𝑑]安德拉]undera𝑇–𝐷_平铺，广泛-𝐷_tiling,broad-铸造每个 FP32 _ℎcasting each FP32 _ℎ预系数与pre coefficient with aBrcb指令并消耗 BF16 激活，以便四通道收缩在一个 UB 驻留通道中完成，而不是在 matmul-plus-cast 链中完成。然后，单个向量乘法将一个广播系数应用于整个特征元素寄存器，并且带有乒乓缓冲的三级 MTE2/VEC/MTE3 管道将下一个图块的负载与当前图块的计算和存储重叠——通用 Triton 降低留给全局内存往返的显式驻留、广播和管道调度。这种底层变化使前 BMM 从移动绑定转向计算绑定，第 4.2 节报告了测得的加速以及矢量计算占用率的相应增长。instruction and consuming BF16 activations, so that the four-channel contraction is completed in one UB-resident pass instead of a matmul-plus-cast chain. A single vector multiply then applies one broadcast coefficient across a full register of feature elements, and a three-stage MTE2/VEC/MTE3 pipeline with ping-pong buffering overlaps the load of the next tile with the compute and store of the current one—the explicit residency, broadcast, and pipeline scheduling that a generic Triton lowering leaves to global-memory round trips. This substrate change moves the pre-BMM forward from movement-bound to compute-bound, and Section 4.2 reports the measured speedup and the corresponding rise in vector-compute occupancy.

有限 SwiGLU 的落后。Backward of Limited SwiGLU.有限的 SwiGLU 激活为标准门控产品添加了预 SiLU 钳位：门的一半被上限裁剪，上半部分在乘法之前被对称地裁剪。以急切的形式，向后传递将这个紧凑的数学规则扩展为包含The limited SwiGLU activation adds a pre-SiLU clamp to the standard gated product: the gate half is clipped by an upper bound and the up half is clipped symmetrically before multiplication. 融合边界将两个梯度半部直接写入其输出偏移量，从而消除了串联和前端可见的布尔组合链。 BF16/FP16 输入被提升一次以进行局部导数计算，而 sigmoid、钳位掩码和中间产品保留在 UB 中，并且结果仅在输出边界处转换。我们象征性地验证了该公式，并根据 PyTorch autograd 黄金参考（包括钳位边界情况）进行了验证。不支持的数据类型和非正限制会回退到原始路径。The fused boundary writes the two gradient halves directly to their output offsets, removing the concatenation and the frontend-visible boolean-composition chain. BF16/FP16 inputs are promoted once for the local derivative computation, while the sigmoid, clamp masks, and intermediate products remain in UB and the result is converted only at the output boundary. We verified the formula symbolically and against a PyTorch autograd golden reference, including clamp-boundary cases. Unsupported dtypes and non-positive limits fall back to the original path.

在 MoE 设置中，相同的内核还融合了路由梯度缩减，急切路径将其作为自己的链发出。每个专家激活都按每个令牌路由权重进行缩放In the MoE setting the same kernel also fuses the routing-gradient reduction, which the eager path emits as its own chain.
