统一旋转嵌入。Unified rotary embedding.旋转位置嵌入出现在具有不同周围环境的三个注意位点中。查询路径首先乘以公开的 RMS 归一化比例，然后旋转 RoPE 尾部。压缩键值路径应用 RoPE，无需外部规模并使用本地频率。输出投影路径应用反向旋转，并且之前依赖于克隆加缀合物构造。这些站点在前端表达方面有所不同，但它们共享相同的阶段边界：保留全行前缀，旋转 RoPE 尾部，并返回行而不将切片分配暴露给 autograd。在向后传递中，暴露该赋值会产生Rotary position embedding appears in three attention sites with different surrounding context. The query path first multiplies by the exposed RMS-normalization scale and then rotates the RoPE tail. The compressed key-value path applies RoPE without an external scale and uses local frequencies. The output-projection path applies the inverse rotation and previously relied on a clone plus conjugate construction. These sites differ in frontend expression, but they share the same stage boundary: preserve a full-row prefix, rotate the RoPE tail, and return the row without exposing slice assignment to autograd. In the backward pass, exposing that assignment producesZerosLike , TensorMove , ViewCopy , Cast, 和, andAdd围绕实际旋转梯度的链。图 11 总结了统一运算符应用的三种机制。chains around the actual rotation gradient. Figure 11 summarizes the three mechanisms the unified operator applies.

我们用一名前向操作员和一名后向操作员实施三个站点。该运算符将传递前缀和旋转尾部作为整行输出返回，因此不会向 autograd 公开任何切片分配，并且永远不会生成后向散射链（图 11a）。因为We implement the three sites with one forward operator and one backward operator. The operator returns the pass-through prefix and the rotated tail as a whole-row output, so no slice assignment is exposed to autograd and the backward scatter chain is never generated (Figure 11a). Because the

Fusion replaces the entire chain with a single UB-residentSwiGluLimitBackwardV2内核将 sigmoid、钳位掩码、中间产品和路由梯度缩减保留在芯片上，并且从不将它们具体化到全局内存中。kernel that keeps the sigmoid, clamp masks, intermediate products, and routing-gradient reduction on chip and never materializes them to global memory.

目标硬件没有原生复数类型，该运算符在实际算术中再现复数旋转 Su 等人。 (2024)：它将每个相邻元素对视为一个复数，并通过余弦乘法、相邻对交换和符号翻转正弦对其进行旋转，以便单个向量代码路径无需复数构造链即可实现复数乘积（图 11b）。正向和反向旋转通过正弦项的符号共享一个内核，并且同一内核服务于 q、kv 和 o，因为特定位置的差异减少到规模、频率和方向参数，而不是单独的运算符实现。target hardware has no native complex type, the operator reproduces the complex rotation in real arithmetic Su et al. (2024): it treats each adjacent element pair as one complex number and rotates it with a cosine multiply, an adjacent-pair swap, and a sign-flipped sine, so that a single vector code path realizes the complex product without a complex-construction chain (Figure 11b). Forward and inverse rotation share one kernel through the sign of the sine term, and the same kernel serves q, kv, and o because site-specific differences reduce to scale, frequency, and direction arguments rather than separate operator implementations.

后向核遵循相同的边界选择，将旋转导数和尺度梯度缩减保留在融合算子内。查询路径仍然向后提供数学上所需的 RMS 归一化，并保留两个梯度分支的最终累加。在孤立的每个站点验证中，统一算子涵盖 q、kv 和 o，相对误差在可接受的训练容差范围内。The backward kernel follows the same boundary choice, keeping the rotation derivative and the scale-gradient reduction inside the fused operator. The query path still feeds the mathematically required RMS-normalization backward and preserves the final accumulation of the two gradient branches. In isolated per-site validation, the unified operator covers q, kv, and o with relative error within the accepted training tolerance.

运算符消耗实余弦和正弦张量，这些张量是通过准备链从复数频率表生成的The operator consumes real cosine and sine tensors, which are produced from the complex frequency table by a preparation chain ofRepeatInterleave , StridedSlice , Neg, 和, andPack。在图中左侧，该链重新执行每个步骤，即使频率仅取决于位置并且不会随步骤变化。因此，我们将转换后的张量缓存在从频率表派生的键下，以便在第一步之后缓存命中，整个准备链及其内存移动流量都会被跳过（图 11c）。缓存被锁定，以便跨步骤的稳定频率表保持下游查找有效，这使得准备成本在稳定状态下降至零。. Left in the graph, this chain re-executes every step, even though the frequencies depend only on position and do not change across steps. We therefore cache the converted tensors under a key derived from the frequency table, so that after the first step the cache hits and the entire preparation chain, together with its memory-movement traffic, is skipped (Figure 11c). The cache is keyed so that a stable frequency table across steps keeps the downstream lookup valid, which is what lets the preparation cost fall to zero in steady state.

图11：Figure 11:统一旋转包埋机的三种机构。 (a) 急切的就地切片分配和它强制的全角向后分散，与避免它的整行输出形成对比。 (b) 复数旋转的实算实现，显示产生旋转对的余弦乘法、相邻对交换和符号翻转正弦。 (c) 每步余弦/正弦准备链与其缓存对应物的对比，其中步不变频率表允许在第一步之后跳过该链。Three mechanisms of the unified rotary-embedding operator. (a) The eager in-place slice assignment and the full-width backward scatter it forces, contrasted with the whole-row output that avoids it. (b) The real-arithmetic realization of the complex rotation, showing the cosine multiply, adjacent-pair swap, and sign-flipped sine that produce the rotated pair. (c) The per-step cosine/sine preparation chain against its cached counterpart, where a step-invariant frequency table lets the chain be skipped after the first step.

  1. 面向 OR 的训练后工作流程OR-Oriented Post-Training Workflow

混合精度数据流。Mixed-precision dataflow.mHC 预投影路径还包含阶段范围的精确伪影。 Python 实现执行了早期The mHC pre-projection path also contained a stage-wide precision artifact. The Python implementation performed an earlyx.float()展平输入后，在 RMS 归一化、投影和 BMM 之前将激活张量从 BF16 提升到 FP32。由于周围的训练路径是 BF16 混合精度，因此这种提升强制进行全阶段往返：激活、梯度和中间累加以 FP32 宽度重复转换或移动，并将最终输出转换回来。在配置文件中，mHC 预投影阶段每步被调用数百次（896 次前向调用和 448 次后向调用）；的after flattening the input, which promoted the activation tensor from BF16 to FP32 before RMS normalization, the projection, and the BMM. Because the surrounding training path is BF16 mixed precision, this promotion forced a full-stage round trip: activations, gradients, and intermediate accumulations were repeatedly converted or moved at FP32 width, and the final outputs were converted back. In the profile, the mHC pre-projection stage is invoked hundreds of times per step (896 forward and 448 backward calls); thex.float()因此选择放大为可见的choice therefore amplifies into visibleCast内核并使前向和后向链的传输量加倍。至关重要的是，这个阶段是受内存限制的，而不是受计算限制的：其主要算子以仅 ≈ 0.06 的 AIV 矢量计算比运行，而 MTE2 负载比为 ≈ 0.87（kernels and doubled transfer volume across the forward and backward chains. Crucially, this stage is memory-bound rather than compute-bound: its dominant operators run at an AIV vector-compute ratio of only ≈ 0.06 against an MTE2 load ratio of ≈ 0.87 (Pows 0.97, ReduceMean 0.77, Cast0.87；图 9c)，因此挂起时间由跨全局内存的字节数来设置，并将整个链提升为 FP32，使其边界数量恰好加倍。最大的推动因素是入场0.87; Figure 9c), so the wall time is set by how many bytes cross global memory, and promoting the whole chain to FP32 doubles exactly the quantity that bounds it. The single largest mover is the entryCast从 BF16 到 FP32，每步 222 毫秒。from BF16 to FP32, at 222 ms per step.

我们通过明确混合精度契约来消除向上转换，核心举措是改变操作数跨全局内存的数据类型。由于该链受 MTE2 约束，因此内核从 BF16 中的全局内存加载激活，并将其转换为向量核心内的 FP32，而不是加载已升级的 FP32 张量。这使得限定舞台的管道的负载量减半，同时保持精度，因为 FP32 转换仍然在任何减少之前发生。 RMS 归一化内核接受 BF16 激活，同时在减少需要时保留 FP32 倒数平方根值和 FP32 内部算术。该投影通过 FP32 累加计算 BF16×BF16 乘积，并发出下游阶段所需的 dtype。 BMM前向消耗We remove the up-cast by making the mixed-precision contract explicit, and the central move is to shift the dtype at which operands cross global memory. Because the chain is MTE2-bound, the kernels load activations from global memory in BF16 and convert them to FP32 inside the vector core, rather than loading an already-promoted FP32 tensor. This halves the load volume of exactly the pipe that bounds the stage while preserving accuracy, since the FP32 conversion still happens before any reduction. The RMS-normalization kernels accept BF16 activations while retaining FP32 reciprocal-square-root values and FP32 internal arithmetic where the reduction requires it. The projection computes BF16×BF16 products with FP32 accumulation and emits the dtype required by the downstream stage. The pre-BMM forward consumesℎ预置在 FP32 和pre in FP32 and𝑥入BF16，并返回BF16输出；其向后返回 BF16 ∇in BF16, and returns BF16 output; its backward returns BF16 ∇𝑥和 FP32 ∇and FP32 ∇ℎ预。权重梯度路径类似地返回具有匹配数据类型的梯度作为原始 BF16 权重，而不是保留 FP32 张量，因为相应的激活被向上转换以进行计算。pre. The weight-gradient path similarly returns gradient with the matched data type as the original BF16 weight, rather than retaining the FP32 tensors because the corresponding activation was upcast for computation.

此更改在代码中很小，但效果很广泛，因为它更改了整个 mHC 子图所携带的 dtype。该分析识别每个删除或缩小的运动：初始激活转换、在 matmul 之前插入的权重转换、前向和后向 BMM 输入/输出转换、梯度添加转换以及最终的转换This change is small in code but broad in effect because it changes the dtype carried by the whole mHC subgraph. The analysis identifies each removed or narrowed movement: the initial activation cast, weight casts inserted before matmul, forward and backward BMM input/output casts, gradientadd casts, and the finalx.float()向后转换。机会的规模仅由条目强制转换决定，FP32 提升在前向传播中将其膨胀到 241 毫秒，因此改变操作数跨全局内存的 dtype 会作用于主导项而不是边缘项。 4.2 节报告了组合阶段前后具体的减少量，以及融合后测得的端到端模块加速。正确性是由操作员争论的：要求 FP32 在内部保留 FP32 的归约和累加，而张量范围的 BF16 值在阶段边界处保持 BF16，与周围训练系统使用的混合精度合约相匹配。backward conversion. The scale of the opportunity is set by the entry cast alone, which the FP32 promotion inflates to 241 ms in the forward pass, so shifting the dtype at which operands cross global memory acts on the dominant term rather than a marginal one. The concrete before-and-after reduction across the combined stage, together with the end-to-end module speedups measured after fusion, is reported in Section 4.2. Correctness is argued operator by operator: reductions and accumulations that require FP32 retain FP32 internally, while tensor-wide BF16 values remain BF16 at the stage boundaries, matching the mixed-precision contract used by the surrounding training system.

总而言之，这种融合和重写将长尾诊断转变为实施规则。在这四个目标中，重复的动作是相同的：从前端扩展中恢复模型级操作，保持其数学契约固定，并选择与 NPU 相遇的粒度和基底，无论起点是急切的 autograd 链还是通用的 Triton 内核。改变的不是算术，而是它的运行位置以及操作数跨全局内存的频率。这些融合的模块级效果在第 4.2 节中报告，它们对端到端训练吞吐量的总体贡献在第 2 节中与其他操作员级优化一起报告。Taken together, this fusion and rewrite turns the long-tail diagnosis into an implementation discipline. Across the four targets the recurring move is the same: recover the model-level operation from its frontend expansion, hold its mathematical contract fixed, and choose the granularity and substrate at which it meets the NPU, whether the starting point was an eager autograd chain or a generic Triton kernel. What changes is not the arithmetic but where it runs and how often its operands cross global memory. The module-level effect of these fusions is reported in Section 4.2, and their aggregate contribution to end-to-end training throughput is reported with the other operator-level optimizations in Section 2.

3 面向 OR 的训练后工作流程3 OR-Oriented Post-Training Workflow

在第 2 节中建立的系统基础架构的基础上，我们进一步探索如何使 DeepSeek-V4（DeepSeek-AI 等人，2026）系列适应运筹学（OR）的复杂领域。我们使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 作为面向 OR 的后训练管道的验证模型。 DeepSeek-V4-Flash保留了V4家族的核心结构化推理能力，经过验证的管道可以直接作为DeepSeek-V4-Pro等更大模型的蓝图。Building on the system infrastructure established in Section 2, we further explore how to adapt the DeepSeek-V4 (DeepSeek-AI et al., 2026) family to the complex domain of Operations Research (OR). We use DeepSeek-V4-Flash as a validation model for the OR-oriented post-training pipeline. DeepSeek-V4-Flash retains the core structured reasoning capabilities of the V4 family, and the verified pipelines can directly serve as a blueprint for larger models such as DeepSeek-V4-Pro.

运筹学 (OR) 建模为评估面向领域的培训后提供了一个要求严格的环境。模型必须将自然语言要求转换为变量、目标、约束、数据接口和求解器可执行程序，同时保留数学Operations Research (OR) modeling provides a demanding setting for evaluating domain-oriented post-training. A model must translate natural-language requirements into variables, objectives, constraints, data interfaces, and solver-executable programs, while preserving the mathematical

底层优化问题的结构。最近对 LLM 优化的研究表明，这个过程不仅仅涉及答案生成：它需要语义理解、符号表述、解算器感知实现和结构敏感验证（Xiao 等人，2025；Wang & Li，2025；Mostajabdaveh 等人，2025；Wang 等人，2025b）。这些属性使 OR 成为检查训练后是否可以提高可执行行为和结构建模能力的合适领域。structure of the underlying optimization problem. Recent studies on LLMs for optimization have shown that this process involves more than answer generation: it requires semantic understanding, symbolic formulation, solver-aware implementation, and structure-sensitive verification (Xiao et al., 2025; Wang & Li, 2025; Mostajabdaveh et al., 2025; Wang et al., 2025b). These properties make OR a suitable domain for examining whether post-training can improve both executable behavior and structural modeling competence.

作为 DeepSeek-V4 系列的轻量级成员（DeepSeek-AI et al., 2026），DeepSeek-V4Flash 能够快速迭代数据构建、持续预训练、监督微调、检查点选择和基准评估，同时保留实际垂直领域研究所需的建模和推理特性。最初的 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 检查点已经表现出了不平凡的 OR 建模能力，但其行为在 Pass@1、Pass@16 和 5-shot 中有所不同。这种变化提供了一个有用的起点，用于分析哪些功能已经存在、哪些功能仅由上下文激活以及哪些故障需要参数级调整。As a lighter-weight member of the DeepSeek-V4 family (DeepSeek-AI et al., 2026), DeepSeek-V4Flash enables rapid iteration over data construction, continued pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, checkpoint selection, and benchmark evaluation, while retaining the modeling and reasoning characteristics needed for a realistic vertical-domain study. The original DeepSeek-V4-Flash checkpoint already exhibits non-trivial OR modeling ability, but its behavior varies across Pass@1, Pass@16, and 5-shot. This variation provides a useful starting point for analyzing which capabilities are already present, which capabilities are only activated by context, and which failures require parameter-level adaptation.

本节介绍了一个面向 OR 的训练后验证框架，该框架将原始检查点诊断、CPT-SFT 设计和分阶段评估连接起来。我们首先分析 OR 基准上的原始检查点并总结代表性的失败案例，重点关注协议合规性、面向求解器的实现、数学公式和结构等效性。然后，我们将这些观察结果与训练设计联系起来：CPT 用于拓宽 OR 领域知识和建模先验，而 SFT 用于将检查点与经过验证的 Gurobi 代码、特定于任务的输出合约和规范的公式样式保持一致。最后，我们通过分阶段实验验证工作流程，包括 CPT 训练稳定性、SFT 数据缩放和清理以及匹配的 CPT 到 SFT 传输。This section presents an OR-oriented post-training validation framework that connects originalcheckpoint diagnosis, CPT–SFT design, and stage-wise evaluation. We first analyze the original checkpoint on OR benchmarks and summarize representative failure cases, focusing on protocol compliance, solver-facing implementation, mathematical formulation, and structural equivalence. We then connect these observations to the training design: CPT is used to broaden OR-domain knowledge and modeling priors, while SFT is used to align the checkpoint with verified Gurobi code, task-specific output contracts, and canonical formulation styles. Finally, we validate the workflow through stage-wise experiments, including CPT training stability, SFT data scaling and cleaning, and matched CPT-to-SFT transfer.

3.1 原检查点能力边界分析3.1 Capability Boundary Analysis of the Original Checkpoint

在介绍训练后配方之前，我们在整个工作中使用的同一 OR 评估套件上诊断原始 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 检查点。基本检查点并不是一张白纸：它已经编写了许多有效的 Gurobi 程序并解决了大部分自然语言优化问题。实际问题更为具体：OR 建模管道的哪些部分已经隐藏在模型中，哪些部分可以通过提示或采样来激活，哪些部分需要通过 CPT 或 SFT 进行参数更新。Before introducing the post-training recipe, we diagnose the original DeepSeek-V4-Flash checkpoint on the same OR evaluation suite used throughout this work. The base checkpoint is not a blank slate: it already writes many valid Gurobi programs and solves a substantial fraction of natural-language optimization problems. The practical question is more specific: which parts of the OR modeling pipeline are already latent in the model, which parts can be activated by prompting or sampling, and which parts require parameter updates through CPT or SFT.

3.1.1 基准级信号3.1.1 Benchmark-Level Signal

表 4 总结了 Pass@1、Pass@16 和 5-shot 下的原始检查点。对于 NL4OPT、OptiBench 和 B4O-Feasible，当生成的求解器程序返回参考目标值时，样本被视为正确。对于 B4O-ORGEval，正确性需要与参考优化模型的结构等效，通过 ORGEval WL 奖励来衡量。这种区别对于我们的诊断至关重要：可执行代码甚至合理的目标值都不能保证正确的变量族、约束族或约束变量图。Table 4 summarizes the original checkpoint under Pass@1, Pass@16, and 5-shot. For NL4OPT, OptiBench, and B4O-Feasible, a sample is counted as correct when the generated solver program returns the reference objective value. For B4O-ORGEval, correctness requires structural equivalence to the reference optimization model, measured by the ORGEval WL reward. This distinction is central to our diagnosis: executable code and even plausible objective values do not guarantee the correct variable family, constraint family, or constraint–variable graph.

结果揭示了三个诊断信号。首先，B4O暴露出明显的结构性瓶颈。在Pass@1下，B4O-Feasible达到60.47%，而B4O ORGEval仅达到34.26%。同时，B4O ORGEval 的代码/构建通过率为 79.19%，这表明许多生成的程序是可执行的，但没有保留预期的数学结构。这一差距表明，求解器的可执行性和结构等效性在原始检查点中仅弱对齐。The results reveal three diagnostic signals. First, B4O exposes a clear structural bottleneck. Under Pass@1, B4O-Feasible reaches 60.47%, while B4O ORGEval reaches only 34.26%. Meanwhile, the code/build pass rate for B4O ORGEval is 79.19%, indicating that many generated programs are executable but do not preserve the intended mathematical structure. This gap shows that solver executability and structural equivalence are only weakly aligned in the original checkpoint.

其次，与 Pass@1 相比，Pass@16 改进了 NL4OPT、OptiBench 和 B4O-Feasible，这表明额外的采样可以恢复更有效的求解器值解决方案。然而，在 B4O ORGEval 上，Pass@16 仍然低于 5 次提示，这表明单独采样并不能可靠地选择规范优化公式。Second, Pass@16 improves NL4OPT, OptiBench, and B4O-Feasible compared with Pass@1, showing that additional sampling can recover more valid solver-value solutions. However, Pass@16 still remains below 5-shot prompting on B4O ORGEval, suggesting that sampling alone does not reliably select canonical optimization formulations.

第三，B4O-ORGEval 的 5 次性能从 34.26% 增加到 71.57%，这表明上下文示例可以激活检查点中已经部分存在的面向求解器的协议、Gurobi 实现模式和公式模板。这些观察结果激发了以下 CPT-SFT 设计：CPT 加强了 OR 域建模先验和结构公式知识，而 SFT 将提示激活行为整合为稳定的一次性生成。Third, the 5-shot performance of B4O-ORGEval increases from 34.26% to 71.57%, which demonstrates that the contextual examples can activate solver-facing protocols, Gurobi implementation patterns, and formulation templates that are already partially present in the checkpoint. These observations motivate the following CPT–SFT design: CPT strengthens OR-domain modeling priors and structural formulation knowledge, while SFT consolidates prompt-activated behaviors into stable one-pass generation.

3.1.2 跨设置的误差谱3.1.2 Error Spectrum Across Settings

为了使诊断具有可操作性，我们对三个推理设置和四个基准单元的原始检查点进行了全面的错误分析，总共覆盖了 1456 个失败案例。表 5 和图 12 将这些故障分为广泛的错误系列，以显示分布如何从 Pass@1 更改为 Pass@16 和 5-shot。总体而言，结构等价错误构成了最大的家族：WL 图不匹配、变量计数不匹配和约束计数不匹配共同导致了 B4O-ORGEval 失败的很大一部分。在 Pass@1 下，最明显的失败面是协议、API 和模式合规性，包括纯代码任务中的降价栅栏、模型提取失败和格式错误的 Python。其余的高频错误反映了缺少 OR 建模先验：建模为连续变量的离散实体、不完整的约束系统、目标尺度错误、比率或效率建模错误以及与求解器不兼容的非线性形式。To make the diagnosis actionable, we conducted a comprehensive error analysis on the original checkpoint across the three inference settings and four benchmark cells, covering 1456 failed cases in total. Table 5 and Figure 12 group these failures into broad error families to show how the distribution changes from Pass@1 to Pass@16 and 5-shot. Overall, structural equivalence errors form the largest family: WL graph mismatch, variable-count mismatch, and constraint-count mismatch together account for a large fraction of B4O-ORGEval failures. Under Pass@1, the most visible failure surface is protocol, API, and schema compliance, including markdown fences in code-only tasks, model extraction failures, and malformed Python. The remaining high-frequency errors reflect missing OR modeling priors: discrete entities modeled as continuous variables, incomplete constraint systems, objective-scale mistakes, ratio or efficiency modeling errors, and nonlinear forms that are not compatible with the solver.

3.1.3 推理设置转变3.1.3 Inference-Setting Shift

推理设置之间的分布变化将及时的行为失败与更深层次的建模失败分开。在 Pass@1 下，检查点产生 661 次失败，其中协议/API/模式错误构成最大的家族：199 例，即 30.1%。这是原始检查点面向评估者的原始故障面。转向 5-shot 将故障减少到 425 例，并将协议/API/模式错误减少到 59 例，这表明许多纯代码合约、Gurobi 习语和模式处理习惯已经是潜在的，可以通过演示激活。迁移到 Pass@16 进一步将故障总数减少到 370 例，并提高了基于价值的基准，但结构性 ORGEval 错误成为最大的剩余家族，有 127 例或 34.3%。因此，额外的采样可以检索更多数值正确的解决方案，但它不能可靠地选择规范变量约束图。The distribution shift across inference settings separates promptable behavioral failures from deeper modeling failures. Under Pass@1, the checkpoint produces 661 failures, with protocol/API/schema errors forming the largest family: 199 cases, or 30.1%. This is the raw evaluator-facing failure surface of the original checkpoint. Moving to 5-shot reduces failures to 425 and cuts protocol/API/schema errors to 59 cases, showing that many code-only contracts, Gurobi idioms, and schema-handling habits are already latent and can be activated by demonstrations. Moving to Pass@16 further reduces total failures to 370 and improves value-based benchmarks, but structural ORGEval errors become the largest remaining family, 127 cases or 34.3%. Thus, additional sampling can retrieve more numerically correct solutions, but it does not reliably choose the canonical variable–constraint graph.

相同的观察结果可以解读为面向培训的失败分类法。下面，故障类型按其在 1,456 个扫描的失败案例中的总体份额进行排序。该段落级视图将主要阻碍评估界面的故障与暴露缺失的 OR 建模先验的故障区分开来，同时将代表性案例和训练影响与具体样本联系起来。The same observations can be read as a training-oriented failure taxonomy. Below, the failure types are ordered by their overall share among the 1,456 scanned failed cases. This paragraph-level view separates failures that mainly block the evaluation interface from failures that expose missing OR modeling priors, while keeping the representative cases and training implications tied to concrete samples.

输出协议、语法、API 和模式失败 (23.9%)。Output protocol, syntax, API, and schema failures (23.9%).这是最致命的评价界面问题。在This is the most fatal evaluationinterface problem. InBENCH4OPT_4，一个资本预算问题，Pass@1 响应在纯代码目标中添加了降价代码栅栏，导致模型构建之前出现语法故障。在, a capital-budgeting problem, the Pass@1 response adds a markdown code fence in a code-only target, causing a syntax failure before model construction. InBENCH4OPT_176，护士调度问题，字符串移位标签用于索引需求列表，触发架构/索引运行时错误。这些案例表明，检查点可能在内部具有可用的 OR 推理，但无法通过所需的程序接口公开它。无论数学思想是否正确，不可执行的代码都被评分为零，因此该家族可以严重掩盖检查点的真实 OR 能力。它主要激励 SFT：纯代码合约、经过验证的 Gurobi API 和模式安全模板必须成为稳定的 Pass@1 行为。, a nurse-scheduling problem, a string shift label is used to index a list of requirements, triggering a schema/indexing runtime error. These cases show that the checkpoint may have usable OR reasoning internally but fail to expose it through the required program interface. Non-executable code is scored as zero regardless of whether the mathematical idea is correct, so this family can severely mask the checkpoint’s real OR capability. It primarily motivates SFT: code-only contracts, verified Gurobi APIs, and schema-safe templates must become stable Pass@1 behavior.

离散变量语义（12.5%）。Discrete variable semantics (12.5%).NL4OPT 15 是一个人员配置问题，需要年长工人和年轻工人的数量。该模型将工人计数编码为NL4OPT 15 is a staffing problem that asks for numbers of senior and young workers. The model encodes the worker counts asGRB.CONTINUOUS并返回 LP 松弛值 28125.0，而不是整数最优值 28250.0。同样的松弛在 5-shot 和 Pass@16 下仍然存在，表明提示和采样不能可靠地恢复丢失的先验完整性。这是一个核心 OR 语义问题：模型理解许多约束，但无法将可数业务实体映射到整数或二进制变量。 CPT 应加强可数实体先验，而 SFT 应为工人、行程、批次、车辆、分配和选择提供明确的整数/二进制 Gurobi 演示。and returns the LP relaxation value 28125.0 instead of the integer optimum 28250.0. The same relaxation persists under 5-shot and Pass@16, showing that prompting and sampling do not reliably recover the missing integrality prior. This is a core OR semantics problem: the model understands many constraints, but fails to map countable business entities to integer or binary variables. CPT should strengthen countable-entity priors, while SFT should provide explicit integer/binary Gurobi demonstrations for workers, trips, batches, vehicles, assignments, and selections.

损失/产量驱动的保护限制（10.8%）。Loss/yield-driven conservation constraints (10.8%). BENCH4OPT_2是一个涉及容量、成本、蒸发率和节点需求的配水网络流量问题。 Pass@1 模型求解了 LP，但将蒸发混合到成本和守恒中，结果返回 265.58，而不是 531.15。 5-shot和Pass@16恢复了正确的值，表明模板可以提示但不稳定。这代表了复杂的约束语义，而不仅仅是代码生成。损失、产量、收缩和蒸发决定了守恒方程的变化is a water-distribution network-flow problem with capacity, cost, evaporation rate, and node demand. The Pass@1 model solves an LP but mixes evaporation into both cost and conservation in a way that returns 265.58 instead of 531.15. 5-shot and Pass@16 recover the correct value, showing that the template is promptable but not stable. This represents complex constraint semantics rather than mere code generation. Loss, yield, shrinkage, and evaporation determine where conservation equations change

3.1 原检查点能力边界分析3.1 Capability Boundary Analysis of the Original Checkpoint

以及成本或交付数量是否发生变化。 CPT 应该教授这些领域模式，而 SFT 应该将可提示的模板提炼成稳定的数据分隔代码。and whether costs or delivered quantities change. CPT should teach these domain patterns, while SFT should distill promptable templates into stable data-separated code.

比率、单位和总量耦合（7.4%）。Ratio, unit, and total-quantity coupling (7.4%).OptiBench 6 是一个物流投资问题，其中路线优化投资可降低每辆卡车的运输成本，且总投资不得超过总节省额。 Pass@1和Pass@16认识到双线性关系，但错误地处理了投资单位和总储蓄； 5-shot 消除了卡车数量乘数的节省约束。这种失败破坏了优化模型的经济意义，同时仍然产生合理的代数。比率、投资回报率、平均成本、百分比和效率表达式要求模型区分单位数量、总计、分母和缩放单位。 CPT 应该拓宽这种语义先验，而 SFT 可以教授经过验证的公式。OptiBench 6 is a logistics investment problem where route-optimization investment reduces per-truck delivery cost and total investment must not exceed total savings. Pass@1 and Pass@16 recognize the bilinear relation but mishandle investment units and total savings; 5-shot removes the truck-count multiplier from the savings constraint. This failure corrupts the economic meaning of an optimization model while still producing plausible algebra. Ratio, ROI, average-cost, percentage, and efficiency expressions require the model to distinguish per-unit quantities, totals, denominators, and scaling units. CPT should broaden this semantic prior, while SFT can teach verified formulations.

与求解器兼容的非线性重构 (6.4%)。Solver-compatible nonlinear reformulation (6.4%).OptiBench 13 要求抛物线边界上最近的可行救援点。 Pass@1 解决方案将四阶距离目标直接写入 Gurobi 并触发“目标必须是线性或二次”错误。 5-shot 和 Pass@16 避免了构建错误，但仍然返回错误的距离，这意味着缺少的不仅仅是 API 语法，还有非线性重构知识。这会阻止一整类数学上有效的 OR 任务。模型必须知道何时引入辅助变量、何时使用二次/非凸/一般约束以及何时需要分析简化。 CPT是主要杠杆； SFT 强化了经过验证的 Gurobi 模式。OptiBench 13 asks for the closest feasible rescue point on a parabolic boundary. The Pass@1 solution writes a fourth-order distance objective directly into Gurobi and triggers an “objective must be linear or quadratic” error. 5-shot and Pass@16 avoid the build error but still return the wrong distance, which means the missing piece is not just API syntax but nonlinear reformulation knowledge. This blocks a whole class of mathematically valid OR tasks. The model must know when to introduce auxiliary variables, when to use quadratic/nonconvex/general constraints, and when analytic simplification is required. CPT is the main lever; SFT hardens the verified Gurobi patterns.

这种分类法解释了为什么设置明智的分布会激励两个训练阶段。 Pass@1 发行版以协议/API/模式和结构故障为主，因此需要 SFT 来使纯代码生成、Gurobi API 使用、目标提取和 JSON 式数据访问稳定，而无需上下文演示。 5 次分布显示了哪些失败是可以提炼出来的：降价围栏、几个 API 错误以及一些常见的公式模板一旦出现示例就会大幅改善。这直接推动了经过验证的 SFT 和少量蒸馏。然而，残留的5次射击错误集中在整数语义、复杂约束、比率/非线性建模和ORGEval结构上，而这些不仅仅是输出习惯。他们需要 CPT 来拓宽模型的 OR 公式先验，然后 SFT 才能可靠地引出它们。This taxonomy explains why the setting-wise distribution motivates both training stages. The Pass@1 distribution is dominated by protocol/API/schema and structural failures, so SFT is needed to make code-only generation, Gurobi API usage, objective extraction, and JSON-style data access stable without in-context demonstrations. The 5-shot distribution shows which failures are distillable: markdown fences, several API mistakes, and some common formulation templates improve sharply once examples are present. This directly motivates verified SFT and few-shot distillation. However, the residual 5-shot errors concentrate in integer semantics, complex constraints, ratio/nonlinear modeling, and ORGEval structure, which are not just output habits. They require CPT to broaden the model’s OR formulation priors before SFT can reliably elicit them.

Pass@16 发行版增加了一个不同的教训。由于总失败次数从 425 次下降到 370 次，并且基于值的准确度提高，因此原始采样分布已经包含许多可恢复的正确程序；这支持验证者引导的 SFT 和来自 Pass@ 的拒绝采样蒸馏The Pass@16 distribution adds a different lesson. Because total failures fall from 425 to 370 and valuebased accuracies improve, the original sampling distribution already contains many recoverable correct programs; this supports verifier-guided SFT and rejection-sampled distillation from Pass@𝑁输出。但 Pass@16 比 5 次射击留下更多的结构性 ORGEval 失败，无论是在绝对数量（127 比 108 分组结构错误）还是在比例上（34.3% 比 25.4%）。因此，采样改进了对解决方案的搜索，而无需强制执行规范的 LP 图。这是 CPT 加 SFT 的关键桥梁：CPT 将模型转向 OR 原生结构先验，而 SFT 将这些先验转化为面向基准的、可执行的和对齐的输出。outputs. But Pass@16 leaves more structural ORGEval failures than 5-shot, both in absolute count (127 vs. 108 grouped structural errors) and in share (34.3% vs. 25.4%). Sampling therefore improves search over solutions without enforcing the canonical LP graph. This is the key bridge to CPT plus SFT: CPT shifts the model toward OR-native structural priors, while SFT turns those priors into benchmark-facing, executable, and aligned outputs.

3.1.4 两阶段训练适应：为什么同时采用 CPT 和 SFT3.1.4 Two-Stage Training Adaptation: Why Both CPT and SFT

在大型语言模型流程中，持续预训练充当知识注入阶段，通过对未标记语料库的自监督学习使模型适应某个领域（Gururangan 等，2020；Ke 等，2025；Siriwardhana 等，2024）——而指令调整则充当行为塑造阶段（Ouyang 等，2022；Wei 等， 2022；Sanh 等人，2021）通过指令-响应对的监督学习使模型与人类意图保持一致。核心区别在于，持续的预训练决定了模型知道什么，而指令调整控制了模型的响应方式。它们的关系是基础和激活的关系：持续的预训练首先构建一个领域信息知识库，随后的指令调整将其引出为连贯的、遵循指令的行为（Yang et al., 2025a；Jindal et al., 2024；Jiang et al., 2025；Zhang et al., 2025a）。只有这种级联协同才能产生既精通领域又可靠可用的模型。In large language model pipelines, continual pre-training serves as a knowledge-infusion stage— adapting the model to a domain through self-supervised learning on unlabeled corpora (Gururangan et al., 2020; Ke et al., 2025; Siriwardhana et al., 2024)—while instruction tuning acts as a behaviorshaping stage (Ouyang et al., 2022; Wei et al., 2022; Sanh et al., 2021) that aligns the model with human intent via supervised learning on instruction-response pairs. The core distinction is that continual pre-training determines what the model knows, and instruction tuning controls how it responds. Their relationship is one of foundation and activation: continual pre-training first builds a domain-informed knowledge base, and subsequent instruction tuning elicits it as coherent, instruction-following behavior (Yang et al., 2025a; Jindal et al., 2024; Jiang et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2025a). Only this cascaded synergy yields models that are both domain-proficient and reliably usable.

明智的设置诊断提供了明确的分工。 CPT 的目标是知识获取：OR 术语、标准公式模式、解算器感知非线性推理、分数和效率建模、数据结构基础和结构建模先验。即使添加示例或多个样本，这些故障模式仍然存在：整数域语义、非线性重构、比率建模、缺失约束族和规范 LP 图结构。The setting-wise diagnosis gives a clean division of labor. CPT targets knowledge acquisition: OR terminology, standard formulation patterns, solver-aware nonlinear reasoning, fractional and efficiency modeling, data-structure grounding, and structural modeling priors. These are the failure modes that persist even when examples or multiple samples are added: integer-domain semantics, nonlinear reformulation, ratio modeling, missing constraint families, and canonical LP graph structure.

27

3.2 面向OR的持续预训练3.2 OR-Oriented Continued Pre-Training

SFT 的目标是面向求解器的行为对齐。它应该教导模型遵守仅代码和 LP 编写合同，避免降价围栏，使用经过验证的 Gurobi API，将 JSON/list/dict 数据安全地映射到索引集中，使用正确的单位提取客观值，并重现面向基准的规范公式。 5 次改进表明，其中许多习惯已经可以及时养成； SFT 将这种即时依赖转化为稳定的 Pass@1 行为。 Pass@16的改进表明，正确的解往往存在于抽样分布中；验证者引导的蒸馏可以将这些检索到的样本转换为参数化的能力。其余的结构和建模失败解释了为什么这个 SFT 阶段应该构建在面向 OR 的 CPT 之上而不是取代它。SFT targets solver-oriented behavioral alignment. It should teach the model to obey code-only and LP-writing contracts, avoid markdown fences, use verified Gurobi APIs, map JSON/list/dict data safely into index sets, extract objective values with the right units, and reproduce benchmark-facing canonical formulations. The 5-shot improvements show that many of these habits are already promptable; SFT converts that prompt dependence into stable Pass@1 behavior. The Pass@16 improvements show that correct solutions often exist in the sampling distribution; verifier-guided distillation can convert those retrieved samples into parameterized competence. The remaining structural and modeling failures explain why this SFT stage should be built on top of OR-oriented CPT rather than replacing it.

3.2 面向OR的持续预训练3.2 OR-Oriented Continued Pre-Training

原始检查点分析表明，DeepSeek-V4-Flash 已经包含有用的 OR 建模核心，特别是在标准 LP 和 MILP 模板上，但其剩余错误集中在变量域语义、求解器兼容的非线性重构、基于比率和效率的建模、复杂约束构造和结构等价上。这些故障模式表明下一阶段不仅应该教导模型遵循基准协议，还应该加强其优先于 OR 域概念、数学编程结构和求解器感知建模模式的能力。因此，我们使用 CPT 作为面向 OR 的后训练工作流程中的知识获取阶段，遵循领域自适应预训练范例，使语言模型适应专门的分布（Gururangan 等人，2020；Chen 等人，2026a）。The original-checkpoint analysis shows that DeepSeek-V4-Flash already contains a useful OR modeling core, especially on standard LP and MILP templates, but its remaining errors concentrate on variable-domain semantics, solver-compatible nonlinear reformulation, ratio- and efficiencybased modeling, complex constraint construction, and structural equivalence. These failure modes indicate that the next stage should not only teach the model to follow benchmark protocols, but also strengthen its prior over OR-domain concepts, mathematical-programming structures, and solver-aware modeling patterns. We therefore use CPT as the knowledge-acquisition stage in the OR-oriented post-training workflow, following the domain-adaptive pre-training paradigm for adapting language models to specialized distributions (Gururangan et al., 2020; Chen et al., 2026a).

在此阶段，模型将在围绕求解器验证的 OR 文档构建的领域主导混合物上进行进一步训练。该设计以原始检查点中观察到的能力差距为指导：整数和二进制变量语义促使更广泛地接触可数实体公式；非线性和基于比率的故障激发解算器感知的重构数据； ORGEval 结构不匹配激发数据保留规范变量族、约束族和约束变量连接。遵循这一原则，我们的 CPT 工作流程以 OR-CPT 数据引擎为中心，将参数化优化生成器转换为可跟踪、面向业务且可执行的建模文档。由此产生的 CPT 阶段充当数据构建和 SFT 之间的桥梁：它在 SFT 将检查点与经过验证的 Gurobi 代码、严格的输出契约和特定于基准的公式样式对齐之前扩展了模型的 OR 域建模分布（Jiang 等人，2025）。In this stage, the model is further trained on a domain-dominant mixture built around solververified OR documents. The design is guided by the capability gaps observed in the original checkpoint: integer and binary variable semantics motivate broader exposure to countable-entity formulations; nonlinear and ratio-based failures motivate solver-aware reformulation data; and ORGEval structural mismatches motivate data that preserves canonical variable families, constraint families, and constraint–variable connections. Following this principle, our CPT workflow centers on an OR-CPT data engine that converts parameterized optimization generators into traceable, business-oriented, and executable modeling documents. The resulting CPT stage serves as a bridge between data construction and SFT: it expands the model’s OR-domain modeling distribution before SFT aligns the checkpoint with verified Gurobi code, strict output contracts, and benchmark-specific formulation styles (Jiang et al., 2025).

3.2.1 OR-CPT 数据构建和领域主导混合3.2.1 OR-CPT Data Construction and Domain-Dominant Mixture

我们的 CPT 数据构建的核心是 OR-CPT 数据引擎，这是一个经过求解器验证的双向综合工作流程，可将结构化优化实例转换为自然语言建模文档，如图 13 所示。该工作流程针对原始检查点分析中观察到的能力差距，包括可变域模糊性、弱参数基础、不完整的约束构造、非线性或基于比率的建模失败以及 ORGEval 式评估下的结构不匹配。每个接受的 CPT 文档都可以追溯到生成的优化实例、独立验证的求解器参考和经过检查的正向建模结果。The core of our CPT data construction is the OR-CPT Data Engine, a solver-verified bidirectional synthesis workflow that converts structured optimization instances into natural-language modeling documents, as illustrated in Figure 13. The workflow targets the capability gaps observed in the original-checkpoint analysis, including variable-domain ambiguity, weak parameter grounding, incomplete constraint construction, nonlinear or ratio-based modeling failures, and structural mismatch under ORGEval-style evaluation. Each accepted CPT document is traceable to a generated optimization instance, an independently verified solver reference, and a checked forward modeling result.

工作流程从参数化优化实例生成器开始。每个生成器指定优化系列的决策变量、目标、约束、数值参数和特定于任务的结构。当前的发电机注册涵盖分配、调度、设施位置、网络流程、生产规划、批量大小、路线、覆盖、运输、投资组合优化和收入管理。对于每个实例，引擎都会记录结构化元数据，包括任务族、生成器身份、概念标签、难度标签、生成参数和规范数学签名。此元数据用于语料库采样、审核和概念级分析，但不会作为面向模型的 CPT 文本公开。我们还保持数据生成资产和 ORGEval 评估资产之间的分离：在渲染 CPT 文档时，不会重用 ORGEval 参考公式、规范 LP 图或评估器端模板。预期的重叠是在广泛的 OR 问题族和建模概念的层面上，而不是在共享的规范模板或实例来源的层面上。The workflow starts from parameterized optimization instance generators. Each generator specifies the decision variables, objective, constraints, numerical parameters, and task-specific structure of an optimization family. The current generator registry covers assignment, scheduling, facility location, network flow, production planning, lot sizing, routing, covering, transportation, portfolio optimization, and revenue management. For each instance, the engine records structured metadata, including task family, generator identity, concept tags, difficulty label, generation parameters, and canonical mathematical signature. This metadata is used for corpus sampling, auditing, and conceptlevel analysis, but is not exposed as model-facing CPT text. We also maintain separation between the data-generation assets and the ORGEval evaluation assets: no ORGEval reference formulation, canonical LP graph, or evaluator-side template is reused when rendering CPT documents. The intended overlap is at the level of broad OR problem families and modeling concepts, rather than at the level of shared canonical templates or instance provenance.

每个生成的实例都使用 Gurobi 独立执行，以获得参考求解器状态、目标值、变量分配和约束诊断。然后，引擎应用实例级质量过滤器来删除不稳定或低价值的样本，包括不可行的实例，Each generated instance is independently executed with Gurobi to obtain a reference solver status, objective value, variable assignment, and constraint diagnostics. The engine then applies instance-level quality filters to remove unstable or low-value samples, including infeasible instances,

图 13：Figure 13:OR-CPT 数据构建流程。参数化优化生成器生成结构化实例，这些实例通过 Gurobi 进行验证，呈现为面向业务的问题陈述，重构为可执行公式，并在导出前通过合同检查、求解器执行和目标价值协议进行过滤。Pipeline of OR-CPT data construction. Parameterized optimization generators produce structured instances, which are verified with Gurobi, rendered into business-oriented problem statements, reconstructed into executable formulations, and filtered by contract checks, solver execution, and objective-value agreement before export.

退化全零解、重复的参数配置、重复的解签名和弱优化权衡。这种基于求解器的过滤步骤为合成语料库提供了数学锚点，并提高了后续自然语言建模文档的质量。degenerate all-zero solutions, duplicated parameter configurations, duplicated solution signatures, and weak optimization trade-offs. This solver-grounded filtering step provides the mathematical anchor for the synthetic corpus and improves the quality of the subsequent natural-language modeling documents.

经过求解器验证后，结构化实例将转换为面向业务的自然语言问题。场景目录控制行业背景、组织类型、规划范围、实体命名风格、单位和叙述视角。确定性泄漏检查可防止问题陈述暴露最佳目标值、求解器状态、实现代码或其他与答案相关的工件。此步骤将数学实例转变为现实的建模提示，同时保留底层优化结构。After solver validation, the structured instance is converted into a business-oriented naturallanguage problem. A scenario catalog controls the industry background, organization type, planning horizon, entity naming style, units, and narrative perspective. Deterministic leakage checks prevent the problem statement from exposing the optimal objective value, solver status, implementation code, or other answer-related artifacts. This step turns a mathematical instance into a realistic modeling prompt while preserving the underlying optimization structure.

工作流程的第二个方向根据生成的自然语言问题执行正向建模。仅给定问题陈述，模型就会生成决策变量、目标函数、约束、建模解释和可执行的 Gurobi 代码。生成的答案通过静态规则和任务族契约进行检查。例如，设施位置样本应将设施开放变量与分配变量联系起来，网络流量样本应包含流量保护约束，调度样本应保留分配、覆盖范围和容量结构。然后，独立执行重建的 Gurobi 程序，并与原始结构化实例的求解器参考进行比较。仅当程序达到有效的求解器状态并且其目标值与验证容差内的参考解相匹配时，才接受样本。可通过结构化求解器反馈修复可恢复的故障，并在导出之前呈现已接受的文档并进行重复数据删除。The second direction of the workflow performs forward modeling from the generated naturallanguage problem. Given only the problem statement, a model produces decision variables, objective functions, constraints, modeling explanations, and executable Gurobi code. The generated answer is checked by static rules and task-family contracts. For example, facility-location samples are expected to connect facility-opening variables with assignment variables, network-flow samples should contain flow-conservation constraints, and scheduling samples should preserve assignment, coverage, and capacity structures. The reconstructed Gurobi program is then executed independently and compared with the solver reference from the original structured instance. A sample is accepted only when the program reaches a valid solver status and its objective value matches the reference solution within the verification tolerance. Recoverable failures are repaired with structured solver feedback, and accepted documents are rendered and deduplicated before export.

这种双向设计为合成 CPT 语料库提供了三个有用的属性。它是可扩展的，因为新数据可以从跨许多任务系列的参数化 OR 生成器生成。它是可验证的，因为每个接受的文档都与求解器执行和参考目标协议相关。它在结构上具有丰富的信息，因为工作流程保留了自然语言需求、变量族、约束族、求解器代码和目标值之间的联系。这些属性使 OR-CPT 数据引擎成为我们 CPT 阶段中基于求解器的建模信号的主要来源。This bidirectional design gives the synthetic CPT corpus three useful properties. It is scalable because new data can be produced from parameterized OR generators across many task families. It is verifiable because each accepted document is tied to solver execution and reference-objective agreement. It is structurally informative because the workflow preserves the connection among natural-language requirements, variable families, constraint families, solver code, and objective values. These properties make the OR-CPT Data Engine the main source of solver-grounded modeling signal in our CPT stage.

29

3.2 面向OR的持续预训练3.2 OR-Oriented Continued Pre-Training

完整的 CPT 语料库将这些经过求解器验证的合成文档与收集的 ORdomain 资源相结合。收集的部分涵盖优化建模、数学推理和面向求解器的问题解决，包括 OptMATH (Lu et al., 2025)、ReSocratic 和 OptiBench (Yang et al., 2025c)、Text2Opt-Bench (Gao et al., 2026)、OR-Instruct (Huang et al., 2025)、NLP4LP (Ahmaditeshnizi et al., 2025)、NLP4LP (Ahmaditeshnizi et al., 2025) 2024）、ORQA 衍生数据（Mostajabdaveh 等人，2025；Ge，2025）和 MAMO（Huang 等人，2024）。我们还包括 OR 研究论文、官方 Gurobi 建模示例和教科书式材料，以扩大对形式建模约定、求解器使用和领域术语的覆盖范围（Kong 等人，2026 年；Gurobi Optimization, LLC，2026 年；Lownes，2024 年）。所有收集的资源都会转换为统一的 CPT 文档格式，并通过源感知清理、格式化和重复数据删除进行处理。现阶段OR数据池包含大约10万个样本，作为CPT采样的语料库。The complete CPT corpus combines these solver-verified synthetic documents with collected ORdomain resources. The collected portion covers optimization modeling, mathematical reasoning, and solver-oriented problem solving, including OptMATH (Lu et al., 2025), ReSocratic and OptiBench (Yang et al., 2025c), Text2Opt-Bench (Gao et al., 2026), OR-Instruct (Huang et al., 2025), NLP4LP (Ahmaditeshnizi et al., 2024), ORQA-derived data (Mostajabdaveh et al., 2025; Ge, 2025), and MAMO (Huang et al., 2024). We also include OR research papers, official Gurobi modeling examples, and textbook-style materials to increase coverage of formal modeling conventions, solver usage, and domain terminology (Kong et al., 2026; Gurobi Optimization, LLC, 2026; Lownes, 2024). All collected resources are converted into a unified CPT document format and processed through source-aware cleaning, formatting, and deduplication. At the current stage, the OR data pool contains approximately 100K samples, which serve as the corpus inventory for CPT sampling.

我们采用域主导混合策略，其中 OR 文档提供主要适应信号。数学、代码和通用英语语料库作为支持组件包含在内。数学组件取自选定的 AutoMathText-V2 子集（Li 等人，2025），遵循 AutoMathText 数据选择方法（Zhang 等人，2025c），并支持符号公式、推导和多步推理。该代码组件取自 NVIDIA Nemotron 预训练数据版本，包括与 Nemotron Nano 系列相关的科学编码数据和与 Nemotron 3 Super 相关的代码概念数据（NVIDIA 等人，2025、2026），并支持程序推理、面向求解器的实现和稳健的代码生成。通用英语组件使用 Ultra-FineWeb-L3，源自 UltraData 分层数据管理框架下的 Ultra-FineWeb（Wang 等人，2025a，2026），以保持广泛的语言理解和生成质量。在这种混合中，OR 数据增强了公式语义和结构建模先验，数学数据支持重新公式化和符号推理，代码数据支持可执行求解器实现，通用英语数据稳定了广泛的语言能力。准确的采样分配被视为配方级参数，并与训练配置一起报告。We adopt a domain-dominant mixture strategy in which OR documents provide the main adaptation signal. Mathematical, code, and general English corpora are included as supporting components. The mathematical component is drawn from selected AutoMathText-V2 subsets (Li et al., 2025), following the AutoMathText data-selection methodology (Zhang et al., 2025c), and supports symbolic formulation, derivation, and multi-step reasoning. The code component is drawn from NVIDIA Nemotron pretraining data releases, including scientific-coding data associated with the Nemotron Nano series and code-concept data associated with Nemotron 3 Super (NVIDIA et al., 2025, 2026), and supports procedural reasoning, solver-oriented implementation, and robust code generation. The general English component uses Ultra-FineWeb-L3, derived from Ultra-FineWeb under the UltraData tiered data management framework (Wang et al., 2025a, 2026), to maintain broad language comprehension and generation quality. In this mixture, OR data strengthens formulation semantics and structural modeling priors, mathematical data supports reformulation and symbolic reasoning, code data supports executable solver implementation, and general English data stabilizes broad language capability. The exact sampling allocation is treated as a recipe-level parameter and reported with the training configuration.

3.2.2 Ascend生态系统中的训练执行3.2.2 Training Execution in the Ascend Ecosystem

我们在 Ascend 软件生态系统中执行面向 OR 的 CPT，使用 MindSpeed 和 TorchTitan-NPU 作为两条实施路线。两条路线都遵循相同的自回归训练目标、面向 OR 的数据接口、监控协议和检查点评估工作流程。这种设计使 CPT 配方在不同的 Ascend 训练抽象中保持一致，以便数据构建、优化行为和下游验证保持可比性。We execute OR-oriented CPT within the Ascend software ecosystem, using MindSpeed and TorchTitan-NPU as two implementation routes. Both routes follow the same autoregressive training objective, OR-oriented data interface, monitoring protocol, and checkpoint-evaluation workflow. This design keeps the CPT recipe consistent across different Ascend training abstractions, so that data construction, optimization behavior, and downstream validation remain comparable.

执行设置是围绕 DeepSeek-V4-Flash CPT 的主要要求进行配置的。长上下文训练通过上下文并行性得到支持，这对于包含业务描述、数学公式、求解器跟踪和多步骤建模解释的 OR 文档非常重要。通过专家并行、令牌调度和分组专家计算支持稀疏 MoE 执行。训练堆栈还支持特定于模型的注意力和 MoE 算子路径、MTP 训练、激活重新计算和内存感知优化器执行。这些能力为全参数CPT提供了执行基础，同时保持模型架构和语言建模目标不变。The execution setup is configured around the main requirements of DeepSeek-V4-Flash CPT. Longcontext training is supported through context parallelism, which is important for OR documents that contain business descriptions, mathematical formulations, solver traces, and multi-step modeling explanations. Sparse MoE execution is supported through expert parallelism, token dispatch, and grouped expert computation. The training stack also supports model-specific attention and MoE operator paths, MTP training, activation recomputation, and memory-aware optimizer execution. These capabilities provide the execution basis for full-parameter CPT while keeping the model architecture and language-modeling objective unchanged.

第 2 节介绍了更广泛的 Ascend 系统设计，包括集群组织、集体通信、操作员优化、运行时监控和故障恢复。这里，训练执行作为面向 OR 的 CPT 配方的基础。在 CPT 期间，我们监控损失动态、学习率进展、梯度范数、数值稳定性、吞吐量、记忆行为和迭代稳定性。这些信号用于识别中间评估和后续 SFT 的稳定候选检查点。附录 B 总结了配方级设置，包括序列长度、批量大小、学习率计划、并行度、优化器配置和内存节省机制。The broader Ascend system design, including cluster organization, collective communication, operator optimization, runtime monitoring, and fault recovery, is reported in Section 2. Here, the training execution serves as the substrate for the OR-oriented CPT recipe. During CPT, we monitor loss dynamics, learning-rate progression, gradient norms, numerical stability, throughput, memory behavior, and iteration stability. These signals are used to identify stable candidate checkpoints for intermediate evaluation and subsequent SFT. Recipe-level settings, including sequence length, batch size, learning-rate schedule, parallelism degrees, optimizer configuration, and memory-saving mechanisms, are summarized in Appendix B.

3.2.3 检查点选择和 CPT 到 SFT 传输验证3.2.3 Checkpoint Selection and CPT-to-SFT Transfer Validation

CPT 检查点根据其值作为 SFT 初始化进行选择。我们用三个互补信号评估中间检查点：OR 域似然、一般能力保留和下游 OR 转移。 OR 似然信号是在与 CPT 训练混合物不相交的保留 OR 验证集上测量的。它提供了检查点是否更好的模型或术语、公式模式和面向求解器的文档的早期指示。CPT checkpoints are selected according to their value as SFT initializations. We evaluate intermediate checkpoints with three complementary signals: OR-domain likelihood, general-capability retention, and downstream OR transfer. The OR likelihood signal is measured on a held-out OR validation set that is disjoint from the CPT training mixture. It provides an early indication of whether the checkpoint better models OR terminology, formulation patterns, and solver-oriented documents.

30

3.3 运筹学的数据高效监督微调3.3 Data-Efficient Supervised Fine-Tuning for Operations Research

并行跟踪一般验证损失和代表性一般能力基准，以监控检查点是否保留了后续指令调整所需的数学、编码和语言能力。General validation loss and representative general-capability benchmarks are tracked in parallel to monitor whether the checkpoint preserves the mathematical, coding, and language abilities needed for later instruction tuning.

我们进一步评估 OR 下游基准的选定检查点。这些基准涵盖优化建模的不同方面：NL4OPT 强调自然语言到公式化的能力，OptiBench 评估更广泛的优化问题理解，B4O-Feasible 衡量面向求解器的可行性行为，B4O-ORGEval 通过更严格的公式化级别评估来测试结构建模质量。该基准集使我们能够检查 OR 域可能性的改进是否转化为建模、实现和结构等效行为方面的可衡量的收益。We further evaluate selected checkpoints on OR downstream benchmarks. These benchmarks cover different aspects of optimization modeling: NL4OPT emphasizes natural-language-to-formulation ability, OptiBench evaluates broader optimization problem understanding, B4O-Feasible measures solver-facing feasibility behavior, and B4O-ORGEval tests structural modeling quality through a stricter formulation-level evaluation. This benchmark set allows us to check whether improvements in OR-domain likelihood translate into measurable gains in modeling, implementation, and structural-equivalence behavior.

由于CPT被设计为SFT的准备阶段，因此其最重要的评估是匹配转移验证。我们比较两种受控训练路线：Since CPT is designed as a preparation stage for SFT, its most important evaluation is matched transfer validation. We compare two controlled training routes:

两条路线之间的 SFT 数据集、优化器配置、更新步骤和评估协议保持相同。因此，训练模型之间的差异反映了 CPT 作为初始化阶段的贡献。对于下游基准The SFT dataset, optimizer configuration, update steps, and evaluation protocol are kept the same between the two routes. As a result, the difference between the trained models reflects the contribution of CPT as an initialization stage. For a downstream benchmark𝑏，我们将传输增益定义为：, we define the transfer gain as:

最终检查点是根据多目标标准选择的。首选检查点应减少 OR 验证损失，在训练期间保持稳定的优化信号，将一般功能保留在预期范围内，并在主要 OR 基准上产生积极的 CPT 到 SFT 转移。这个选择过程将 CPT 阶段直接连接到后面的 SFT 和实验分析：CPT 不仅被评估为语言建模延续，而且被评估为域自适应初始化，其值由匹配训练条件下的下游 OR 建模增益决定。The final checkpoint is selected with a multi-objective criterion. A preferred checkpoint should reduce OR validation loss, maintain stable optimization signals during training, preserve general capabilities within the expected range, and produce positive CPT-to-SFT transfer on the primary OR benchmarks. This selection procedure connects the CPT stage directly to the later SFT and experimental analysis: CPT is not only evaluated as a language-modeling continuation, but as a domain-adaptive initialization whose value is determined by downstream OR modeling gains under matched training conditions.

3.3 运筹学的数据高效监督微调3.3 Data-Efficient Supervised Fine-Tuning for Operations Research

我们对 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 在运筹学建模任务上进行了监督微调。目标是将模型从遵循一般指令调整为面向求解器的数学编程，重点是 NL4OPT、OptiBench 和 Bench4Opt 等基准测试。与一般的代码生成任务不同，OR 建模评估不仅检查可执行性。它还评估自然语言语义理解、变量类型选择、目标和约束构造、Gurobi 风格的 API 使用、外部数据加载、答案格式契约以及数学公式的结构等价性。We conduct supervised fine-tuning for DeepSeek-V4-Flash on operations research modeling tasks. The goal is to adapt the model from general instruction following to solver-oriented mathematical programming, with emphasis on benchmarks such as NL4OPT, OptiBench, and Bench4Opt. Unlike general code-generation tasks, OR modeling evaluation does not only check executability. It also evaluates natural-language semantic understanding, variable-type selection, objective and constraint construction, Gurobi-style API usage, external data loading, answer-format contracts, and structural equivalence of mathematical formulations.

由于 OR 领域的大规模 SFT 数据稀缺、异构且质量不确定，因此我们采用了 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 循环自蒸馏方法，其灵感来自迭代数据构建框架，例如 ReSocratic 式合成（Yang 等人，2025c；Zhang 等人，2025b）。更广泛地说，自蒸馏已被证明可以有效地引导高质量的合成数据（Lee＆Liang，2026），特别是与优化建模任务的结构化验证相结合时（Wu 等人，2025b）。我们构建了三个训练数据尺度，即 3K、10K 和 50K。基于4.4节中报告的对比实验，我们选择10K规模的数据作为进一步清洗和增强的基础。因此，最终的 SFT 设计不仅关注扩展合成数据，还关注提高蒸馏样本的正确性、契约意识和可学习性。Since large-scale SFT data in the OR domain is scarce, heterogeneous, and uncertain in quality, we adopt a DeepSeek-V4-Flash cyclic self-distillation method inspired by iterative data-construction frameworks such as ReSocratic-style synthesis (Yang et al., 2025c; Zhang et al., 2025b). More broadly, self-distillation has been shown to be effective for bootstrapping high-quality synthetic data (Lee & Liang, 2026), especially when combined with structured validation for optimization modeling tasks (Wu et al., 2025b). We construct three training-data scales, namely 3K, 10K, and 50K. Based on the comparative experiments reported in Section 4.4, we choose the 10K-scale data as the basis for further cleaning and enhancement. The final SFT design therefore focuses not only on scaling synthetic data, but also on improving the correctness, contract awareness, and learnability of the distilled samples.

3.3.1 面向求解器的 SFT 目标3.3.1 Solver-Oriented SFT Objective

SFT阶段旨在训练模型形成稳定的优化建模习惯。模型应识别集合、参数、变量、目标和约束；正确读取结构化数据文件；使用 Gurobi 风格的 Python API 来构建和求解模型；并且，在需要时，写入 LP 文件而不求解它们。因此，目标响应不是简单的数字答案，The SFT stage is designed to train the model to form stable optimization-modeling habits. The model should identify sets, parameters, variables, objectives, and constraints; correctly read structured data files; use Gurobi-style Python APIs to build and solve models; and, when required, write LP files without solving them. The target response is therefore not a simple numerical answer,

提示、系统和格式化标记的损失被屏蔽，以便梯度更新仅应用于目标响应。这鼓励模型学习所需的推理轨迹、数学公式和求解器实现，同时避免对固定提示模板进行不必要的优化。The loss on prompt, system, and formatting tokens is masked, so that gradient updates are applied only to the target response. This encourages the model to learn the desired reasoning trajectory, mathematical formulation, and solver implementation, while avoiding unnecessary optimization on fixed prompt templates.

3.3.2 自蒸馏或建模数据飞轮3.3.2 Self-Distilled OR Modeling Data Flywheel

我们为运筹学和数学编程任务构建了一个自蒸馏数据生产管道。输入是一组种子样本，包括原始问题、参考答案、参考代码、数据文件或来自高质量建模 QA 数据的 LP 文件。这些种子样本不会直接导出为最终训练数据。相反，它们充当问题类型、建模结构和目标响应规范的参考。We construct a self-distillation data-production pipeline for operations research and mathematical programming tasks. The input is a set of seed samples, including original problems, reference answers, reference code, data files, or LP files from high-quality modeling QA data. These seed samples are not directly exported as final training data. Instead, they serve as references for problem types, modeling structures, and target response specifications.

种子样本分为三种输入模式，如表 6 所示。The seed samples are organized into three input modes, as summarized in Table 6.

为了使自蒸馏过程可控和可审计，我们引入了三个层次的中间表示。这种分层设计遵循优化数据生成中的渐进综合策略，其中中间表示有助于控制问题复杂性和结构多样性（Yang 等人，2025c；Wu 等人，2025b）。To make the self-distillation process controllable and auditable, we introduce three levels of intermediate representations. This layered design follows progressive synthesis strategies in optimization data generation, where intermediate representations help control problem complexity and structural diversity (Yang et al., 2025c; Wu et al., 2025b).

  • L1 规范红外L1 Canonical IR标准化异构样本并记录源路径、目标合约、数据接口、参考文件状态、静态代码特征、风险标签和目标函数提示。normalizes heterogeneous samples and records the source path, target contract, data interface, reference-file status, static code features, risk labels, and objectivefunction hints.

  • L2 语义 IRL2 Semantic IR提取种子样本的数学编程语义，包括目标函数、集合、参数、变量、约束、数据接口和潜在的建模风险。extracts the mathematical-programming semantics of the seed sample, including the objective function, sets, parameters, variables, constraints, data interface, and potential modeling risks.

  • L3 合成红外L3 Synthetic IR基于 L2 种子和历史接受的合成池生成新的建模任务抽象。它不得复制种子问题陈述、答案、代码、LP 文件、文件名、路径、确切数字或公式。相反，它改变了域、实体、客观语义、变量族、约束族、参数模式、数值范围和数据形状。generates a new modeling-task abstraction based on the L2 seed and the historical accepted synthetic pool. It must not copy the seed problem statement, answer, code, LP file, file name, path, exact numbers, or formulation. Instead, it varies the domain, entities, objective semantics, variable families, constraint families, parameter schema, numerical range, and data shape.

为了进一步降低数据泄漏的风险，我们在整个蒸馏过程中应用泄漏控制检查。候选合成样本与保留的评估样本在多个层面上进行比较，包括表面问题措辞和抽象建模结构。语义相似性过滤用于拒绝几乎重复的问题陈述，而 IR 级约束则防止重复使用相同的客观语义、约束模式、参数模式或数值。这些控制确保接受的 SFT 样本不仅在文本上与测试集不同To further reduce the risk of data leakage, we apply leakage-control checks throughout the distillation process. Candidate synthetic samples are compared against held-out evaluation samples at multiple levels, including surface-level problem wording and abstract modeling structure. Semantic-similarity filtering is used to reject near-duplicate problem statements, while IR-level constraints prevent the reuse of the same objective semantics, constraint patterns, parameter schema, or numerical values. These controls ensure that the accepted SFT samples differ from the test set not only in textual

图 14：Figure 14:OR 建模的 SFT 数据蒸馏框架概述。该管道将​​高质量的种子样本转换为规范和语义的中间表示，生成新颖的合成 IR，将其呈现为多种问题格式，验证相应的答案，并将接受的合成 IR 反馈到下一轮自蒸馏中。Overview of the SFT data distillation framework for OR modeling. The pipeline converts high-quality seed samples into canonical and semantic intermediate representations, generates novel synthetic IRs, renders them into multiple problem formats, validates the corresponding answers, and feeds accepted synthetic IRs back into the next self-distillation round.

表达式，而且还包括数据形式和建模结构，从而减少模型学习泄漏的基准实例而不是可概括的 OR 建模行为的可能性。expression, but also in data form and modeling structure, thereby reducing the possibility that the model learns leaked benchmark instances rather than generalizable OR modeling behaviors.

L3 生成后，管道将合成 IR 渲染为 DP、DT 或 DPS 问题。 DP 样本将数据直接嵌入到语句中，DT 样本包含结构化 Markdown 表，DPS 样本生成外部文件，例如After L3 generation, the pipeline renders the synthetic IR into DP, DT, or DPS problems. DP samples embed data directly into the statement, DT samples contain structured Markdown tables, and DPS samples generate external files such asdata.json , instance.json，或 CSV 文件。然后，答案呈现阶段根据特定于任务的输出契约生成目标响应。对于 Markdown 风格的答案，响应包含, or CSV files. The answer rendering stage then produces target responses according to the task-specific output contract. For Markdown-style answers, the response contains<think> , <model>, 和, and<python>标签，而Python代码使用Gurobi构建和求解模型并输出目标值。这样，模型学习的不是单一的代码模板，而是不同任务要求下的一系列建模输出协议。tags, while the Python code uses Gurobi to build and solve the model and output the objective value. In this way, the model learns not a single code template, but a family of modeling-output protocols under different task requirements.

该管道进一步采用飞轮式迭代。每轮接受 SFT 的样本都会将其相应的合成 IR 累积到父池中，用于下一代轮。因此，该模型不仅引用了原始的语义 IR，还引用了先前接受的合成结构。这逐渐减少了对原始种子的直接依赖并扩展了建模结构空间，同时仍然使用种子数据作为锚点以防止分布漂移。如图 14 所示，整体框架连接种子样本、中间表示、问题呈现、答案呈现、自动验证和接受池反馈。The pipeline further adopts a flywheel-style iteration. Samples accepted into SFT in each round have their corresponding Synthetic IRs accumulated into the parent pool for the next generation round. The model therefore refers not only to the original Semantic IR, but also to previously accepted synthetic structures. This gradually reduces direct dependence on the original seeds and expands the modeling-structure space, while still using the seed data as anchors to prevent distributional drift. As illustrated in Figure 14, the overall framework connects seed samples, intermediate representations, problem rendering, answer rendering, automatic validation, and accepted-pool feedback.

3.3.3 合同意识清洁和 CoT 增强3.3.3 Contract-Aware Cleaning and CoT Enhancement

初步 SFT 运行的观察结果促使我们从仅仅扩大自蒸馏数据集转向提高已蒸馏样本的可学习性。在 4.4 节中报告的 3K、10K 和 50K 之间的比较中，10K 数据集已经显着改善了 Bench4Opt 面向协议的任务，但仍低于 NL4OPT 和 OptiBench 的基线。进一步扩展到 50K 并不能恢复这种能力。因此，我们不是继续将相同的管道扩展到100K，而是对现有的10K数据进行细粒度的清理，重点关注建模错误和思想链表达。The observations from preliminary SFT runs motivate a shift from merely enlarging the self-distilled dataset to improving the learnability of already distilled samples. In the comparison among 3K, 10K, and 50K reported in Section 4.4, the 10K dataset already significantly improves Bench4Opt protocol-oriented tasks, but it remains below the baseline on NL4OPT and OptiBench. Further scaling to 50K does not recover this ability. Therefore, instead of continuing to expand the same pipeline to 100K, we perform fine-grained cleaning of the existing 10K data, focusing on modeling errors and chain-of-thought expression.

清理过程针对前一阶段暴露的具体错误类型。在优化建模任务中，直接损害训练信号的结构错误包括不正确的变量类型、错误的目标方向、错误的约束方向、缺失容量或需求约束、比率或平均值处理不当以及Gurobi API的错误使用。例如，计数、车辆、路线、分配和选择等对象通常建模为整数或二进制变量，而吨、升、流量和工作时间等连续量不应过度整数化。The cleaning process targets concrete error types exposed in the previous stage. In optimization modeling tasks, structural errors that directly damage the training signal include incorrect variable types, wrong objective direction, wrong constraint direction, missing capacity or demand constraints, improper handling of ratios or averages, and incorrect use of the Gurobi API. For example, objects such as counts, vehicles, routes, assignments, and selections are usually modeled as integer or binary variables, whereas continuous quantities such as tons, liters, flow rates, and working hours should not be over-integerized.

33

3.4 人工智能辅助数据构建和细化3.4 AI-Assisted Data Construction and Refinement

我们设计了两个清洁分支。第一个分支是We design two cleaning branches. The first branch is仅清洁clean-only，修复真实的建模或实现错误，同时尽可能保留原始推理风格。它不会主动添加新的解释内容。第二个分支是, which repairs real modeling or implementation errors while preserving the original reasoning style as much as possible. It does not actively add new explanatory content. The second branch is清洁CoTClean-CoT，它修复错误并向目标响应添加简洁的建模检查表。该清单使, which repairs errors and adds a concise modeling checklist to the target response. This checklist makes the<think>块明确涵盖变量类型、目标方向、关键约束、比率或非线性形式以及 Gurobi 执行操作。目标不是生成长的自由形式推理，而是将模型重复暴露于稳定的建模模式：确定变量域、确定目标、检查约束并在 Gurobi 中实施公式。这种设计与最近的研究结果一致，即当推理痕迹结构化且与任务相关时，CoT 式 SFT 可以更有效地转移推理能力（Yu 等人，2025；Lin 等人，2025；Yeo 等人，2025）。block explicitly cover variable types, objective direction, key constraints, ratio or nonlinear forms, and Gurobi execution actions. The goal is not to generate long free-form reasoning, but to expose the model repeatedly to a stable modeling pattern: determine variable domains, determine the objective, check constraints, and implement the formulation in Gurobi. This design is consistent with recent findings that CoT-style SFT can transfer reasoning ability more effectively when the reasoning traces are structured and task-relevant (Yu et al., 2025; Lin et al., 2025; Yeo et al., 2025).

清理工作流程使用四个级别的门控：清理者、审核者、本地验证和导出检查。清理器生成修复后的候选答案。评审者独立确定候选者是否适合 SFT 并阻止过度修复，例如错误地将连续变量更改为整数、删除必要的约束或错误处理比率和大 M 公式。然后，本地验证检查代码可执行性、Gurobi 调用、输出契约和 LP 写入路径。在完整的 10K 运行中，clean-only 通过了审阅者筛选 10,000 个样本中的 9,043 个样本，8,731 个样本最终通过了验证并导出。 Clean-CoT 在 10,000 个样本中通过了审稿人筛选，其中 9,174 个样本通过了审核，最终导出了 8,881 个样本。The cleaning workflow uses four levels of gating: cleaner, reviewer, local validation, and export checking. The cleaner generates repaired candidate answers. The reviewer independently determines whether a candidate is suitable for SFT and blocks over-repair, such as incorrectly changing continuous variables into integers, deleting necessary constraints, or mishandling ratio and big-M formulations. Local validation then checks code executability, Gurobi calls, output contracts, and LP writing paths. In the full 10K run, clean-only passes reviewer filtering for 9,043 out of 10,000 samples, and 8,731 samples finally pass validation and are exported. Clean-CoT passes reviewer filtering for 9,174 out of 10,000 samples, and 8,881 samples are finally exported.

总体而言，这个清理阶段使自蒸馏的 SFT 数据更具契约意识且更易于学习。对于标记的 Markdown 目标，简短的结构Overall, this cleaning stage makes self-distilled SFT data more contract-aware and more learnable. For tagged Markdown targets, short structured<think>块将最容易出错的优化建模检查点注入训练分布中。对于纯代码和 LP 结构等价任务，输出契约仍然严格。blocks inject the most error-prone optimization-modeling checkpoints into the training distribution. For code-only and LP-structureequivalence tasks, the output contract remains strict.

3.3.4 具有通用能力锚定的渐进式 SFT3.3.4 Progressive SFT with General Capability Anchoring

我们的扩展实验揭示了 SFT 必须同时灌输的两种功能之间的冲突：求解器 API 使用中的语法精度和自然语言建模中的语义推理。在单阶段设置中，来自纯代码目标（例如 Bench4Opt）和推理丰富的目标（例如 NL4OPT）的梯度更新可能会产生破坏性干扰，导致从 10K 样本扩展到 50K 样本时观察到非单调性能。为了减少这种冲突，我们将这些能力维度解耦为两阶段渐进训练策略。这遵循渐进领域适应的总体思路，其中广泛的任务能力和专门的调整被分为不同的训练阶段（Zhang et al., 2025a）。Our scaling experiments reveal a conflict between two capabilities that SFT must instill simultaneously: syntactic precision in solver-API usage and semantic reasoning in natural-language modeling. In a single-stage setup, gradient updates from pure code targets such as Bench4Opt and reasoning-rich targets such as NL4OPT may interfere destructively, leading to the non-monotonic performance observed when scaling from 10K to 50K samples. To reduce this conflict, we decouple these capability dimensions into a two-stage progressive training strategy. This follows the general idea of progressive domain adaptation, where broad task competence and specialized tuning are separated into different training stages (Zhang et al., 2025a).

第一阶段，Stage I,求解器-API 对齐Solver-API Alignment，专门针对纯代码 OR 目标进行训练，包括 LP 编写和可行性任务。它使用保守的学习率 5 × 10, trains exclusively on code-only OR targets, including LP-writing and feasibility tasks. It uses a conservative learning rate of 5 × 10−7为 Gurobi 调用、数据加载模式和输出契约建立稳定的语法主干。第二阶段，to establish a stable syntactic backbone for Gurobi invocations, data-loading patterns, and output contracts. Stage II,完整 OR 建模 SFTFull OR Modeling SFT，从这个检查点继续在完整的 Clean-CoT 10K 数据集上进行，学习率为 1 × 10, continues from this checkpoint on the complete Clean-CoT 10K dataset with a learning rate of 1 × 10−6。这使得模型能够学习公式化和推理，同时保留低级求解器语法。通过将求解器-API 对齐与完整建模适应分开，训练过程减少了在单片 SFT 中观察到的破坏性干扰（Yıldız 等人，2024）。. This allows the model to learn formulation and reasoning while preserving low-level solver syntax. By separating solver-API alignment from full modeling adaptation, the training process reduces the destructive interference observed in monolithic SFT (Yıldız et al., 2024).

然而，OR 特定的 SFT 仍然会导致一般语言理解和代码语法健康状况的退化，如表 13 中的课程监控和 NL4OPT 下降所观察到的那样。这种向领域专业化的转变可能会侵蚀自然语言建模和主体多样性所需的语义表示。为了抵消这种遗忘，我们用 10% - 15% 的通用指令数据来增强第二阶段的混合物，包括开放域 QA、多轮对话和创意写作。这种通用能力锚定可以维护来自不同语言任务的梯度更新，有助于保持 NL4OPT 性能，并减轻在代理推出实验中观察到的参数同质化。因此，SFT 检查点为下游代理管道提供了更稳定和可探索的初始化。However, OR-specific SFT can still cause degradation in general language understanding and code syntax health, as observed in curriculum monitoring and the NL4OPT decline in Table 13. This drift toward domain specialization may erode the semantic representations needed for natural-language modeling and agentic diversity. To counteract this forgetting, we augment the Stage II mixture with 10% − 15% general-purpose instruction data, including open-domain QA, multi-turn dialogue, and creative writing. This general-capability anchoring maintains gradient updates from diverse linguistic tasks, helps preserve NL4OPT performance, and mitigates the parametric homogenization observed in agentic rollout experiments. As a result, the SFT checkpoint provides a more stable and explorable initialization for the downstream agentic pipeline.

3.4 人工智能辅助数据构建和细化3.4 AI-Assisted Data Construction and Refinement

As illustrated in Figure 15, the workflow combines an AI-based Cleaner and independent Reviewer with executable validation gates. Their tasks are inspired by multi-agent validation architectures (Vallabhaneni et al., 2026). 图 15：Figure 15:人工智能辅助质量门工作流程。此多阶段验证过程使用 Cleaner、Reviewer 和代码执行验证来确保最终数据集的质量和可审核性。The AI-Assisted Quality Gates workflow. This multi-stage validation process uses the Cleaner, Reviewer, and code execution Validation to ensure the quality and auditability of the final dataset.

发明由代理人执行以确保再现性。本节介绍这些工具的用途以及它们在我们的管道中的编排方式。invention is performed by the agents to ensure reproducibility. This section describes what these tools do and how they are orchestrated in our pipeline.

  • 更干净。Cleaner.给定原始问题和答案，清理器会生成修复后的候选者。其系统提示对案例研究中描述的领域约定、目标契约和基于证据的修复原则进行编码。它主动修复明显的错误（例如，变量域不匹配、缺少约束、明显的 Gurobi API 误用），但有时会引入新的不一致。Given an original problem and answer, the Cleaner generates a repaired candidate. Its system prompt encodes the domain conventions, target contracts, and evidence-based repair principles described in the case studies. It is proactive in fixing clear errors (e.g., variable domain mismatches, missing constraints, obvious Gurobi API misuse) but can sometimes introduce new inconsistencies.

  • 审稿人。Reviewer.审核者独立评估每个被清理的候选者，而无需了解清理者的推理。它只能看到原始问题、原始答案和清理后的答案。它的任务是检查内部一致性和事实与提示的一致性，并阻止不支持的更改或引入新的错误。这种对抗性检查对于防止过度修复至关重要。The Reviewer evaluates each cleaned candidate independently, without access to the Cleaner’s reasoning. It sees only the original problem, the original answer, and the cleaned answer. Its task is to check internal consistency and factual alignment with the prompt, and to block changes that are unsupported or introduce new errors. This adversarial check is essential to prevent over-repair.

  • 诊断解析器。Diagnostic Resolver.当本地验证（针对 Gurobi 的代码执行）失败时，解析器会摄取执行报告、问题陈述、当前公式和生成的代码。它会生成结构化诊断，将故障归因于一个或多个类别：语义建模错误、API 使用错误、数据模式不匹配或求解器不兼容的表达式。此诊断可以触发第二阶段修复或标记样本以供手动审查。When local validation (code execution against Gurobi) fails, the Resolver ingests the execution report, the problem statement, the current formulation, and the generated code. It produces a structured diagnosis attributing the failure to one or more categories: semantic modeling error, API usage error, data schema mismatch, or solverincompatible expression. This diagnosis can trigger a second-stage repair or flag the sample for manual review.

开发过程中的一个事件说明了独立审阅者的重要性。由于运输问题，清洁工非常有信心地接受了样品（The importance of the independent Reviewer is illustrated by an incident during development. For a transportation problem, the Cleaner accepted the sample with high confidence (confidence = 0.95）并仅报告格式更改。然而，对清理后的输出进行检查后发现，从仓库 2 到商店 3 的成本已无意中从 2 更改为 3。) and reported only formatting changes. However, inspection of the cleaned output revealed that the cost from Warehouse 2 to Store 3 had been inadvertently changed from 2 to 3 in the<model>描述，而 Python 代码正确使用 2. 审阅者检测到这种不一致（description, while the Python code correctly used 2. The Reviewer detected this inconsistency (decision = needs_repair , confidence = 0.95）诊断：) with the diagnosis:issues = ["data_inconsistency"]。如果没有这个门，冲突样本就会进入 SFT。. Without this gate, the conflicting sample would have entered SFT.

在整个 Clean-CoT 运行的 10,000 个样本中，审查者拦截了 Cleaner 已接受的 826 个样本；随后的本地验证又捕获了 293 个样本。因此，最终导出的 8,881 个样本是三个独立质量检查的交集：清洁生成、审阅者批准和验证者执行。这种多阶段质量架构纯粹是规则驱动和审计跟踪的：最终 SFT 集中的每个样本都带有可验证的历史记录。Across the full Clean-CoT run of 10,000 samples, the Reviewer intercepted 826 samples that the Cleaner had accepted; subsequent local validation caught an additional 293 samples. The final exported 8,881 samples are thus the intersection of three independent quality checks: Cleaner generation, Reviewer approval, and Validator execution. This multi-stage quality architecture is purely rule-driven and audit-trailed: every sample in the final SFT set carries verifiable history.

  1. 实验结果Experimental Results

4 实验结果4 Experimental Results

我们的评估涵盖了第 3 节中全栈工作的两部分：提高设备效率的操作员级优化，以及将稳定堆栈转化为下游功能的训练后管道。我们从操作员级别开始，针对相同的 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 训练工作负载（其瓶颈激励了它们）验证了两种内核优化策略（第 2.3.1 节）。第 4.1 节在架构固有内核的重头上评估 AuraKernel，其中循环中的分析调整减少了主要稀疏注意力和标准化内核的每次调用持续时间。第 4.2 节评估了碎片化、内存绑定尾部的主动算子链融合，其中将框架发出的急切链折叠到融合的 AscendC 内核中，消除了冗余启动、中间张量和全局内存流量。由于两者都是根据分析的训练工作负载而不是孤立的微基准来衡量的，因此报告的加速直接附加到第 2.1 节中确定的设备时间制度。Our evaluation covers both halves of the full-stack effort in Section 3: the operator-level optimizations that raise device efficiency, and the post-training pipeline that turns the stabilized stack into downstream capability. We begin at the operator level and validate the two kernel-optimization strategies against the same DeepSeek-V4-Pro training workload whose bottlenecks motivated them (Section 2.3.1). Section 4.1 evaluates AuraKernel on the heavy head of architecture-intrinsic kernels, where profiling-in-the-loop tuning reduces the per-invocation duration of the dominant sparseattention and normalization kernels. Section 4.2 evaluates the proactive operator-chain fusion on the fragmented, memory-bound tail, where collapsing framework-emitted eager chains into fused AscendC kernels removes redundant launches, intermediate tensors, and global-memory traffic. Because both are measured on the profiled training workload rather than on isolated microbenchmarks, the reported speedups attach directly to the device-time regimes identified in Section 2.1.

然后，我们使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 作为实验平台在 Ascend 910C 上评估训练后流程。我们组织了从基础设施优化到手术室特定模型适应的实验。我们首先验证训练堆栈在 Ascend NPU 上是否稳定，仅保留较大的 SFT 稳定性运行作为代表性系统证据。然后我们分析 SFT 结垢和清洁实验，然后进行匹配的 CPT→SFT 转移实验。最终的端到端结果部分作为 Base、CPT、SFT 和 CPT+SFT 之间的总结比较。We then evaluate the post-training pipeline on Ascend 910C using DeepSeek-V4-Flash as the experimental platform. We organize the experiments from infrastructure optimization to OR-specific model adaptation. We first verify that the training stack is stable on Ascend NPUs, retaining only the larger SFT stability run as the representative systems evidence. We then analyze the SFT scale and cleaning experiments, followed by the matched CPT→SFT transfer experiment. The final end-to-end result section is left as the summary comparison across Base, CPT, SFT, and CPT+SFT.

为了清楚起见，本节中的 SFT 标签指的是训练轨迹中两个相关但不同的点。 SFT-10K 是未清洗的 10K 运行，用于水垢分析（表 10）并作为表 11 中的预清洗基线。 Clean-CoT 是已清洗的 CoT 增强检查点；传输和端到端表（表 12 和 13）中的 SFT 行使用此匹配条件清理的 SFT 检查点，因此其 0-shot Pass@1 值与 Clean-CoT 行对齐。For clarity, the SFT labels in this section refer to two related but distinct points in the training trajectory. SFT-10K is the uncleaned 10K run used for scale analysis (Table 10) and as the precleaning baseline in Table 11. Clean-CoT is the cleaned, CoT-enhanced checkpoint; the SFT row in the transfer and end-to-end tables (Tables 12 and 13) uses this matched-condition cleaned SFT checkpoint, so its 0-shot Pass@1 values align with the Clean-CoT row.

4.1 通过 AuraKernel 实现高效内核4.1 Efficient Kernels via AuraKernel

MAC 比率仅为 ≈ 0.10，并且稀疏闪电索引器梯度是标量和负载绑定的（AIV 标量 ≈ 0.81，CFA 路径上的 MTE2 负载 ≈ 0.66），因此持续时间下限是由全局内存 (GM) 流量和地址计算而不是算术设置的。一个顶-against a MAC ratio of only ≈ 0.10, and the sparse lightning-indexer gradient is scalar- and loadbound (AIV scalar ≈ 0.81, MTE2 load ≈ 0.66 on the CFA path), so the duration floor is set by global-memory (GM) traffic and address computation rather than arithmetic. A top-𝑘对真实路由索引的局部性分析解释了此流量的来源：相邻查询选择几乎相同的压缩键值块（相邻查询 Jaccard ≈ 0.998 atlocality analysis on real routing indices explains where this traffic originates: adjacent queries select almost identical compressed key-value blocks (adjacent-query Jaccard ≈ 0.998 at𝐾=1024），但基线每个任务处理一个查询，并每个查询重新收集和重新分散相同的共享块一次。 AuraKernel 将其映射到两个基于硬件的重写：（i）它对每个任务进行批处理连续查询，并在因果范围内密集地访问压缩端，而不是每个查询稀疏收集，从而大致按批处理因子削减压缩端 GM 流量； (ii) 一旦批处理将地板转移到立方体引擎上，它就会在 BF16 中存储最重的 FP32 立方体→矢量切换，以在精确的顶部-下缩小剩余的 GM 往返范围。=1024), yet the baseline processes one query per task and re-gathers and re-scatters the same shared blocks once per query. AuraKernel maps this to two hardware-grounded rewrites: (i) it batches consecutive queries per task and accesses the compressed side densely over the causal range instead of per-query sparse gather, cutting compressed-side GM traffic by roughly the batch factor; and (ii) once batching shifts the floor onto the cube engine, it stores the heaviest FP32 cube→vector hand-offs in BF16 to narrow the remaining GM round-trips, under an exact top-𝑘隶属掩码、梯度幅度和损失稳定性门，使重写在数字上保持忠实。增益落在建模瓶颈上：在压缩比为 128 和 4 时，稀疏注意力后向提高了 1.24× 和 1.21×，前向提高了 1.23× 和 1.09×，闪电索引器梯度在 CFA 路径上提高了 1.16×，在 SCFA 路径上提高了 1.21×（表 7，图 16 和 18）；在每种情况下，每次调用的减少都集中在 MTE2/MTE3 加载存储管道以及索引器的标量管道中，而不是矢量计算中，这证实了改进来自于消除 GM 流量和地址开销，而不是来自于提高原始计算利用率。membership mask and gradient-magnitude and loss-stability gates that keep the rewrite numerically faithful. The gain lands on the modeled bottleneck: the sparse-attention backward improves by 1.24× and 1.21× at compression ratios 128 and 4, its forward by 1.23× and 1.09×, and the lightning-indexer gradient by 1.16× on the CFA path and 1.21× on the SCFA path (Table 7, Figures 16 and 18); in every case the per-call reduction concentrates in the MTE2/MTE3 load-store pipes, and additionally the scalar pipe for the indexer, rather than in vector compute, confirming that the improvement comes from removing GM traffic and address overhead rather than from raising raw compute utilization.

图 18：Figure 18:AuraKernel 对 Ascend 910C 上的稀疏闪电索引器梯度进行优化。 (a) 每次调用后向任务持续时间加速，在完整 CFA 路径（cmp.比率 0）上为 1.16×，在 SCFA 路径（cmp.比率 4）上为 1.21×。 (b,c) 两个比率优化前后的 AIV 管道时间分解：内核受标量和负载限制，减少集中在标量和 MTE2 负载管道而不是向量计算，这与削减地址计算和全局内存负载流量一致。AuraKernel optimization of the sparse lightning-indexer gradient on Ascend 910C.
