(b,c) AIV pipeline-time decomposition before and after optimization at the two ratios: the kernel is scalar- and load-bound, and the reductions concentrate in the scalar and MTE2-load pipes rather than in vector compute, consistent with cutting address-computation and global-memory-load traffic.

和标量限制而不是计算限制。分析定位了这些内核内部结构效率低下的原因：无权重 RMS 归一化前向运行串行每头循环，使矢量引擎在高标量比率后空闲，其后向发出较差的标量代码，因为减少未显式提升到 FP32，并且多个 MHC 运算符通过原子添加进行累积或在主内核之前进行独立的零初始化启动。由于这些是 Triton 运算符，AuraKernel 在 Triton 编程模型中重组它们，仅调整主机端平铺和管道参数，同时保留内核数学：2D 头阻塞和多行处理来替换串行头循环、显式 FP32 累加和头维度展开、三相加载计算存储软件管道以重叠 MTE2、向量和 MTE3 管道，以及消除原子添加和独立零初始化启动。单个活动可驱动所有 14 个 Triton-Ascend 算子（RMS 归一化对和 DeepSeek-V4 MHC 系列）以 2.06 倍的平均速度和 11.90 倍的最佳加速比 100% 完成，向后 RMS 归一化提高了 3.42 倍（表 7，图 17）。增益表现为管道移位：AIV 向量计算比率急剧上升，而标量比率下降（RMS 归一化前向 27.8% → 67.5% 向量和 31.5% → 17.2% 标量；后向 17.8% → 32.0% 向量和 34.8% →≈ 18% 标量），将标量和地址限制的缩减循环转变为向量计算主导内核。最大的胜利集中在结构效率低下的缩减级运营商上，而已经达到带宽限制的运营商仅获得了几个百分点。and scalar-bound rather than compute-bound. Profiling localizes the cause to structural inefficiency inside these kernels: the weight-free RMS-normalization forward runs a serial per-head loop that leaves the vector engine idle behind a high scalar ratio, its backward emits poor scalar code because the reductions are not explicitly promoted to FP32, and several MHC operators accumulate through atomic-add or precede the main kernel with a standalone zero-init launch. Because these are Triton operators, AuraKernel restructures them within the Triton programming model, adjusting only host-side tiling and pipeline parameters while preserving the kernel mathematics: 2D head blocking and multi-row processing to replace the serial head loop, explicit FP32 accumulation and headdimension unrolling, three-phase load-compute-store software pipelining to overlap the MTE2, vector, and MTE3 pipes, and elimination of atomic-add and standalone zero-init launches. A single campaign drives all 14 Triton-Ascend operators (the RMS-normalization pair and the DeepSeek-V4 MHC family) to 100% completion at a 2.06× average and an 11.90× best speedup, the backward RMS-normalization improving by 3.42× (Table 7, Figure 17). The gain shows up as a pipeline shift: the AIV vector-compute ratio rises sharply while the scalar ratio falls (RMS-normalization forward 27.8% → 67.5% vector and 31.5% → 17.2% scalar; backward 17.8% → 32.0% vector and 34.8% →≈ 18% scalar), turning scalar- and address-bound reduction loops into vector-computedominated kernels. The largest wins concentrate on reduction-class operators with structural inefficiency, whereas operators already at the bandwidth limit gain only a few percent.

4.2 内核融合的性能改进4.2 Performance Improvements from Kernel Fusion

接下来我们在 2.3.3 节中评估算子融合和重写路径。测量单位与上面的 AuraKernel 结果不同。尽管 AuraKernel 改进了已在跟踪中显示为大型执行单元的内核，但此路径更改了执行单元本身。因此，基线是 Python、torch 和 autograd 为模型级操作发出的完整的 eager 链，或者是先前将阶段降低到的通用 Triton 内核，而不是从该链中选择的单个原始运算符。这种区别对于解释结果很重要：融合或重写的实现可以保留相同的数学工作，同时删除原始跟踪中围绕它的启动、中间张量、数据类型往返和分散式写回，同时将剩余的算术重新定位到具有显式片上驻留和管道调度的基板上。我们按照第 2.3.3 节的顺序提出目标：首先是 mHC 套件，然后是有限的 SwiGLU 和统一的 RoPE。We next evaluate the operator fusion and rewrite path in Section 2.3.3. The measurement unit is different from the AuraKernel results above. Whereas AuraKernel improves kernels that already appear as large execution units in the trace, this path changes the unit of execution itself. The baseline is therefore the complete eager chain emitted by Python, torch, and autograd for a modellevel operation, or the generic Triton kernel that a stage was previously lowered into, not a single primitive operator selected from that chain. This distinction is important for interpreting the result: a fused or rewritten implementation may preserve the same mathematical work while removing the launches, intermediate tensors, dtype round trips, and scatter-style writebacks that surrounded it in the original trace, and while relocating the remaining arithmetic onto a substrate with explicit on-chip residency and pipeline scheduling. We present the targets in the order of Section 2.3.3: the mHC suite first, then limited SwiGLU and unified RoPE.

mHC 结果一起执行了该路径的两个条目，因此表 8 分别报告了每个阶段。预投影吸收了RMS归一化片段和原始的预BMM内核；它的后向吸收了 BMM 前向路径、RMS 后向片段、转换和元素重建；后投影取代了 add/post-BMM1/cast 部分。这三个阶段在模块级别分别达到 3.03×、1.88× 和 2.74×（表 8）。这种归因的证实与记录计数无关：直接从优化后跟踪中对插入的 mHC 融合内核求和，可重现相同阶段的总数，其中The mHC results exercise the two entries of this path together, so Table 8 reports each stage separately. Pre-projection absorbs the RMS-normalization fragments and the original pre-BMM kernel; its backward absorbs the pre-BMM backward path, RMS backward fragments, casts, and elementwise reconstruction; and post-projection replaces the add/post-BMM1/cast portion. The three stages reach 3.03×, 1.88×, and 2.74× at the module level (Table 8). This attribution is corroborated independently of the record counts: summing the inserted mHC fused kernels directly from the post-optimization trace reproduces the same stage totals, withWindMhcPostPart所有匹配调用中最大的贡献者为 0.313 秒。后向投影保留在原来的位置the single largest contributor at 0.313 s across all matched calls. 其中四个内核（前向和后向无权 RMS 归一化以及前向和后向预 BMM 收缩）通过 Triton 重写而不是通过急切链融合到达 AscendC 基底，并且在单算子级别将原始 Triton 内核与其 AscendC 替换版本进行比较，以隔离基底更改本身的贡献。表 9 报告了这一比较。在相同 BF16 形状下测量的 RMS 归一化对，向前从 2307 µs 下降到 309 µs (7.48×)，向后从 3318 µs 下降到 439 µs (7.55×)。其中大部分来自平铺变化，因为通用 Triton 内核跨 4096 个块启动，而Four of these kernels—the weight-free RMS normalization forward and backward and the pre-BMM contraction forward and backward—reach the AscendC substrate through the Triton rewrite rather than through eager-chain fusion, and comparing the original Triton kernel against its AscendC replacement at the single-operator level isolates what the substrate change alone contributes. Table 9 reports this comparison. The RMS-normalization pair, measured at an identical BF16 shape, drops from 2307 µs to 309 µs forward (7.48×) and from 3318 µs to 439 µs backward (7.55×). Much of this comes from the tiling change, as the generic Triton kernel launches across 4096 blocks whereas

AscendC 内核每个内核跨 48 个块处理多行。这些比率根据原始 Triton 内核衡量 Triton 到 AscendC 的重写，因此与第 4.1 节中的 AuraKernel 数字 (11.90×/3.42×) 不同，后者以不同的形状进一步调整生成的 AscendC 内核。 BMM 之前的收缩，其重写也折叠在混合精度合约中（FLOAT 输入变为 BF16），向前提高了 2.10 倍，向后提高了 1.89 倍。 AIV 管道分解证实了第 2.3.3 节的亲和力机制，而不仅仅是时钟减少：在前 BMM 上，矢量计算比率从 0.17 上升到 0.86，而 MTE2 负载比率从 0.59 下降到 0.35，因此the AscendC kernel processes multiple rows per core across 48 blocks. These ratios measure the Triton-to-AscendC rewrite against the original Triton kernel, and are therefore distinct from the AuraKernel figures in Section 4.1 (11.90×/3.42×), which tune the resulting AscendC kernel further at a different shape. The pre-BMM contraction, whose rewrite also folds in the mixed-precision contract (FLOAT inputs become BF16), improves by 2.10× forward and 1.89× backward. The AIV pipeline decomposition confirms the affinity mechanism of Section 2.3.3 rather than a mere clock reduction: on the pre-BMM forward the vector-compute ratio rises from 0.17 to 0.86 while the MTE2 load ratio falls from 0.59 to 0.35, so theBrcb广播和 UB 驻留收缩将运动限制的通用内核转变为矢量计算主导的内核。同样的偏移以较弱的形式出现在后向内核上（BMM 前后向矢量比 0.14 → 0.47，RMS 后向 0.28 → 0.36）。除了这些单算子增益之外，重写还带来了通用 Triton 降低的全阶段精度优势：使混合精度合约显式删除了跨 mHC 预投影调用的全阶段 FP32 向上转换。根据优化后轨迹测量，broadcast and UB-resident contraction turn a movement-bound generic kernel into a vector-compute-dominated one. The same shift, in weaker form, appears on the backward kernels (pre-BMM backward vector ratio 0.14 → 0.47, RMS backward 0.28 → 0.36). Beyond these single-operator gains, the rewrite carries a stage-wide precision benefit that a generic Triton lowering left on the table: making the mixed-precision contract explicit removes a full-stage FP32 up-cast across the mHC pre-projection calls. 其余目标隔离边界融合效应，其中自梯度可见张量边界消失。有限的 SwiGLU 前向将其急切的钳位和门链折叠成单个The remaining targets isolate the boundary-fusion effect, where autograd-visible tensor boundaries disappear. Limited SwiGLU forward collapses its eager clamp-and-gate chain into a singleSwiGluLimitV2调用，将模块从 1.480 s 削减到 0.167 s (8.86×)，因为急切的物化钳位常数和融合内核保留在芯片上的串联。后向路径仍然更加密集——填充、布尔物化、选择、强制转换、call, cutting the module from 1.480 s to 0.167 s (8.86×), because the eager forward materialized clamp constants and a concatenation that the fused kernel keeps on chip. The backward path is more launch-dense still—fills, boolean materialization, selections, casts,SwiGluGrad，和串联——还有融合的, and concatenation—and there the fusedSwiGluLimitBackwardV2操作员几乎删除了整个链（图 10）。在 MoE 路径中，相同的内核还吸收路由权重梯度，因此形成它的急切减少（全尺寸operator removes almost the entire chain (Figure 10). In the MoE path the same kernel also absorbs the routing-weight gradient, so the eager reduction that formed it (a full-sizeℓ · ℎ61 毫秒加 a 的乘积product of 61 ms plus aReduceSum每步 20 毫秒，总共大约 82 毫秒）消失在融合通道中，不增加内核成本。将路由梯度链算作向后的一部分，该模块从 805 ms 下降到 246 ms (3.27×)。 RoPE 保留了更大的优化残差，因为重写的路径仍然包含形状和索引支持运算符，但表 8 中的计数核算暴露了相同的机制，产生 2.33×（前向）和 3.54×（后向）。后向增益是两者中较大的一个，因为融合内核在图级别删除了急切的切片分配分散链。这些模块级数据聚合了查询、键值和输出站点。of 20 ms per step, about 82 ms together) disappears into the fused pass at no added kernel cost. Counting the routing-gradient chain as part of the backward, the module falls from 805 ms to 246 ms (3.27×). RoPE retains a larger optimized residual because the rewritten path still contains shape and indexing support operators, but the count accounting in Table 8 exposes the same mechanism, yielding 2.33× (forward) and 3.54× (backward). The backward gain is the larger of the two because the fused kernel removes the eager slice-assignment scatter chain at the graph level. These module-level figures aggregate the query, key-value, and output sites.

这些结果与第 2.3.1 节中的瓶颈解剖结构形成了闭环。这些增益不能仅用更快的算术来解释，如图 19 所明确的：它们来自于改变哪些张量被具体化、哪些边界对 autograd 可见、哪些 dtype 跨越全局内存，以及（对于 Triton 起源的阶段）算术运行的基底。这就是为什么最强烈的影响出现在由移动、转换和写回主导的链上，而不是在计算密集的立方体内核上，为什么重写内核上的资源利用转变将标量和移动限制循环变成矢量计算主导的循环，以及为什么适当的基线是发出的跟踪切片、阶段子图或通用 Triton 内核而不是孤立的原始运算符。These results close the loop with the bottleneck anatomy in Section 2.3.1. The gains are not explained by faster arithmetic alone, as Figure 19 makes explicit: they come from changing which tensors are materialized, which boundaries are visible to autograd, which dtype crosses global memory, and—for the Triton-originated stages—which substrate the arithmetic runs on. This is why the strongest effects appear on chains dominated by movement, conversion, and writeback rather than on compute-dense cube kernels, why the resource-utilization shift on the rewritten kernels turns scalar- and movement-bound loops into vector-compute-dominated ones, and why the appropriate baseline is the emitted trace slice, stage subgraph, or generic Triton kernel rather than an isolated primitive operator.

4.3 Ascend SuperPOD 的长期训练稳定性4.3 Long-term Training Stability with Ascend SuperPOD

稳定的训练是有意义的下游评估的先决条件。如果训练过程不稳定，后续的基准差异将变得难以解释。在这项工作中，我们在基于Ascend的环境中实现了DeepSeek-V4-Flash的稳定SFT训练，总共有8个节点和128个Ascend 910C NPU。Stable training is a prerequisite for meaningful downstream evaluation. If the training process is unstable, subsequent benchmark differences become difficult to interpret. In this work, we implement stable SFT training of DeepSeek-V4-Flash in a Ascend-based environment with 8 nodes and 128 Ascend 910C NPUs in total.

以50K自蒸馏数据集为例，我们在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上完成了相对全面的SFT验证运行。训练配置使用Using the 50K self-distilled dataset as an example, we complete a relatively full-scale SFT verification run on DeepSeek-V4-Flash. The training configuration usesGBS=128 , MBS=1, 和, andSEQ_LEN=8192。该模型经过训练. The model is trained for800步骤和检查点保存在steps, and checkpoints are saved at200 , 400 , 600, 和, and800步骤。训练日志显示作业成功达到steps. The training log shows that the job successfully reachesiteration 800/800，没有跳过迭代或 NaN 迭代。在最后一次迭代中，, without skipped iterations or NaN iterations. At the final iteration, thelm loss收敛到大约converges to approximately0.079, 的, themtp_1 loss大约是is approximately0.093，梯度范数保持在正常范围内。整体训练曲线不断下降，然后逐渐趋于稳定。, and the gradient norm remains within a normal range. The overall training curve decreases continuously and then gradually becomes stable.

如图 20 所示，这些观察结果表明，当前的数据预处理管道、MindSpeed-LLM（华为 Ascend，2024）训练脚本、DeepSeek-V4-Flash 基础模型和 8 节点 910C 训练环境形成了可重复且可持续的全参数 SFT 工作流程。这为后续不同数据规模、数据混合和面向任务的数据蒸馏策略的实验提供了稳定的基础。As shown in Figure 20, these observations indicate that the current data preprocessing pipeline, MindSpeed-LLM (Huawei Ascend, 2024) training script, DeepSeek-V4-Flash base model, and 8-node 910C training environment form a reproducible and sustainable full-parameter SFT workflow. This provides a stable foundation for subsequent experiments on different data scales, data mixtures, and task-oriented data-distillation strategies.

4.4 SFT结垢和清洗分析4.4 SFT Scaling and Cleaning Analysis

对3K、10K和50K自蒸馏数据的首次系统实验表明，SFT带来了两种相反的效果。一方面，SFT 帮助模型学习 B4OFeasible 等代码协议任务。使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 作为基线，我们发现 B4O-Feasible 使用 SFT-10K 从 60.47 提高到 65.07，B4O-ORGEval 从 34.26 提高到 47.21。这些收益表明 SFT 使模型更熟悉仅代码输出，The first systematic experiments on 3K, 10K, and 50K self-distilled data show that SFT brings two opposite effects. On the one hand, SFT helps the model learn code-protocol tasks such as B4OFeasible. Using DeepSeek-V4-Flash as the baseline, we find that B4O-Feasible improves from 60.47 to 65.07 with SFT-10K, and B4O-ORGEval improves from 34.26 to 47.21. These gains indicate that SFT makes the model more familiar with code-only output,data.json加载、Gurobi 模型构建、LP 写入路径和其他特定于基准的协议，其中最强的相对改进集中在结构等效性评估上。loading, Gurobi model construction, LP writing paths, and other benchmark-specific protocols, with the strongest relative improvement concentrated on structural-equivalence evaluation.

另一方面，SFT也削弱了模型原有的部分自然语言建模任务能力。 NL4OPT 上的基线获得 84.08，而 SFT-10K 获得 81.66。同样，基线在 OptiBench 上获得 63.33，而 SFT-10K 获得 62.67。进一步扩展到 50KOn the other hand, SFT also weakens part of the model’s original ability on natural-language modeling tasks. The baseline obtains 84.08 on NL4OPT, while SFT-10K obtains 81.66. Similarly, the baseline obtains 63.33 on OptiBench, while SFT-10K obtains 62.67. Further scaling to 50K does

不解决这个问题；相反，OptiBench 下降至 58.68。由于 3K、10K 和 50K 设置遵循几乎相同的结构设计，因此 50K 的降级很难将其解释为数据混合变化。一个更合理的解释是，连续迭代蒸馏引入了质量较低的样本并放大了模型自身的噪声。not solve this issue; instead, OptiBench decreases to 58.68. Since the 3K, 10K, and 50K settings follow almost the same construction design, the degradation of 50K is difficult to explain as a datamixture change. A more plausible explanation is that continuous iterative distillation introduces lower-quality samples and amplifies the model’s own noise.

表 10 支持两个观察结果。首先，监督微调为面向协议的 OR 建模任务提供了明显的好处：所有 SFT 变体相对于基本模型都改进了 B4O-Feasible，更突出的是 B4O-ORGEval，这表明自蒸馏数据包含对面向求解器的输出格式和特定​​于基准的约定的有效监督。其次，收益与数据规模并不单调。如果额外的自蒸馏样品一致有益，则 SFT-50K 预计将主导 SFT-10K，并且未清理的 SFT-10K 检查点不会低于 NL4OPT 和 OptiBench 上的基本模型。观察到的回归表明，扩展相同的自蒸馏管道也会增加弱验证或有偏差样本的权重。这些样本可以强化有用的建模模式，但它们也可以放大从基本模型继承的系统误差。因此，50K 结果不应被解释为反对 SFT 规模本身的证据，而应更多地被解释为规模必须与更强大的验证和数据质量控制相结合的证据。这促使返回到 10K 设置进行细粒度清洁，而不是进一步将未过滤的管道扩展到 100K。Table 10 supports two observations. First, supervised fine-tuning provides a clear benefit for protocol-oriented OR modeling tasks: all SFT variants improve B4O-Feasible and, more prominently, B4O-ORGEval relative to the base model, indicating that the self-distilled data contains effective supervision for solver-facing output formats and benchmark-specific conventions. Second, the gains are not monotonic with data scale. If additional self-distilled samples were uniformly beneficial, SFT-50K would be expected to dominate SFT-10K, and the uncleaned SFT-10K checkpoint would not fall below the base model on NL4OPT and OptiBench. The observed regressions suggest that scaling the same self-distillation pipeline also increases the weight of weakly verified or biased samples. Such samples can reinforce useful modeling patterns, but they can also amplify systematic errors inherited from the base model. Therefore, the 50K result should be interpreted less as evidence against SFT scale itself and more as evidence that scale must be coupled with stronger verification and data-quality control. This motivates returning to the 10K setting for fine-grained cleaning rather than further expanding the unfiltered pipeline to 100K.

清洁后的 SFT 结果验证了第 3.3.3 节中介绍的合同感知清洁和 CoT 增强框架的有效性。与未清洗的SFT-10K相比，Clean-CoT将NL4OPT提高了5.27个百分点，OptiBench提高了1.50个百分点，B4O-Feasible提高了0.86个百分点，B4O-ORGEval提高了1.52个百分点。这一结果表明，10K 数据的主要问题不是规模不足，而是语义噪声足以损害自然语言建模能力。一旦此类噪声被系统地过滤和修复，SFT 就不再损害 NL4OPT 和 OptiBench，而是超越基线和原始 SFT-10K，同时进一步改进 B4O 指标。The SFT results after cleaning validate the effectiveness of the contract-aware cleaning and CoT enhancement framework introduced in Section 3.3.3. Compared with the uncleaned SFT-10K, Clean-CoT improves NL4OPT by 5.27 percentage points, OptiBench by 1.50 percentage points, B4O-Feasible by 0.86 percentage points, and B4O-ORGEval by 1.52 percentage points. This result suggests that the main issue of the 10K data is not insufficient scale, but semantic noise that is strong enough to damage natural-language modeling ability. Once such noise is systematically filtered and repaired, SFT no longer harms NL4OPT and OptiBench, but instead outperforms both the baseline and the original SFT-10K while further improving both B4O metrics.

然而，Clean-CoT 的收益并未均匀分布在所有任务中。它在 NL4OPT 和 OptiBench 上最有效，因为这些目标允许However, the gains of Clean-CoT are not uniformly distributed across all tasks. It is most effective on NL4OPT and OptiBench because these targets allow<think> , <model>, 和, and<python>风格的答案。结构化建模检查表可以直接输入训练响应，帮助模型学习对变量域、目标方向和约束方向的明确判断。相比之下，B4O-Feasible 和 B4O-ORGEval 是纯代码任务，其中解释性的思路不能出现在输出中。因此，Clean-CoT 的优势较少直接体现在响应格式中。在更新的结果中，Clean-CoT 在 B4O-Feasible（65.93 vs. 64.31）和 B4O-ORGEval（48.73 vs. 48.28）上的表现也优于 clean-only，但差距小于 NL4OPT。这表明面向 CoT 的增强对于纯代码任务是有益的，但这些任务仍然需要在模式读取、索引对齐、变量族设计等方面进行更专门的培训。style answers. The structured modeling checklist can directly enter the training response and help the model learn explicit judgments about variable domains, objective direction, and constraint direction. By contrast, B4O-Feasible and B4O-ORGEval are code-only tasks, where explanatory chain-of-thought cannot appear in the output. Therefore, the advantage of Clean-CoT is less directly expressed in the response format. In the updated results, Clean-CoT also outperforms clean-only on B4O-Feasible (65.93 vs. 64.31) and B4O-ORGEval (48.73 vs. 48.28), but the margins are smaller than on NL4OPT. This suggests that CoT-oriented enhancement is beneficial for code-only tasks, yet these tasks still require more specialized training on schema reading, index alignment, variable-family design,model.write()路径和 LP 结构一致性。paths, and LP-structure consistency.

4.5 匹配SFT条件下CPT初始化的影响4.5 Effect of CPT Initialization under Matched SFT Conditions

OptiBench，相同的错误类型从 154 例减少到 128 例，表明建模检查表确实减少了目标和约束级别的粗略错误。然而，OptiBench, the same error type decreases from 154 to 128 cases, indicating that modeling checklists indeed reduce coarse errors at the objective and constraint levels. However,nonlinear_or_bad_gurobi_formOptiBench 上的错误没有减少；相反，病例数从 49 例增加到 56 例。这说明普通的清理和短链思想增强可以改善应该建立什么模型的问题，但不足以稳定地解决Gurobi中如何正确表达非线性项、比率、平均值和二次项的问题。这一观察结果促进了非线性修复和专门的非线性/Gurobi 合成数据的后续设计，而不是将所有问题强加到单个 Clean-CoT 路径中。errors on OptiBench do not decrease; instead, they increase from 49 to 56 cases. This shows that ordinary cleaning and short chain-of-thought enhancement can improve the question of what model should be built, but are insufficient to stably solve the question of how to correctly express nonlinear terms, ratios, averages, and quadratic terms in Gurobi. This observation motivates the subsequent design of nonlinear repair and specialized nonlinear/Gurobi synthetic data, rather than forcing all issues into a single Clean-CoT path.

因此，本次 SFT 实验的主要结论是，面向思想链的数据增强是有效的，但它必须受到约束、契约感知，并受到审阅者和验证检查的控制。它的价值不是让模型产生更长的推理，而是以稳定的形式将最容易出错的优化建模检查点注入到训练分布中。Therefore, the main conclusion of this SFT experiment is that chain-of-thought-oriented data enhancement is effective, but it must be constrained, contract-aware, and gated by reviewer and validation checks. Its value is not to make the model generate longer reasoning, but to inject the most error-prone optimization-modeling checkpoints into the training distribution in a stable form.

4.5 匹配SFT条件下CPT初始化的影响4.5 Effect of CPT Initialization under Matched SFT Conditions

按照第 3.2.3 节中的评估协议，我们评估 CPT 检查点是否在匹配的 SFT 条件下提高了下游 SFT 性能。直接SFT路线和CPTinitialized SFT路线都使用相同的10K思想链OR建模样本，220次SFT迭代，峰值学习率5×10Following the evaluation protocol in Section 3.2.3, we evaluate whether the CPT checkpoint improves downstream SFT performance under matched SFT conditions. Both the direct SFT route and the CPTinitialized SFT route use the same 10K chain-of-thought OR modeling samples, 220 SFT iterations, peak learning rate 5 × 10−6具有余弦衰减、全局批量大小 128 和序列长度 8192。因此，两条路线之间的性能差异可归因于 CPT 初始化，而不是不同的 SFT 数据或优化设置。with cosine decay, global batch size 128, and sequence length 8192. Therefore, performance differences between the two routes can be attributed to CPT initialization rather than different SFT data or optimization settings.

我们报告了四个 OR 基准的结果。 NL4OPT 和 OptiBench 评估自然语言优化建模，B4O-Feasible 评估生成的 Gurobi 程序是否成功执行并生成可行的解决方案，B4O-ORGEval 评估生成的优化模型在结构上是否与参考模型等效，独立于实现。表 12 显示仅 SFT 就已经是一个强大的基线。与基础模型相比，直接SFT将NL4OPT从84.08%提高到86.93%，OptiBench从63.33%提高到64.17%，B4O-Feasible从60.47%提高到65.93%，B4O-ORGEval从34.26%提高到48.73%。这证实了 10K CoT OR 建模样本为激活优化建模功能提供了有效的任务级监督。We report results on four OR benchmarks. NL4OPT and OptiBench evaluate natural-language optimization modeling, B4O-Feasible evaluates whether the generated Gurobi program executes successfully and produces a feasible solution, and B4O-ORGEval evaluates whether the generated optimization model is structurally equivalent to the reference model, independent from implementations. Table 12 shows that SFT alone is already a strong baseline. Compared with the base model, direct SFT improves NL4OPT from 84.08% to 86.93%, OptiBench from 63.33% to 64.17%, B4O-Feasible from 60.47% to 65.93%, and B4O-ORGEval from 34.26% to 48.73%. This confirms that the 10K CoT OR modeling samples provide effective task-level supervision for activating optimization modeling capabilities.

CPT 初始化在所有四个基准测试中在此 SFT 基线之上提供了进一步的增益：NL4OPT 上 +2.59 pp、OptiBench 上 +2.95 pp、B4O-Feasible 上 +5.29 pp 和 B4OORGEval 上 +10.66 pp。可执行性和结构上都有增益，但 B4O-ORGEval 上的增益最大，这表明 CPT 提供了 OR 域建模先验，可以提高面向求解器的可行性，并且最重要的是，结构等效性 - 仅匹配条件 SFT 无法完全激活的功能。CPT initialization provides further gains on top of this SFT baseline across all four benchmarks: +2.59 pp on NL4OPT, +2.95 pp on OptiBench, +5.29 pp on B4O-Feasible, and +10.66 pp on B4OORGEval. The gain is present on both executability and structure but is largest on B4O-ORGEval, indicating that CPT supplies OR-domain modeling priors that improve solver-facing feasibility and, most strongly, structural equivalence—capabilities that matched-condition SFT alone does not fully activate.

由于 B4O-ORGEval 强调生成的优化模型和参考优化模型之间的数学等价性，因此它更直接地与 CPT 期间引入的领域知识和建模模式保持一致。Since B4O-ORGEval emphasizes mathematical equivalence between the generated and reference optimization models, it is more directly aligned with the domain knowledge and modeling patterns introduced during CPT.

总体而言，这些结果支持域自适应 CPT 作为面向 OR 的 SFT 准备阶段的价值。虽然直接 SFT 已经提高了任务性能，但 CPT 进一步增强了优化模型等效性所需的结构要求能力。这促使在未来的实验中通过更广泛的 OR 任务覆盖范围、更高质量的求解器验证文档以及保留感知数据混合来扩展 CPT。Overall, these results support the value of domain-adaptive CPT as a preparation stage for ORoriented SFT. While direct SFT already improves task performance, CPT further strengthens the structurally demanding capabilities required for optimization model equivalence. This motivates scaling CPT with broader OR task coverage, higher-quality solver-verified documents, and retentionaware data mixtures in future experiments.

4.6 拟议的培训后流程的性能4.6 Performance of the Proposed Post-Training Pipeline

评估设置。Evaluation Setup.我们评估了四个 OR 基准——用于自然语言优化建模和问题理解的 NL4OPT（Ramamonjison 等人，2022）和 OptiBench（Yang 等人，2025c）、用于可执行性的 B4O-Feasible 和用于结构等价的 B4O-ORGEval（Wang 等人，2025b）——以及通用基准 MMLU（Hendrycks 等人，2025b）。 2021a）、MMLU-Pro（Wang 等人，2024）、CMMLU（Hendrycks 等人，2021a）、HumanEval（Chen 等人，2021）、GSM8K（Cobbe 等人，2021）和 MATH（Hendrycks 等人，2021b）（表 14）。对于解码，我们对 Pass@1 使用贪婪并在以下位置采样We evaluate on four OR benchmarks—NL4OPT (Ramamonjison et al., 2022) and OptiBench (Yang et al., 2025c) for natural-language optimization modeling and problem understanding, B4O-Feasible for executability, and B4O-ORGEval for structural equivalence (Wang et al., 2025b)—alongside general benchmarks MMLU (Hendrycks et al., 2021a), MMLU-Pro (Wang et al., 2024), CMMLU (Hendrycks et al., 2021a), HumanEval (Chen et al., 2021), GSM8K (Cobbe et al., 2021), and MATH (Hendrycks et al., 2021b) (Table 14). For decoding, we use greedy for Pass@1 and sample at𝑇= 0.8 带顶-= 0.8 with top-𝑝Pass@4 = 0.95，应用无偏估计（Zhao 等人，2023）。 B4O-Feasible 的正确性需要运行时执行和合同遵守； B4O-ORGEval 使用标准化公式图上的 ORGEval WL 奖励来衡量语义结构匹配。所有评估共享相同的提示模板和解码预算，以进行公平比较。= 0.95 for Pass@4, applying unbiased estimation (Zhao et al., 2023). Correctness for B4O-Feasible requires runtime execution and contract adherence; B4O-ORGEval uses the ORGEval WL reward on the normalized formulation graph to measure semantic structural match. All evaluations share the same prompt template and decoding budget for fair comparison.

虽然 SFT 改进了提取任务、结构等效性和零样本 B4O 可行性能，但它并不能始终如一地增强所有解码设置的可执行性； 5 次 B4OFeasible 结果仍然低于基本模型。完整的 CPT+SFT 管道在 OR 基准测试中持续改进 0-shot Pass@1，并在 B4O-ORGEval（结构等效性）和 B4O-Feasible（可执行性）方面产生最强的增益，但不应将其解释为主导每个解码机制。这表明 CPT 提供了对于深层结构推理和可执行代码生成至关重要的领域知识，而 SFT 使输出与格式和协议要求保持一致。While SFT improves extraction tasks, structural equivalence, and zero-shot B4O-Feasible performance, it does not consistently enhance executability across all decoding settings; the 5-shot B4OFeasible result remains below the base model. The full CPT+SFT pipeline consistently improves 0-shot Pass@1 across the OR benchmarks and yields its strongest gains on B4O-ORGEval (structural equivalence) and B4O-Feasible (executability), but it should not be interpreted as dominating every decoding regime. This indicates that CPT supplies domain knowledge critical for both deep structural reasoning and the generation of executable code, while SFT aligns outputs with format and protocol requirements.

为了评估知识注入和指令调整的互补作用，我们在相同的 SFT 条件下进行了端到端实验，比较 Base、SFT 和 CPT+SFT。我们的中心假设是 CPT 提供特定领域的结构先验，而 SFT 则基于执行协议和格式合规性。表 13 中的结果证实，在 0-shot Pass@1 中的所有 OR 基准测试中，完整流程 (CPT+SFT) 始终优于 Base 和 SFT。 B4O-ORGEval 上的增益最大（例如，0 次 Pass@1 中的增益为 +10.66 pp，三设置平均值中的 SFT 为 +7.71 pp），B4O-Feasible 上的增益也很大（0 次 Pass@1 中的增益为 +5.29 pp，SFT 中的三设置平均值中的 +6.75 pp）。 SFT 单独改进了零次设置中的 B4O-Feasible，但在 5 次设置中下降到 Base 以下，这表明指令调整本身不足以稳定提示机制中的可执行性。To evaluate the complementary roles of knowledge injection and instruction tuning, we conduct endto-end experiments comparing Base, SFT, and CPT+SFT under identical SFT conditions. Our central hypothesis is that CPT supplies domain-specific structural priors, while SFT grounds execution protocols and format compliance. The results in Table 13 confirm that the full pipeline (CPT+SFT) consistently outperforms both Base and SFT across all OR benchmarks in 0-shot Pass@1. The gain is largest on B4O-ORGEval (e.g., +10.66 pp in 0-shot Pass@1 and +7.71 pp in the three-setting mean over SFT) and is also substantial on B4O-Feasible (+5.29 pp in 0-shot Pass@1 and +6.75 pp in the three-setting mean over SFT). SFT alone improves B4O-Feasible in zero-shot settings but drops below Base in 5-shot, indicating that instruction tuning by itself is insufficient to stabilize executability across prompting regimes.

按任务类型分类揭示了每个阶段的独特贡献。对于结构等价任务 (B4O-ORGEval)，单独的 SFT 比基本模型有适度的改进，但额外的 CPT 前缀产生了实质性的飞跃。这表明优化建模判断（需要理解独立于表面形式的数学意图）受益于 CPT 期间对 OR 语料库的密集暴露，而 SFT 由于其以指令为中心、规模较小的格式而无法完全复制这种暴露。对于代码执行和可行性任务（B4O-Feasible），SFT 改进了 0-shotDisaggregating by task type reveals the distinct contributions of each stage. For structural equivalence tasks (B4O-ORGEval), SFT alone improves moderately over the base model, but the additional CPT prefix yields a substantial leap. This suggests that optimization modeling judgments—which require understanding mathematical intent independent of surface form—benefit from dense exposure to OR corpora during CPT, an exposure that SFT cannot fully replicate due to its instruction-focused, smaller-scale format. For code-execution and feasibility tasks (B4O-Feasible), SFT improves 0-shot

Pass@1 和 Pass@4，但在 5 次射击中仍低于 Base，仅产生适度的三设置平均增益（69.39 vs. 68.51），而 CPT+SFT 达到 76.14。这表明特定领域的预训练对于生成可执行优化代码至关重要，超出了单独的格式和协议对齐所能提供的范围。在一般基准上（表 14），CPT+SFT 实现了与 Base 相当的性能，但在两个方向上都有轻微波动（例如，MMLU 上为 +0.9（Hendrycks 等人，2021a），MATH 上为 -1.7（Hendrycks 等人，2021b）），并且没有灾难性遗忘的迹象。这证实了分阶段数据混合物（~75% OR + 保留的通用语料库）保留了广泛的推理能力。如表 15 所示，我们的 CPT+SFT 模型在所有四个 OR 基准测试中获得了最高的总体得分，大幅优于 GPT-5.4-Mini（OpenAI，2026）和 Gemini-3-Flash（Google Cloud，2026）等强大的基准。Pass@1 and Pass@4 but remains below Base in 5-shot, yielding only a modest three-setting mean gain (69.39 vs. 68.51), whereas CPT+SFT reaches 76.14. This suggests that domain-specific pretraining is crucial for generating executable optimization code, beyond what format and protocol alignment alone can provide. On general benchmarks (Table 14), CPT+SFT achieves performance comparable to Base, with slight fluctuations in both directions (e.g., +0.9 on MMLU (Hendrycks et al., 2021a), -1.7 on MATH (Hendrycks et al., 2021b)), and no signs of catastrophic forgetting. This confirms that the staged data mixture (~75% OR + retained general corpora) preserves broad reasoning capabilities. As shown in Table 15, our CPT+SFT model attains the highest overall score across all four OR benchmarks, outperforming strong baselines such as GPT-5.4-Mini (OpenAI, 2026) and Gemini-3-Flash (Google Cloud, 2026) by a substantial margin.

为了剖析 CPT 有效性的来源，我们在同一端到端管道下消除了 CPT 数据混合（图 21）。我们比较两种配置：平衡（OR 和一般数据的混合）和纯 OR（仅 OR 数据）。如图所示，纯 OR 会导致总体评分大幅下降（10-15 分）To dissect the sources of CPT’s effectiveness, we ablate the CPT data mixture under the same end-toend pipeline (Figure 21). We compare two configurations: Balanced (our mixture of OR and general data) and Pure OR (only OR data). As shown, Pure OR causes a drastic drop (10–15 points) on general

45

  1. 结论、局限性和未来方向Conclusion, Limitations, and Future Directions

与平衡相比，OR 总体上没有任何改进，事实上，它的表现略逊于平衡。这表明专有的域预训练会导致过度拟合，从而损害一般功能而不利于域性能。相比之下，Balanced 在 OR 和一般基准上都取得了强劲的结果，这表明在 CPT 期间交错特定领域和一般数据对于注入结构先验、同时保留广泛的推理能力和防止灾难性遗忘至关重要。benchmarks while yielding no improvement on OR Overall compared to Balanced—in fact, it slightly underperforms Balanced. This indicates that exclusive domain pretraining leads to overfitting that harms general capabilities without benefiting domain performance. In contrast, Balanced achieves strong results on both OR and general benchmarks, demonstrating that interleaving domain-specific and general data during CPT is essential to inject structural priors while retaining broad reasoning abilities and guarding against catastrophic forgetting.

总之，端到端实验显示了 SFT 和 CPT 之间的互补模式。 SFT 建立有监督的任务对齐和面向协议的输出行为，而 CPT 提供领域结构先验，可驱动最大的额外收益，在 B4O-ORGEval 和 B4O-Feasible 上最明显。这两个阶段共同构成了一个互补的管道，用于提高 OR 域性能，同时保留一般功能。In summary, the end-to-end experiments show a complementary pattern between SFT and CPT. SFT establishes supervised task alignment and protocol-oriented output behavior, while CPT supplies domain-structural priors that drive the largest additional gains, most clearly on B4O-ORGEval and also on B4O-Feasible. Together, the two stages form a complementary pipeline for improving OR-domain performance while preserving general capability.

5 结论、局限性和未来方向5 Conclusion, Limitations, and Future Directions

本技术报告介绍了 DeepSeekV4 模型系列在 Ascend NPU SuperPOD 上进行全参数后训练的第一阶段实践。我们将整体框架称为This technical report presents a first-stage practice for full-parameter post-training of the DeepSeekV4 model family on Ascend NPU SuperPOD. We refer to the overall framework asSLAI 霸王龙SLAI T-Rex，它将全栈 Ascend SuperPOD 训练优化与基于求解器的 CPT-SFT 专业化 OR 结合起来。, which couples full-stack Ascend SuperPOD training optimization with solver-grounded CPT–SFT specialization for OR.

在系统层面，我们展示了在非 GPU 计算平台上进行万亿参数 MoE LLM 训练的首批全栈优化工作之一。所提出的端到端优化策略涵盖系统级训练编排和瓶颈级内核优化，实现高达 34.22% 的 MFU。这些结果验证了Ascend NPU SuperPOD上大规模后训练的训练效率和实际可行性。At the system level, we demonstrate one of the first full-stack optimization efforts for trillionparameter MoE LLM training on a non-GPU computing platform. The proposed end-to-end optimization strategy spans system-level training orchestration and bottleneck-level kernel optimization, achieving up to 34.22% MFU. These results validate both the training efficiency and practical feasibility of large-scale post-training on Ascend NPU SuperPOD.

对于训练后任务，我们在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 上建立了面向 OR 的 CPT-SFT 工作流程。在相同的 SFT 设置下，直接 SFT 将 B4O-Feasible 从 60.47% 提高到 65.93%，将 B4OORGEval 从 34.26% 提高到 48.73%。随着CPT初始化，相应的分数进一步分别提高到71.22%和59.39%。完整的基准测试结果显示 NL4OPT 和 OptiBench 的趋势一致。这些结果表明，直接 SFT 提供了监督任务对齐，而 CPT 初始化引入了额外的 OR 域建模先验，从而提高了面向求解器的可行性和结构等效性。最显着的改进是在 B4O-ORGEval 上观察到的，凸显了域自适应预训练对于复杂 OR 建模的价值。For post-training tasks, we establish an OR-oriented CPT–SFT workflow on DeepSeek-V4-Flash. Under identical SFT settings, direct SFT improves B4O-Feasible from 60.47% to 65.93% and B4OORGEval from 34.26% to 48.73%. With CPT initialization, the corresponding scores further increase to 71.22% and 59.39%, respectively. The full benchmark results show consistent trends across NL4OPT and OptiBench. These results suggest that direct SFT provides supervised task alignment, while CPT initialization introduces additional OR-domain modeling priors that improve both solverfacing feasibility and structural equivalence. The most significant improvement is observed on B4O-ORGEval, highlighting the value of domain-adaptive pre-training for complex OR modeling.

SFT 实验进一步凸显了数据质量的重要性。将自蒸馏数据从 10K 扩展到 50K 不会导致单调改进，甚至可能会降低自然语言建模基准的性能。相比之下，契约感知清理和简洁的 CoT 导向增强可以更可靠地改进 10K 数据，尤其是在 NL4OPT、OptiBench 和 ORGEval 上。这表明 OR 域 SFT 更多地受益于有针对性的建模错误修复、严格的输出契约执行、紧凑的推理检查表和基于求解器的验证，而不是简单地增加合成训练数据量。诚然，目前的后训练实验是在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 上进行的。在未来的工作中，我们将把相同的训练流程扩展到 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 并发布相应的训练结果，以进一步验证所提出的基础设施和训练后工作流程的可扩展性。The SFT experiments further highlight the importance of data quality. Scaling self-distilled data from 10K to 50K does not lead to monotonic improvements and may even degrade performance on natural-language modeling benchmarks. In contrast, contract-aware cleaning and concise CoToriented enhancement improve the 10K data more reliably, especially on NL4OPT, OptiBench, and ORGEval. This indicates that OR-domain SFT benefits more from targeted repair of modeling errors, strict output-contract enforcement, compact reasoning checklists, and solver-grounded verification than from simply increasing the volume of synthetic training data. Admittedly, the current posttraining experiments are conducted on DeepSeek-V4-Flash. In future work, we will extend the same training pipeline to DeepSeek-V4-Pro and release the corresponding training results to further validate the scalability of the proposed infrastructure and post-training workflow.

再现性和工件发布。Reproducibility and artifact release.为了支持完全的可重复性并促进后续研究，我们发布了拟议的优化和培训工作流程背后的关键工件，包括提示模板、格式合同规范、课程安排配置和监控回调。我们的管道中使用的人工智能辅助数据和评估工具，包括 Cleaner、Reviewer 和 Diagnostic Resolver，都是作为可配置的提示和规则驱动模块实现的。他们的模板和配置文件也被发布，允许未来的用户检查、重现、改编和扩展本工作中引入的数据清理、样本审查、诊断解决和监控程序。To support full reproducibility and facilitate follow-up research, we release the key artifacts underlying the proposed optimization and training workflow, including prompt templates, format-contract specifications, curriculum scheduling configurations, and monitoring callbacks. The AI-assisted data and evaluation tools used in our pipeline, including Cleaner, Reviewer, and Diagnostic Resolver, are implemented as configurable prompt- and ruledriven modules. Their templates and configuration files also are released, allowing future users to inspect, reproduce, adapt, and extend the data-cleaning, sample-review, diagnostic-resolution, and monitoring procedures introduced in this work.

此外，我们计划单独发布通过我们的内核优化管道开发的优化 AscendC 内核。此版本将提供具体的内核级实现和优化示例，使未来的工作能够重现所报告的操作员级改进，并进一步使优化后的内核适应相关的基于 Ascend 的训练工作负载。In addition, we plan to separately release the optimized AscendC kernels developed through our kernel-optimization pipeline. This release will provide concrete kernel-level implementations and optimization examples, enabling future work to reproduce the reported operator-level improvements and further adapt the optimized kernels to related Ascend-based training workloads.

未来的方向。Future directions.基于拟议的 Ascend SuperPOD 高性能训练基础设施，未来的工作将进一步提高端到端全参数训练的效率，同时将训练后工作流程扩展到代理强化学习（AgenticRL）。特别是，我们将研究基于运筹学的数学模型来训练并行性优化，旨在在内存、通信和编排约束下自动探索多维并行配置。与此同时，我们将继续将面向 OR 的持续预训练 (CPT) 和监督微调 (SFT) 扩展到更广泛的求解器验证语料库，将评估扩展到更多样化的 OR 问题族和通用能力基准，并加强对非线性表达式、比率约束、二次项和 Gurobi 特定建模模式的结构验证。Building on the proposed high-performance training infrastructure for Ascend SuperPOD, future work will further improve the efficiency of end-to-end full-parameter training while extending the post-training workflow toward agentic reinforcement learning (AgenticRL). In particular, we will investigate operations-research-based mathematical modeling for training parallelism optimization, aiming to automate the exploration of multi-dimensional parallel configurations under memory, communication, and orchestration constraints. In parallel, we will continue scaling OR-oriented continued pre-training (CPT) and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to broader solververified corpora, extend evaluation to more diverse OR problem families and general-capability benchmarks, and strengthen structural verification for nonlinear expressions, ratio constraints, quadratic terms, and Gurobi-specific modeling patterns.

总体而言，该报告证明Ascend NPU SuperPOD可以支持具有MoE和混合注意力架构的万亿规模模型的稳定全参数后训练。此外，所提出的 CPT-SFT 工作流程可以在面向 OR 的数学建模中产生可测量的收益。主要发现是，有效的垂直领域适应取决于领域知识获取、基于求解器的数据构建、监督数据质量、系统稳定性和特定任务评估之间的协调。Overall, this report demonstrates Ascend NPU SuperPOD can support stable full-parameter posttraining of trillion-scale models with MoE and hybrid attention architectures. Furthermore, the proposed CPT-SFT workflow can produce measurable gains in OR-oriented mathematical modeling. The key finding is that effective vertical domain adaptation depends on the alignment among domain knowledge acquisition, solver-grounded data construction, supervised data quality, system stability, and task-specific evaluation.

B 训练后食谱详细信息B Post-training Recipe Details

本附录总结了训练后实验中使用的主要配方级别设置，重点关注影响优化、并行执行、内存使用和再现性的配置。This appendix summarizes the main recipe-level settings used in the post-training experiments, focusing on configurations that affect optimization, parallel execution, memory usage, and reproducibility.

CPT 配方。CPT recipe.表 16 报告了用于基于 Ascend 的 DeepSeek-V4 持续预训练运行的 CPT 配方，包括长上下文设置、优化配置、并行策略和内存节省机制。Table 16 reports the CPT recipe used for the Ascend-based DeepSeek-V4 continuedpretraining run, including the long-context setup, optimization configuration, parallelism strategy, and memory-saving mechanisms.

SFT 配方。SFT recipe.表 17 报告了用于监督微调的主要配方级配置。与 CPT 相比，CPT 在领域自适应语料库上优化自回归语言建模，SFT 优化指令响应轨迹并掩盖提示标记上的损失，以便梯度仅应用于目标响应。选择报告的项目以匹配第 3.3 节中讨论的功能，包括指令跟踪对齐、面向求解器的建模以及升腾 NPU 上的管道优化分布式执行。Table 17 reports the main recipe-level configuration used for supervised fine-tuning. In contrast to CPT, which optimizes autoregressive language modeling over domain-adaptive corpora, SFT optimizes instruction-response trajectories and masks the loss on prompt tokens so that gradients are applied only to the target response. The reported items are selected to match the capabilities discussed in Section 3.3, including instruction-following alignment, solver-oriented modeling, and pipeline-optimized distributed execution on Ascend NPUs.

代理推出配置。Agentic rollout configuration.代理推出管道在推理模式下进行评估，并且在这项工作中不会更新模型参数。因此，它的配置与 CPT 和 SFT 训练方法是分开的。如果报告，推出配置应仅包括影响推理时间行为和未来强化学习再现性的参数，例如抽样公式候选数、最大自我审查轮次、最大代码修复轮次、求解器超时、执行成功标准和目标匹配容差。这些设置是推出参数，而不是优化器或训练超参数。The agentic rollout pipeline is evaluated in inference mode and does not update model parameters in this work. Its configuration is therefore separated from the CPT and SFT training recipes. If reported, the rollout configuration should include only parameters that affect inference-time behavior and future reinforcement-learning reproducibility, such as the number of sampled formulation candidates, maximum self-review rounds, maximum code-repair rounds, solver timeout, execution success criterion, and objective-matching tolerance. These settings are rollout parameters rather than optimizer or training hyperparameters.

C 求解器验证的 OR-CPT 数据合成：引擎设计和说明性案例C Solver-Verified OR-CPT Data Synthesis: Engine Design and Illustrative Cases

3.2 节介绍了运筹学（OR）语料库的综合组件及其双向构建流程。本附录重点介绍该方法：如何将参数化优化生成器转换为独立的 CPT 文档，如何在语言模型转换中保留数学不变量，以及确定性检查如何防止看似合理但不正确的样本进入训练语料库。Section 3.2 introduced the synthesized component of the operations-research (OR) corpus and its bidirectional construction pipeline. This appendix focuses on the method: how a parameterized optimization generator is converted into a self-contained CPT document, how mathematical invariants are preserved across language-model transformations, and how deterministic checks prevent plausible but incorrect samples from entering the training corpus.

核心设计原则是语言模型的流畅性不被用作数学正确性的代表。语言模型执行两种语义转换——从经过验证的优化实例到业务问题，以及从该业务问题回到数学模型和可执行程序。生成器合约、静态检查和独立求解器执行围绕这些转换，并确定生成的文档是否可接受 CPT。The central design principle is that language-model fluency is not used as a proxy for mathematical correctness. Language models perform two semantic transformations—from a verified optimization instance to a business problem, and from that business problem back to a mathematical model and executable program. Generator contracts, static checks, and independent solver execution surround these transformations and determine whether the resulting document is admissible for CPT.

C.1 在培训后工作流程中的作用C.1 Role in the post-training workflow

OR-CPT 引擎是一个上游数据构建系统。它与 Ascend 910C 训练堆栈分开：引擎建立数学保真度、语义覆盖范围和来源，而训练堆栈在生成的语料库上对模型进行标记、打包、采样和优化。该引擎导出简单的独立文档用于自回归持续预训练，而不是指令响应对话。The OR-CPT Engine is an upstream data-construction system. It is separate from the Ascend 910C training stack: the engine establishes mathematical fidelity, semantic coverage, and provenance, while the training stack tokenizes, packs, samples, and optimizes the model on the resulting corpus. The engine exports plain self-contained documents for autoregressive continued pre-training rather than instruction–response conversations.

这种分离也澄清了 CPT 和 SFT 之间的关系。 CPT 文档向模型公开 OR 术语、公式模式、求解器 API 和验证习惯。 SFT 随后教导模型根据任务指令和输出契约引出这些知识。相同的 OR 分类法可以指导这两个阶段，但数据表示和优化目标仍然不同。This separation also clarifies the relationship between CPT and SFT. CPT documents expose the model to OR terminology, formulation patterns, solver APIs, and verification habits. SFT subsequently teaches the model to elicit that knowledge under task instructions and output contracts. The same OR taxonomy can guide both stages, but the data representations and optimization objectives remain distinct.

C.2 端到端引擎架构C.2 End-to-end engine architecture

该引擎遵循双向验证路径。它首先从参数化生成器中采样结构化优化实例，独立求解源模型，并过滤不可行、退化、数值不稳定或重复的种子。然后，经过验证的种子被反译为独立的业务问题，其中自然语言质量过滤器检查数字覆盖率、单元一致性、业务基础和解决方案泄漏。最后，引擎根据生成的问题重建模型，应用静态和基于契约的验证，执行重建的模型，将其状态和目标与独立参考进行比较，并将接受的对呈现为具有沿袭元数据的 CPT 文档。The engine follows a bidirectional verification path. It first samples a structured optimization instance from a parameterized generator, solves the source model independently, and filters infeasible, degenerate, numerically unstable, or duplicated seeds. The verified seed is then backtranslated into a self-contained business problem, where a natural-language quality filter checks numeric coverage, unit consistency, business grounding, and solution leakage. Finally, the engine reconstructs the model from the generated problem, applies static and contract-based validation, executes the reconstructed model, compares its status and objective against the independent reference, and renders accepted pairs into CPT documents with lineage metadata.

客观比较使用绝对和相对容差。它是一个强大的可执行一致性信号，但不是符号等价的证明：两种不同的公式可以在特定的数值实例上共享相同的最佳值。因此，即使目标一致，静态和基于合同的检查仍然是必要的。图 22 总结了从生成的种子到接受的、经过求解器验证的 CPT 文档的最终端到端信任路径。The objective comparison uses absolute and relative tolerances. It is a strong executable consistency signal, but not a proof of symbolic equivalence: two different formulations can share the same optimum on a particular numerical instance. The static and contract-based checks therefore remain necessary even when the objective agrees. C.3 数学种子和领域契约C.3 Mathematical seeds and domain contracts

种子代表。Seed representation.种子是一个结构化的优化实例，而不仅仅是一个文本问题。它包含声明的集合、系数表、目标方向、变量域、边界、约束族以及可执行或 LP 工件。它还携带语义元数据，例如任务族、子任务A seed is a structured optimization instance, not merely a textual question. It contains declared sets, coefficient tables, objective direction, variable domains, bounds, constraint families, and executable or LP artifacts. It also carries semantic metadata such as task family, sub-

61

C.4 速控双向合成C.4 Prompt-controlled bidirectional synthesis

系列、建模概念、难度轴和公式变体。当必须审核或改进生成器时，确定性随机种子支持再生。family, modeling concepts, difficulty axes, and formulation variant. Deterministic random seeds support regeneration when a generator must be audited or refined.

发电机简介和家庭合同。Generator profiles and family contracts.生成器配置文件描述了一个生成器的规范数学签名，包括变量类型、客观术语、核心约束、有效索引和禁止的重新解释。系列契约描述了相关生成器之间共享的不变量，例如平衡方程、链接规则、守恒定律和所需的变量族。这些层基于种子的源事实（声明的集合、系数表、边界、单位和可执行工件），因此语言模型无法发明数字或引用不存在的索引。种子质量记录进一步捕获可行性、客观重新计算、解决方案活动、简并性和数值稳定性，而谱系元数据记录训练文本之外的生成器、场景配置、提示修订、验证状态和渲染状态。A generator profile describes the canonical mathematical signature of one generator, including variable types, objective terms, core constraints, valid indices, and forbidden reinterpretations. A family contract describes invariants shared across related generators, such as balance equations, linking rules, conservation laws, and required variable families. These layers are grounded in the source facts of the seed—declared sets, coefficient tables, bounds, units, and executable artifacts—so that the language model cannot invent numbers or refer to nonexistent indices. Seed-quality records further capture feasibility, objective recomputation, solution activity, degeneracy, and numerical stability, while lineage metadata records the generator, scenario configuration, prompt revision, validation status, and rendering status outside the training text.

种子质量过滤。Seed-quality filtering.仅仅可行性并不能保证培训价值。如果模型中所有有意义的决策都为零，每个决策都固定在一个界限上，或者没有任何约束参与真正的权衡，那么该模型可能是可以解决的，但没有帮助。因此，在种子有资格进行语言生成之前，引擎会检查解决方案活动、约束活动、客观重新计算、数值精度和重复签名。Feasibility alone does not guarantee training value. A model in which all meaningful decisions are zero, every decision is fixed at a bound, or no constraint participates in a genuine trade-off may be solvable but unhelpful. The engine therefore checks solution activity, constraint activity, objective recomputation, numerical precision, and duplicate signatures before a seed is eligible for language generation.

C.4 速控双向合成C.4 Prompt-controlled bidirectional synthesis

反向翻译和正向建模具有不同的信息边界，并由互补的保真度要求控制。反向翻译可以检查已验证的源事实，但它必须保留系数、边界、单位和索引关系，同时隐藏求解器状态、目标值、变量解、LP 工件和代码。它可能会改变行业环境、利益相关者的观点、规划触发因素、范围、命名风格和单位，但不得添加或删除数学结构。正向建模接收生成的问题和适用的结构契约；它必须重构变量、客观意义、核心约束、指数政策、解释、公式、独立的 Gurobi 代码，并仅使用声明的数据进行自检，而不对参考答案进行硬编码或从最优值中推断缺失系数。Backtranslation and forward modeling have different information boundaries and are controlled by complementary fidelity requirements. Backtranslation may inspect the verified source facts, but it must preserve coefficients, bounds, units, and indexed relations while hiding solver status, objective values, variable solutions, LP artifacts, and code. It may vary the industry setting, stakeholder perspective, planning trigger, horizon, naming style, and units, but it must not add or remove mathematical structure. Forward modeling receives the generated problem and the applicable structural contracts; it must reconstruct the variables, objective sense, core constraints, index policy, explanation, formulation, standalone Gurobi code, and self-check using only the declared data, without hard-coding the reference answer or inferring missing coefficients from the optimum.

生产提示是版本化的实现工件。无需复制其全文即可表示其基本逻辑：The production prompts are versioned implementation artifacts. Their essential logic can be represented without reproducing their full text:

清单 1：Listing 1:双向综合阶段使用的抽象提示界面。Abstract prompt interface used by the bidirectional synthesis stage.

BACKTRANSLATE(verified_seed,profile,scenario):
preservesourcefactsandmathematicalstructure
hidereferencesolutionandimplementationartifacts
returnacompletebusinessproblem

FORWARD_MODEL(business_problem,profile,family_contract):
reconstructexplanation,formulation,andexecutablecode
useonlydeclaredindicesandcoefficients
returnastructuredself-check;donothard-codetheoptimum

自检被视为由生成的响应提出的声明，而不是验证器。静态检查和独立执行仍然具有权威性，因为散文可能会提到代码省略或索引不正确的必需约束。The self-check is treated as a claim made by the generated response, not as the validator. Static inspection and independent execution remain authoritative because prose may mention a required constraint that the code omits or indexes incorrectly.

C.5 受控场景和 CPT 渲染C.5 Controlled scenarios and CPT rendering

场景目录将数学多样性与语言多样性分开。基本 OR 结构可以通过不同的行业视角、决策触发器、组织类型、规划框架、实体命名风格和单位约定来表达。这些控制改变了表面叙述——例如，问题是否被定义为医疗分配、物流规划、能源调度、制造审查、停电响应、预算修订或服务网络决策——同时保持源系数表、变量角色、客观项、约束和数学周期不变。The scenario catalog separates mathematical diversity from linguistic diversity. A base OR structure can be expressed through different industry lenses, decision triggers, organization types, planning frames, entity-naming styles, and unit conventions. These controls change the surface narrative—for example, whether the problem is framed as a healthcare allocation, logistics planning, energy scheduling, manufacturing review, outage response, budget revision, or service-network decision—while keeping the source coefficient tables, variable roles, objective terms, constraints, and mathematical periods invariant.

62

C.6 示例性案例 I：有能力的设施位置C.6 Illustrative case I: capacitated facility location

负面引导同样重要。除非存在车辆和排序，否则运输矩阵不得转变为车辆路线。设施定位任务必须保留其固定的开放决策。批量调整任务必须保持其各个期间的库存平衡。这些限制阻止流畅的场景默默地改变模型系列。Negative guidance is equally important. A transportation matrix must not be turned into vehicle routing unless vehicles and sequencing exist. A facility- location task must retain its fixed opening decisions. A lot-sizing task must retain its period-by-period inventory balance. These restrictions prevent a fluent scenario from silently changing the model family.

可执行验证后，渲染器将接受的对转换为独立的 CPT 文档。完整的文档可以包含： (i) 业务问题和数据； (ii) 商业模式解释； (iii) 数学公式； (iv) 独立求解器代码； (v) 解释性解决方案； (vi) 可行性和目标重新计算检查。样式和部分顺序可能会有所不同，但强制性的数学和验证内容不会。After executable verification, the renderer converts the accepted pair into a self-contained CPT document. A full document can contain: (i) the business problem and data; (ii) a business-tomodel explanation; (iii) a mathematical formulation; (iv) standalone solver code; (v) an interpreted solution; and (vi) feasibility and objective-recalculation checks. Style and section order may vary, but mandatory mathematical and validation content does not.

C.6 示例性案例 I：有能力的设施位置C.6 Illustrative case I: capacitated facility location

假设区域服务运营商考虑为三个需求区提供服务的三个候选站点。开设网站需要支付固定费用；服务只能从开放的站点分配，并且不能超出站点容量。说明性数据如表 18 所示。Suppose a regional service operator considers three candidate sites serving three demand zones. Opening a site incurs a fixed cost; service can be allocated only from opened sites and cannot exceed site capacity. The illustrative data are shown in Table 18.

表 18：Table 18:手动构建的设施位置示例。Manually constructed facility-location example.

最佳说明性规划打开B和C。站点B服务于Z1的30个单元和Z2的全部35个单元；站点 C 为 Z1 的其余 10 个单位和 Z3 的所有 25 个单位提供服务。站点 B 正好达到其容量 65。目标是The optimal illustrative plan opens B and C. Site B serves 30 units of Z1 and all 35 units of Z2; site C serves the remaining 10 units of Z1 and all 25 units of Z3. Site B is exactly at its capacity of 65. The objective is

此示例包含真正的固定费用权衡。站点 C 的固定成本最低，对 Z3 的服务最便宜，而 B 对 Z2 的服务最便宜。在规定的容量下，两者都无法单独满足总需求。该模型必须共同选择站点并分配服务，而不是在每个需求列中选择最便宜的系数。This example contains a genuine fixed-charge trade-off. Site C has the lowest fixed cost and the cheapest service to Z3, while B is cheapest for Z2. Neither can serve total demand alone under the stated capacities. The model must jointly choose sites and allocate service rather than select the cheapest coefficient in each demand column.

同样的数学可以用诊所选址、区域维修站或计算服务中心来表示。实体和单位可能会发生变化，但三个不变因素——固定激活、需求满足和容量链接——必须保持存在。仅当正向重建恢复了这些不变量并在容差范围内重现了参考目标时，才接受正向重建。The same mathematics could be rendered as clinic siting, regional repair depots, or computingservice hubs. The entities and units may change, but the three invariants—fixed activation, demand satisfaction, and capacity linking—must remain present. Forward reconstruction is accepted only if it recovers these invariants and reproduces the reference objective within tolerance.

所有三个程序在语法上都是有效的、可执行的，并且可以解决All three programs can be syntactically valid, executable, and solved to anOPTIMAL地位。该状态仅证明实际构建的程序的最佳状态。它并不证明对预期业务问题的忠诚度。因此，该示例激发了三项不同的检查：所需条款的静态存在、家庭级别的结构合同以及独立客观比较。status. That status certifies only the optimum of the program that was actually constructed. It does not certify faithfulness to the intended business problem. The example therefore motivates three distinct checks: static presence of required terms, family-level structural contracts, and independent objective comparison.

修复遵循相同的规则。当问题陈述缺少系数或界限时，安全操作是重新生成、拒绝或从权威源表中恢复。修复不能仅仅为了使模型有界或接近参考目标而发明一个值。Repair follows the same rule. When a problem statement lacks a coefficient or bound, the safe action is regeneration, rejection, or recovery from an authoritative source table. A repair must not invent a value merely to make the model bounded or to approach the reference objective.

D CPT-SFT-部署-评估来源D CPT–SFT–Deployment–Evaluation Provenance

训练后的大规模 MoE 模型涉及多个阶段——CPT、SFT、权重转换、服务和评估——每个阶段都会产生依赖于上游输出的工件。我们引入了一个轻量级的来源系统，通过每个阶段清单和基于本地文件的注册表链接所有阶段，以避免基准分数和训练配置、检查点或部署权重之间的不透明，从而确保评估声明的改进、可重复性和可审计性的明确归属。该系统具有三个功能：（i）它强制执行从训练数据到分数的可验证链，跟踪每个结果到其确切的检查点； (ii) 它通过上游 CPT 运行的转换和训练跟踪部署的模型，从而实现回归的根本原因分析； (iii) 它为自动跟踪、仪表板监控和工件管理提供编程支持，而不依赖于数据库或破坏现有工作流程。Post-training large-scale MoE models involves multiple stages—CPT, SFT, weight conversion, serving, and evaluation—each producing artifacts dependent on upstream outputs. We introduce a lightweight provenance system that links all stages via per-stage manifests and a local file-based registry, to avoid opacity between benchmark scores and training configurations, checkpoints, or deployed weights, thereby ensuring clear attribution of improvements, reproducibility, and auditability of evaluation claims. The system serves three functions: (i) it enforces a verifiable chain from training data to scores, tracing each result to its exact checkpoint; (ii) it enables rootcause analysis of regressions by tracing deployed models back through conversion and training to the upstream CPT run; (iii) it provides programmatic support for automated tracking, dashboard monitoring, and artifact management, without database dependencies or disruption to existing workflows.

D.1 设计原则D.1 Design principles

  1. 模型身份与服务端点解耦。Model identity is decoupled from the service endpoint.所服务的模型名称、IP 地址和端口标识推理入口点，而不是模型版本。权威模型身份由权重目录中的工件清单以及每个阶段记录的上游训练谱系携带。这种分离允许在同一端点名称下提供多个模型版本，同时保留正在使用的权重的明确标识。A served model name, IP address, and port identify the inference entry point, not the model version. The authoritative model identity is carried by the artifact manifest at the weight directory, together with the upstream training lineage recorded at each stage. This separation allows multiple model versions to be served under the same endpoint name while preserving unambiguous identification of which weights are in use.

  2. 每个阶段都会在执行时记录其基本事实。Every stage records its ground truth at the point of execution.每个阶段完成后立即将实际路径、关键环境变量和上游关系写入结构化清单中。这个清单，而不是任何后来的重建，充当了该阶段的权威记录。执行点记录消除了事后文档的脆弱性，并确保在更改或忘记环境详细信息（例如，CANN 版本、并行性配置、分词器路径）之前捕获它们。Each stage writes the actual paths, key environment variables, and upstream relationships into a structured manifest immediately upon completion. This manifest, rather than any later reconstruction, serves as the authoritative record for that stage. The point-of-execution recording eliminates the fragility of post-hoc documentation and ensures that environmental details (e.g., CANN version, parallelism configuration, tokenizer path) are captured before they can be altered or forgotten.

64

D.2 来源链和注册表布局D.2 Provenance chain and registry layout

  1. 历史实验可以事后合并。Historical experiments can be incorporated post-hoc.注册工具接受现有的配置快照、检查点目录和转换的工件，允许在部署来源系统之前完成的运行可以追溯链接到来源图中。这确保了有价值的遗留实验不会被排除在交叉运行比较和趋势分析之外。Registration tooling accepts existing configuration snapshots, checkpoint directories, and converted artifacts, allowing runs completed before the provenance system was deployed to be retroactively linked into the provenance graph. This ensures that valuable legacy experiments are not excluded from cross-run comparison and trend analysis.

D.2 来源链和注册表布局D.2 Provenance chain and registry layout

端到端链跨越五个阶段：CPT 训练、SFT 训练、HF 工件转换、模型部署和基准评估。每个阶段都会在其输出位置生成一个清单文件，并在集中式 JSON 注册表中注册一个副本。注册表按阶段类型（训练运行、工件、部署、评估运行）进行组织，并使用轻量级索引文件维护聚合计数。除了集中条目之外，清单始终与其描述的工件位于同一位置 - HF 工件清单位于权重目录中，部署清单位于服务日志目录中，评估出处位于基准摘要目录中。这种双重放置确保即使集中式注册表不可用，出处记录也会随工件一起移动，并且工件的任何下游消费者（例如，单独的评估管道）都可以在不咨询中央注册表的情况下发现其谱系。The end-to-end chain spans five stages: CPT training, SFT training, HF artifact conversion, model deployment, and benchmark evaluation. Each stage produces a manifest file at its output location and registers a copy in a centralized JSON registry. The registry is organized by stage type (training runs, artifacts, deployments, evaluation runs), with a lightweight index file maintaining aggregate counts. In addition to the centralized entries, manifests are always co-located with the artifacts they describe—the HF artifact manifest resides in the weight directory, the deployment manifest resides in the serving log directory, and the evaluation provenance resides in the benchmark summary directory. This dual placement ensures that the provenance record travels with the artifact even if the centralized registry is unavailable, and that any downstream consumer of the artifact (e.g., a separate evaluation pipeline) can discover its lineage without consulting the central registry.

D.3 阶段级联动机制D.3 Stage-level linkage mechanism

When provided, the harness extends the benchmark summary with full provenance fields spanning evaluation, deployment, artifact, SFT, and CPT. Without it, evaluation proceeds normally but scores cannot be automatically traced to specific weights or associated with the corresponding dashboard experiment.

D.4 置信水平D.4 Confidence levels

Each provenance link carries a confidence annotation, summarized in Table 20, that distinguishes manifest-based exact linkage from path-based or heuristic matching.

D.5 仪表板监控和追溯注册D.5 Dashboard monitoring and retroactive registration

The provenance registry directly feeds a monitoring dashboard that visualizes the full experiment graph, each row corresponds to a training run, and columns display the run identifier, job type, training iterations, key metrics (loss, benchmark scores), and—critically—provenance status and confidence annotations for each link in the chain.具有完整清单覆盖范围的运行标记为The provenance registry directly feeds a monitoring dashboard that visualizes the full experiment graph, each row corresponds to a training run, and columns display the run identifier, job type, training iterations, key metrics (loss, benchmark scores), and—critically—provenance status and confidence annotations for each link in the chain. E 培训计划和监控设计E Training Schedule and Monitoring Design

虽然数据细化流程提高了个体样本质量，但训练数据的宏观组织也需要精心设计。我们的数据混合分析表明，过度集中的 OR 域适应会削弱一般能力，从而激发平衡 OR、数学、代码和一般域数据的训练计划。因此，我们设计了预定义的课程表以及用于跟踪培训稳定性和能力保留的监控仪表板。时间表、阶段边界和过渡规则在训练前指定，而仪表板则作为手动检查的可观察性工具。While the data refinement pipeline improves individual sample quality, the macro-level organization of training data also requires careful design. Our data-mixture analysis shows that overly concentrated OR-domain adaptation can weaken general capabilities, motivating a training schedule that balances OR, mathematics, code, and general-domain data. We therefore design a predefined curriculum schedule together with a monitoring dashboard for tracking training stability and capability retention. The schedule, stage boundaries, and transition rules are specified before training, while the dashboard serves as an observability tool for manual inspection.

E.1 静态课程学习设计E.1 Static Curriculum Learning Design

我们将训练数据混合组织成预定义的三阶段计划。该时间表使用混合向量We organize the training data mixture into a pre-defined, three-stage schedule. The schedule uses a mixing vector𝜶 ( 𝑡 ) = [ 𝛼或者，OR,𝛼数学，math,𝛼代码，code,𝛼gen] 随训练步骤的不同而变化gen] that is varied across training steps𝑡按照既定计划：according to a fixed plan:

  1. 基础阶段（前 30% 的步骤）：Foundation stage (first 30% of steps):高度重视数学推理和简洁的通用代码（Heavy emphasis on mathematical reasoning and clean general code (𝛼数学 = 0.35，math = 0.35,𝛼代码=0.35，code = 0.35,𝛼根=0.20，gen = 0.20,𝛼或 = 0.10）。此阶段加强了精确的令牌生成和算法逻辑。OR = 0.10). This phase reinforces precise token generation and algorithmic logic.

  2. 域提升 (30–70%)：Domain ramp-up (30–70%): 𝛼OR 线性增加到 0.40，同时OR is linearly increased to 0.40 while𝛼代码逐渐减少到0.15。在此阶段中，OR 样本按难度格式引入：首先是数据问题 (DP)，然后是数据表中 (DT)，最后是数据问题分离 (DPS)。向更困难的格式的过渡由当前格式的保留子集的固定评估阈值控制：在解锁下一种格式之前，模型必须实现 85% 以上的执行成功。该阈值是硬性规则，而不是自适应算法。code gradually reduces to 0.15. Within this stage, OR samples are introduced by difficulty format: first Datain-Problem (DP), then Data-in-Table (DT), and finally Data-Problem-Separate (DPS). The transition to a more difficult format is gated by a fixed evaluation threshold on a held-out subset of the current format: the model must achieve more than 85% execution success before the next format is unlocked. This threshold is a hard rule, not an adaptive algorithm.

  3. 合同硬化（70–100%）：Contract hardening (70–100%):混合物又转向仅代码和 LP 编写任务（The mixture shifts back toward code-only and LP-writing tasks (𝛼代码提高到 0.25），使用数据细化管道中经过验证的样本来强化严格的输出合同。code raised to 0.25) to reinforce strict output contracts, using validated samples from the data refinement pipeline.

这个时间表是一个This schedule is a默认轨迹default trajectory其参数（比例、阈值、阶段边界）是根据第 4.4 节中的初步运行和数据混合消融固定的（图 21）。它在训练期间不会动态修改。类课程安排策略已在其他背景下进行了探索（Tzannetos 等人，2026；Zhao 等人，2026；Liang 等人，2025）；我们的具体实例是一种手动设计的配置。whose parameters (proportions, thresholds, stage boundaries) were fixed based on preliminary runs and the data-mixture ablation in Section 4.4 (Figure 21). It is not modified dynamically during training. Curriculum-like scheduling strategies have been explored in other contexts (Tzannetos et al., 2026; Zhao et al., 2026; Liang et al., 2025); our specific instantiation is one manually designed configuration.

66

E.2 监控仪表板E.2 Monitoring Dashboard

E.2 监控仪表板E.2 Monitoring Dashboard

为了观察训练行为并及早发现潜在问题，我们实施了一种监控设置，定期（每 500 个训练步骤）记录两组指标。该仪表板不是闭环控制器；它是人类开发人员的可观察层。To observe training behavior and detect potential problems early, we implemented a monitoring setup that logs two groups of metrics at regular intervals (every 500 training steps). This dashboard is not a closed-loop controller; it is an observability layer for human developers.

培训健康轨道。Training-health track.标准优化信号：损失、梯度范数、NaN 计数和模型 FLOP 利用率 (MFU)。梯度范数的峰值或 NaN 损失立即表明存在需要手动调查的数据或训练问题。Standard optimization signals: loss, gradient norm, NaN counts, and Model FLOPs Utilization (MFU). A spike in gradient norm or a NaN loss immediately signals a data or training issue requiring manual investigation.

能力保留轨道。Capability-retention track.一组跟踪领域和一般性能的指标：A set of metrics that track both domain and general performance:

  • 或能力：OR capability:OR 域集（按格式分区）上的验证困惑，以及 NL4OPT 和 OptiBench 的小型代理子集上的特定于任务的奖励（code_reward、wl_reward）。validation perplexity on an OR-domain set (partitioned by format), and task-specific rewards (code_reward, wl_reward) on small proxy subsets of NL4OPT and OptiBench.

  • 一般代码质量：General code quality: a 语法健康评分syntax health score根据一组保留的 200 个纯 Python 算法问题进行计算。该分数检查非 ASCII 标记、缩进错误、导入正确性和基本静态有效性。computed on a held-out set of 200 pure-Python algorithmic problems. The score checks for non-ASCII tokens, indentation errors, import correctness, and basic static validity.

  • 一般语言流利程度：General language fluency:固定通用领域语料库（维基百科，书籍）上的困惑来检测灾难性遗忘。perplexity on a fixed general-domain corpus (Wikipedia, books) to detect catastrophic forgetting.

这些指标在 WandB 仪表板中记录并可视化。在开发过程中，它们用于手动检查：例如，语法健康状况的持续下降可能会促使开发人员暂停以 OR 为中心的训练、检查最近的数据或调整混合物。具体阈值和由此产生的决策是由人类工程师制定的，而不是由自动状态机制定的。仪表板设计的灵感来自于训练动态的系统分析（Qi 等人，2026），其仪表代码包含在已发布的工件中。These metrics are logged and visualized in WandB dashboards. During development, they were used for manual inspection: for example, a persistent decline in syntax health could prompt the developer to pause OR-centric training, inspect recent data, or adjust the mixture. The specific thresholds and the resulting decisions were made by human engineers, not by an automated state machine. The dashboard design is inspired by systematic analyses of training dynamics (Qi et al., 2026), and its instrumentation code is included in the released artifacts.

67

F 代表原始检查点错误案例F Representative Original-Checkpoint Error Cases

3.1 节总结了原始 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 检查点的聚合故障分布。本附录记录了该诊断中使用的具体例子。这些案例可追溯到相应的评估工件，并按推理设置转变分析中讨论的六种故障类型进行分组。Section 3.1 summarizes the aggregate failure distribution of the original DeepSeek-V4-Flash checkpoint. This appendix records the concrete examples used in that diagnosis. The cases are traced back to the corresponding evaluation artifacts and are grouped by the six failure types discussed in the Inference-Setting Shift analysis.

案例研究：输出契约和模式失败Case Study: Output-Contract and Schema Failures基准 B4O ORGEval 样本 IDBenchmark B4O ORGEval Sample IDBENCH4OPT_4 ; BENCH4OPT_176评价EvaluationBENCH4OPT_4：来自 Markdown 栅栏的语法错误；: syntax failure from markdown fence;BENCH4OPT_176 : TypeError来自带有字符串移位标签的列表索引 故障类型 仅代码from list indexing with a string shift label Failure Type Code-only协议违规和 JSON/列表架构不匹配protocol violation and JSON/list schema mismatch 原始问题Original Problems BENCH4OPT_4是一个资本预算问题：选择投资项目以在预算、风险和依赖性约束下最大化净现值。is a capital-budgeting problem: select investment projects to maximize NPV under budget, risk, and dependency constraints.BENCH4OPT_176是一个医院护士调度问题：分配护士轮班，同时满足轮班覆盖范围、技能要求和最长工作时间。is a hospital nurse-scheduling problem: assign nurses to shifts while satisfying shift coverage, skill requirements, and maximum working hours.模型输出Model Output # BENCH4OPT_4: code-only target, but the generated response starts with: “‘python import json import gurobipy as gp from gurobipy import GRB # BENCH4OPT_176: shifts are string labels, while shift_requirements is a list. for j in shifts: model.addConstr( gp.quicksum(x[i, j] for i in nurses) >= shift_requirements[j] , name=f"shift_coverage_{j}" )

错误注释。Error Annotation.第一个错误在模型构建之前就阻止了评估：执行器将 markdown 栅栏标记视为 Python 语法。第二个错误到达模型构造，但在使用字符串移位名称作为列表索引时失败。这两个示例主要激励 SFT：模型已经具有有用的 OR 知识，但 Pass@1 行为必须遵守纯代码契约和模式安全的数据访问模板。The first error blocks evaluation before the model is even built: the executor sees the markdown fence token as Python syntax. The second error reaches model construction but fails when a string shift name is used as a list index. Both examples primarily motivate SFT: the model already has useful OR knowledge, but Pass@1 behavior must obey code-only contracts and schema-safe data-access templates.

案例研究：NL4OPT 中的离散语义错误Case Study: Discrete Semantics Error in NL4OPT基准 NL4OPT 样本 IDBenchmark NL4OPT Sample IDNL4OPT_15评价地面实况：28250；预测：28125 故障类型 整数劳动力变量的持续放宽Evaluation Ground truth: 28250; Prediction: 28125 Failure Type Continuous relaxation of integer workforce variables原始问题Original Problem一家商店雇用每周收入 500 美元的老年人和每周收入 750 美元的年轻人。商店必须将每周工资保持在 30000 美元以下。商店每天至少需要 50 名工人，其中至少 10 名必须是年轻人。为了保证商店的顺利运营，年轻人的数量应至少是老年人数量的三分之一。制定有限合伙人以最大限度地减少工资支出。A store employs senior citizens who earn $500 per week and young adults who earn $750 per week. The store must keep the weekly wage bill below $30000. On any day, the store requires at least 50 workers, of whom at least 10 must be young adults. To ensure the store runs smoothly, the number of young adults should be at least a third the number of senior citizens. Formulate a LP to minimize the wage bill.模型输出Model Output import gurobipy as gp from gurobipy import GRB m = gp.Model("MinimizeWageBill") x = m.addVar(vtype= GRB.CONTINUOUS , name="seniors") y = m.addVar(vtype= GRB.CONTINUOUS , name="young_adults") m.setObjective(500 * x + 750 * y, sense=GRB.MINIMIZE) m.addConstr(x + y >= 50, "total_workers") m.addConstr(y >= 10, "min_young") m.addConstr(3 * y >= x, "young_senior_ratio") m.addConstr(500 * x + 750 * y <= 30000, "wage_budget") m.optimize() if m.status == GRB.OPTIMAL: print (f"Optimal solution: x = {x.X:.2f}, y = {y.X:.2f}") print (f"Minimum wage bill = ${m.ObjVal:.2f}") else : print ("No optimal solution found.")

错误注释。Error Annotation.该问题要求工人的数量，但模型使用连续变量。这放松了整数劳动力决策并产生了分数最优。The problem asks for numbers of workers, but the model uses continuous variables. This relaxes the integer workforce decision and yields a fractional optimum.

案例研究：损失/产量驱动的保护误差Case Study: Loss/Yield-Driven Conservation Error基准 B4O 可行样本 IDBenchmark B4O Feasible Sample IDBENCH4OPT_2评估Pass@1预测：265.5768398591736；基本事实：531.1536797183472。 5-shot和Pass@16恢复参考值。失效类型 蒸发损失混合成本和流量守恒Evaluation Pass@1 prediction: 265.5768398591736; ground truth: 531.1536797183472. 5-shot and Pass@16 recover the reference value. Failure Type Evaporation loss mixed into both cost and flow conservation原始问题Original Problem该任务是一个配水网络流量问题。每条边都有容量、运输成本和蒸发率。该模型必须满足节点需求，同时最小化由流动和蒸发引起的总成本。The task is a water-distribution network-flow problem. Each edge has a capacity, transportation cost, and evaporation rate. The model must satisfy node demand while minimizing the total cost induced by flow and evaporation.模型输出Model Output flow = {} for i, (source, target, capacity, cost, evaporation_rate) in enumerate (edges): flow[(source, target)] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, ub=capacity, name=f"flow_{source}_{target}") obj = gp.LinExpr() for (source, target), var in flow.items(): ... obj += (cost + evaporation_rate) * var model.setObjective(obj, GRB.MINIMIZE) for node_idx, node in enumerate (nodes): inflow = gp.LinExpr() outflow = gp.LinExpr() evaporation_loss = gp.LinExpr() ... model.addConstr( inflow - outflow - evaporation_loss == demand[node_idx], name=f"flow_conservation_{node}" ) 错误注释。Error Annotation.该代码在语法上是有效的并且达到了最佳解决方案，但它实现了错误的物理系统。蒸发被视为附加单位成本，并从保护中扣除，从而模糊了损失是否影响交付数量、发生成本或两者。这是一个域模式失败：具有损失、产量、收缩和蒸发的网络流需要稳定的保护模板，而不是通用的 LP 支架。The code is syntactically valid and reaches an optimal solution, but it implements the wrong physical system. Evaporation is treated as an additive unit cost and also deducted from conservation, blurring whether loss affects delivered quantity, incurred cost, or both. This is a domain-pattern failure: network flow with loss, yield, shrinkage, and evaporation needs stable conservation templates rather than generic LP scaffolding.

错误注释。Error Annotation.该模型认识到投资和卡车分配是相互作用的，但它没有正确协调美元、数百美元、每辆卡车的减少和总节省。在 5-shot 中，误差改变了形状：模型朝着线性的储蓄模板移动，但可以删除比例变量。因此，这种情况暴露了比 API 使用更深层的语义问题：比率、投资回报率、平均成本和效率语句需要显式跟踪分子、分母、单位和总量。The model recognizes that investment and truck allocation interact, but it does not correctly reconcile dollars, hundreds-of-dollars, per-truck reduction, and total savings. In 5-shot, the error changes shape: the model moves toward a linear-looking savings template but can drop the scale variable. This case therefore exposes a deeper semantic issue than API usage: ratio, ROI, average-cost, and efficiency statements require explicit tracking of numerator, denominator, unit, and total quantity.
